The proposed policy preserves and predicts context according to semantic importance and temporal relevance, yielding more informative representation learning while retaining the efficiency of masked prediction. We evaluate the resulting encoders on four driving benchmarks spanning tracking, semantic segmentation, and depth estimation. The results demonstrate that V-JEPA4A reduces identity switches on BDD100k MOT by 25% over V-JEPA with random masking, achieves 73.2 mIoU on Cityscapes, and 3.75 RMSE on KITTI-2015 depth, while incurring only ~14% additional pre-training iteration overhead.

</details>


### [78] [Probing Association Instability with Track-State Perturbations for Clip-Level Active Learning in Query-Propagation Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17224)
*Riku Inoue,Shogo Sato,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Tomoyasu Shimada,Toshihiko Nishimura,Ryuichi Tanida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QPID是一种针对查询传播式多目标跟踪（MOT）的片段级主动学习方法，通过扰动内部轨迹状态并测量预测差异来估计关联不稳定性，结合多样性选择策略，在相同标注预算下优于现有主动学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练端到端多目标跟踪模型需要密集的边界框和身份标注，构建数据集成本高昂。片段级主动学习虽可降低标注成本，但现有基于输出级时间不确定性的采集准则可能遗漏那些信息量来源于传播轨迹状态关联不稳定的片段。因此需要一种直接面向轨迹状态关联不稳定性的主动学习采集方法。

Method: QPID通过双侧重微扰内部轨迹状态，并与干净参考分支对比预测差异来估计关联不稳定性。该方法提出两个指标：Localization Drift（定位漂移）和Entropy-Weighted Confidence Discrepancy（熵加权置信度差异），聚合为片段级关联不稳定分数。为避免仅选择不确定性冗余片段，QPID采用基于轨迹级视觉原型的Uncertainty-Weighted Visual Coverage策略，从高不稳定片段中选取代表性标注批次。

Result: 在DanceTrack和SportsMOT数据集上，使用MeMOTR和SambaMOTR作为基础模型，QPID在相同标注预算下相比现有主动学习基线取得了更强的性能。

Conclusion: QPID表明，通过直接度量传播轨迹状态的关联不稳定性并进行多样性感知的批次选取，可以更有效地识别信息量丰富的视频片段，从而在有限的标注预算下显著提升查询传播式MOT模型的训练效率。

Abstract: Training query-propagation end-to-end multi-object tracking (MOT) models requires dense bounding-box and identity annotations across video sequences, making dataset construction expensive. Clip-level active learning reduces this cost by selecting video clips for annotation, but prior acquisition criteria based on output-level temporal uncertainty may miss clips whose informativeness comes from association instability in propagated track states. We propose QPID (Query-Propagation Instability and Diversity), a clip acquisition method for query-propagation MOT that targets association instability in propagated track states. QPID estimates this instability by applying two-sided perturbations to internal track states and measuring prediction differences from a clean reference branch. The key idea is that, in stable clips, each propagated track should continue to follow the same target under small perturbations, whereas in ambiguous clips, small changes in the track state can alter which target the track follows, leading to changes in localization or confidence. QPID measures these perturbation-induced prediction differences with two metrics: Localization Drift and Entropy-Weighted Confidence Discrepancy. These metrics are aggregated into a clip-level association-instability score. To avoid redundant uncertainty-only selection, QPID selects a representative annotation batch from high-instability clips using Uncertainty-Weighted Visual Coverage with track-level visual prototypes. Experiments on DanceTrack and SportsMOT with MeMOTR and SambaMOTR show that QPID achieves strong performance compared with active learning baselines under the same annotation budget.

</details>


### [79] [Structural Plan-to-Model Conversion with Deterministic Geometry and Guarded Agentic Vision-Language Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17237)
*Mohammad Talebi-Kalaleh,Qipei Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个无需任务特定训练或微调、将agentic视觉-语言层应用于框架结构平面图PDF的构件检测与模型草绘框架。确定性阶段提取图元、估计比例并识别五类构件，代理阶段在约束下提出类型修正。在100张平面图基准上端到端评估，柱/梁/墙/支撑/开口的召回率与精确率分别为0.922/0.997、0.886/0.990、1.000/1.000、1.000/1.000、1.000/0.964，比例估计误差在0.1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将结构框架平面图转换为可编辑的有限元模型草稿仍然劳动密集且易出错。现有面向建筑构件的图纸理解系统依赖任务特定的训练神经网络，而结构工程中的语言模型智能体则只处理文本或模型数据而非图纸本身。作者希望构建一个无需任务特定检测器训练或微调即可直接从PDF图纸中识别构件并生成模型草稿的框架。

Method: 框架由两个阶段组成：确定性阶段先提取基本图元，通过尺寸比例共识估计比例，用草绘语法识别五类实体（柱、梁、墙、支撑、开口）并组装可编辑布局；代理性阶段提出类型修正，但受确定性候选约束、操作特定的准入测试、变更级审查和fail-closed事务机制限制，确保修正安全可靠。

Result: 在100张平面图基准上（seed不重叠的held-out一半）评估：比例估计在每个图纸上都达到生成器参考值的0.1%以内。柱、梁、墙、支撑、开口的召回率/精确率分别为0.922/0.997、0.886/0.990、1.000/1.000、1.000/1.000、1.000/0.964。受控研究在3张开发图纸上重复两次损坏九次试验：校准全部通过；构件修复在九次中五次满足所有严格端状态谓词；有守卫的审查在明确界限内纠正了遗漏框架和错误标记。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了无需任务特定训练的agentic视觉-语言层在结构构件检测与模型草绘中的有效性。但作者诚实指出局限性：held-out半与开发半共享同一生成器，未包含独立绘制的图纸、光栅评估、分析连通性和求解器验证，因此其泛化能力有待更广泛的独立测试。

Abstract: Converting structural framing plans into editable finite-element model drafts remains labor-intensive and prone to transcription error. Existing drawing-understanding systems for building components rely on task-specific trained neural detectors, and language-model agents in structural engineering operate on text or model data rather than the drawing itself. This paper presents, to the authors' knowledge, the first framework applying an agentic vision-language layer to structural component detection and model drafting from framing-plan PDFs, without task-specific detector training or fine-tuning. A deterministic stage extracts primitives, estimates scale by dimension-ratio consensus, recognizes five entity classes with a drafting grammar, and assembles an editable layout. The agentic stage proposes typed corrections constrained by deterministic candidates, operation-specific admission tests, change-level review, and fail-closed transactions. Evaluation used an author-generated benchmark of 100 plans: a development half that informed every rule revision, and a seed-disjoint held-out half generated after the rules froze, evaluated once. All reported scores are end-to-end results of the complete framework on the held-out half. Scale was estimated within 0.1% of the generator reference for every drawing. Recall and precision were 0.922/0.997 for columns, 0.886/0.990 for beams, 1.000/1.000 for walls, 1.000/1.000 for braces, and 1.000/0.964 for openings. A controlled study repeated two corruptions three times on three development drawings. Calibration passed all nine trials; member repair met every strict end-state predicate in five of nine. Guarded review corrected missed framing and false marks within explicit bounds. The held-out half shares the development generator, so the study excludes independently drafted plans, raster evaluation, analytical connectivity, and solver validation.

</details>


### [80] [Heterogeneity-Aware Deep Learning for Tumour Classification from Multiparametric MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17254)
*Yue Xia,Euijoon Ahn,Tian Xia,Yuan Yuan,Michael Fulham,Jinman Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HA-DLC框架，通过显式建模影像学肿瘤亚区（子区域）来实现肿瘤分类与分子状态预测。HA-DLC包含异质性子区域生成（HSG）模块、跨患者子区域对齐（CPSA）模块以及双流特征提取（DSFE）模块，并在端到端联合优化下，在肝脏病灶和脑肿瘤两个公开数据集上优于现有影像组学与深度学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤内异质性（ITH）反映了肿瘤生物学上的空间差异，对肿瘤行为、预后和治疗反应具有重要影响。影像组学依赖手工特征，而现有深度学习方法大多使用全肿瘤表征或人工定义的子区域，难以规模化地建模肿瘤异质性。因此，需要一个能够自动、可扩展地学习和建模肿瘤影像子区域异质性的框架。

Method: HA-DLC由两个核心模块组成：（1）异质性子区域生成（HSG）模块，通过无监督聚类生成初始伪标记子区域，随后进行跨患者子区域对齐（CPSA），利用软分配将聚类派生区域映射到共享标签空间；（2）双流特征提取（DSFE）模块，整合局部异质性感知特征与全局肿瘤表征。整个框架通过软目标分割和分类目标端到端联合优化聚类掩码、CPSA、分割、特征提取和分类。

Result: 在LLD-MMRI2023肝脏病灶数据集和RSNA-ASNR-MICCAI 2021脑肿瘤影像基因组数据集上，HA-DLC一致性地优于最先进的影像组学和深度学习基线方法，验证了跨患者子区域对齐和双流异质性建模在多参数MRI肿瘤分类中的价值。

Conclusion: 跨患者子区域对齐与双流异质性建模能够有效提升从多参数MRI进行肿瘤分类与分子状态预测的性能，为可扩展的肿瘤异质性建模提供了新的深度学习方法。

Abstract: Intra-tumoural heterogeneity (ITH) reflects spatial variation in tumour biology and is an important determinant of tumour behaviour, prognosis, and treatment response. Radiomics and deep learning have shown promise for tumour classification from multiparametric MRI (mp-MRI), but radiomics relies on handcrafted features, while most deep learning methods use whole-tumour representations or manually defined sub-regions, limiting scalable modelling of tumour heterogeneity. We propose a Heterogeneity-Aware Deep Learning Classification (HA-DLC) framework that explicitly models imaging-derived tumour sub-regions for lesion-type diagnosis and molecular-status prediction. HA-DLC consists of: (1) a Heterogeneous Sub-region Generation (HSG) module that produces initial pseudo-labelled sub-regions via unsupervised clustering, followed by Cross-Patient Sub-region Alignment (CPSA), which maps cluster-derived regions to a shared label space using soft assignments; and (2) a Dual-Stream Feature Extraction (DSFE) module that integrates local heterogeneity-aware features with global tumour representations. Given the initial clustering masks, CPSA, segmentation, feature extraction, and classification are jointly optimized end-to-end using soft-target segmentation and classification objectives. We evaluate HA-DLC on the LLD-MMRI2023 liver lesion dataset and the RSNA-ASNR-MICCAI 2021 Radiogenomic Brain Tumour dataset. HA-DLC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art radiomics and deep learning baselines, demonstrating the value of cross-patient sub-region alignment and dual-stream heterogeneity modelling for tumour classification from mp-MRI.

</details>


### [81] [Learning Where and What to Lift for Bi-planar X-ray-to-CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17255)
*Yifei Wu,Yicheng Wu,Qiang Ma,Qi Chen,Renyang Gu,Xinyu Liu,Yongsheng Pan,Yong Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiftXR，一种交错的、几何引导的X光到CT重建框架，通过显式引入3D解剖布局恢复来分离空间组织重建与强度估计的纠缠，在两个公开数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量X光视图重建CT体积是一个严重病态问题，因为投影坍缩了深度信息，导致解剖区域的3D位置与其强度分布高度纠缠和模糊。作者观察到一旦解剖区域的空间组织被确定，估计其CT强度就变得容易得多，因此希望将空间布局恢复显式地纳入CT重建过程。

Method: LiftXR采用交错的几何引导框架：(1) 布局提升器（layout lifter）从双平面X光生成3D解剖布局，为强度渲染器提供空间引导；(2) 强度渲染器（intensity renderer）重建CT体积；(3) 解剖解析器（anatomical parser）对重建结果进行体积感知，利用空间分辨的边界和强度线索恢复更精细的解剖布局；(4) 解析后的布局为区域特定的强度校准提供反馈。整个过程从投影条件化的布局生成过渡到重建条件化的解剖感知。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，LiftXR持续优于近期X光到CT重建方法，建立了新的SOTA。此外，重建的CT在下游外部分割任务中取得更优性能，表明解剖保真度得到提升。

Conclusion: LiftXR验证了将空间布局恢复显式纳入CT重建的有效性——通过交错的设计，让布局生成与强度估计互相强化，不仅提升了重建质量，还增强了重建CT在下游任务中的解剖保真度。

Abstract: X-ray imaging can be approximately modeled as the projection of an underlying volumetric attenuation field, with each measurement recording the accumulated attenuation along a corresponding ray path. Reconstructing a CT volume from only a few X-ray views is therefore severely ill-posed, as the projections collapse depth information and leave 3D locations of anatomical regions and their corresponding intensity distributions highly entangled and ambiguous. We observe that once the spatial organization of anatomical regions is established, estimating their CT intensities becomes substantially more tractable. Motivated by this, we propose LiftXR, an interleaved, geometry-guided framework that explicitly incorporates spatial layout recovery into CT reconstruction. Specifically, a layout lifter first generates a 3D anatomical layout from bi-planar X-rays, providing spatial guidance for an intensity renderer to reconstruct a CT volume. An anatomical parser then performs volumetric perception on the reconstruction, exploiting its spatially resolved boundary and intensity cues to recover a refined anatomical layout. This transition from projection-conditioned layout generation to reconstruction-conditioned anatomical perception allows the parsed layout to provide feedback for region-specific intensity calibration. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that LiftXR consistently outperforms recent X-ray-to-CT reconstruction methods, establishing a new state of the art. Moreover, the reconstructed CT achieves superior performance in external downstream segmentation, indicating improved anatomical fidelity. Code will be released.

</details>


### [82] [Key-Frame Reasoning with SAM3: Third Place Solution for the MeViS-Text Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17279)
*Ce Bian,Xusheng He,Jinrong Zhang,Canyang Wu,Xianjing Han,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段、无需训练的解决方案，用于第8届LSVOS挑战赛的MeViS-Text赛道。第一阶段利用Gemini-3.1 Pro API将视频级事件分解为实例级目标，为每个目标选择关键帧并生成判别性描述；第二阶段使用SAM3-agent在选定帧上生成像素级种子掩码，并通过SAM3视频跟踪器在视频中双向传播掩码。该方法在挑战测试集上排名第三，取得了J&F=0.761、J=0.7367、F=0.7852、N-acc=0.8333、T-acc=0.9755和Final=0.856593的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此任务要求模型在整个视频中定位并分割自然语言表达所指定的目标对象。由于视频级表达常依赖时间线索（如动作、交互、方向、相对位置等），直接进行视频级定位分割具有挑战性。作者旨在开发一种无需任务特定训练和模型集成的轻量级方案，以应对这一挑战。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段使用Gemini-3.1 Pro API对视频级事件进行分解，将事件转化为实例级目标，为每个目标选取关键帧并生成与该帧对齐的判别性文本描述；第二阶段使用SAM3-agent在选定关键帧上生成像素级种子掩码，然后利用SAM3视频跟踪器对掩码在视频中进行双向传播。每个有效实例独立进行grounding和传播，最终合并各帧的掩码结果。整个过程在单张NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090上运行，无需任务特定训练或模型集成。

Result: 该方法在挑战测试集上排名第三，取得以下指标：J&F分数0.761，J分数0.7367，F分数0.7852，N-acc（名词准确率）0.8333，T-acc（时间准确率）0.9755，最终分数0.856593。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段无训练方案通过大语言模型（LLM）进行事件分解与关键帧选择，结合SAM3的掩码生成与双向视频传播能力，在MeViS-Text任务上取得了具有竞争力的性能。该方法验证了利用LLM进行时序语义理解与SAM3进行像素级跟踪的组合在视频目标分割中的有效性。

Abstract: This report presents a two-stage, training-free solution for the MeViS-Text track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge. The task requires a model to localize and segment the object specified by a natural-language expression throughout a video. Such expressions often depend on temporal cues, including actions, interactions, directions, and relative positions. Our first stage uses Gemini-3.1 Pro via API to decompose a video-level event into instance-level targets, select a key frame for each target, and generate a discriminative description aligned with that frame. In the second stage, SAM3-agent produces a pixel-level seed mask on the selected frame, and the SAM3 video tracker propagates the mask bidirectionally through the video. Valid instances are grounded and propagated independently before their frame-wise masks are merged. All local SAM3 processing runs on a single NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 without task-specific training or model ensembling. Our method ranked third on the challenge test set, obtaining J&F, J, F, N-acc., T-acc., and Final scores of 0.761, 0.7367, 0.7852, 0.8333, 0.9755, and 0.856593, respectively.

</details>


### [83] [UniQuery4R: Unified 4D Scene Reconstruction from a Single Query](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17283)
*Tiancheng Chen,Sheng Tang,Wenhua Jin,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Junsheng Zhou,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniQuery4R是一个查询条件化的动态4D场景重建框架，通过一次性编码多帧clip并在解码时灵活选择源视图、目标视图和连续图像坐标，实现跨任意源-目标对的特征复用，支持稀疏推理和密集重建，并在WorldTrack基准上取得了最佳的场景流估计和动态点重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式动态4D重建方法通常预测密集任务特定图或独立处理源-目标对，导致稀疏查询时存在不必要的计算，且不同帧对之间的特征复用有限。作者旨在提出一个统一的查询条件化框架，以解决计算浪费和特征复用不足的问题。

Method: UniQuery4R采用查询条件化框架：先编码多帧clip一次，解码时通过源到目标的交叉注意力，仅需选择源视图、目标视图和连续源图像坐标即可。每个查询联合预测目标对应点、目标时刻3D位置和场景流，并估计源深度和每视图相机参数。场景流采用方向-幅度参数化，对运动点和静态点分别进行监督。

Result: 在WorldTrack基准上，UniQuery4R在所有评估方法中取得了场景流估计和动态点重建的最佳宏平均结果。

Conclusion: UniQuery4R展示了clip编码复用、查询条件化解码以及方向-幅度场景流参数化的有效性，能够同时支持稀疏推理和密集重建，无需依赖固定clip长度的时序嵌入。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 4D scenes requires jointly estimating correspondence, geometry, object motion, and camera motion. Existing feed-forward methods typically predict dense task-specific maps or independently process source-target pairs, leading to unnecessary computation for sparse queries and limited feature reuse across different frame pairs. We present UniQuery4R, a query-conditioned framework that encodes a multi-frame clip once and selects the source view, target view, and continuous source-image coordinate only at decoding time via source-to-target cross-attention. Each query jointly predicts target correspondence, target-time 3D position, and scene flow, along with source depth, while camera parameters are estimated per view. This design allows the encoded clip to be reused across arbitrary source-target selections and supports both sparse inference and dense reconstruction through batched queries, without learned temporal embeddings tied to a fixed clip length. We further introduce a direction-magnitude parameterization of scene flow with separate supervision for moving and static points. Among the evaluated methods, UniQuery4R achieves the best macro-average results on WorldTrack for both scene-flow estimation and dynamic-point reconstruction.

</details>


### [84] [B-Spline Embedded Structure Learning for 3D Tooth Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17291)
*Xianghan Wei,Jianwen Lou,Zhiguo Lu,Hairong Jin,Haihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了B-Spline嵌入结构学习框架，通过将牙齿的序贯排列参数化为B样条轨迹并引入结构感知动态分类器，实现了3D牙齿分割的新SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界牙列存在拥挤、错位和邻牙形态相似等问题，使得精确的3D牙齿分割极具挑战性，现有方法缺乏对牙齿整体空间结构的全局建模能力。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 将牙齿中心拟合为参数化B样条轨迹，为每个点赋予连续的结构嵌入，迫使共享骨干网络捕获全局牙弓组织形式；(2) 引入结构感知动态分类器（SADC），通过局部高斯邻近门控对动态原型池进行正则化，并利用注意力模块建模牙齿间的空间关系和双侧对称性，实现原型的情境协同演化。

Result: 在3DTeethSeg22基准上达到新的SOTA精度，同时具备出色的结构鲁棒性和低计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过将牙齿的固有序列结构蒸馏为连续的结构约束来正则化表征空间，并辅以自适应决策边界，能够显著增强模型处理复杂牙列构型的能力，验证了结构先验在3D牙齿分割任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D tooth segmentation forms the cornerstone of digital dentistry, yet it remains a formidable challenge due to the inherent intricacy of real-world dentitions, such as crowding, misaligned teeth and high morphological similarity between adjacent teeth. To resolve this, we present B-Spline Embedded Structure Learning, a novel framework that distills the inherent sequential arrangement of teeth into a continuous structural constraint to regularize representation space. Our approach parameterizes the global dental topology by fitting a parametric B-spline trajectory to tooth centers, assigning each point a continuous structural embedding that forces the shared backbone to capture global arch organization. To fully exploit these embedded priors, we introduce a Structure-Aware Dynamic Classifier (SADC) to substitute rigid static templates with adaptive, case-calibrated decision boundaries. SADC regularizes dynamic prototype pooling via a localized Gaussian proximity gate and contextually co-evolves them through an attention block modeling spatial relations and bilateral symmetries across teeth. Extensive evaluations on the 3DTeethSeg22 benchmark demonstrate that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art accuracy with exceptional structural robustness and efficiency in computational overhead, markedly enhancing the model's capacity to handle complex dental configurations.

</details>


### [85] [Learning What Not to Learn: Adversarial Disentangled Prompt Tuning for Robust Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17306)
*Yang Chen,Zhan Zhuang,Yanbin Wei,Zebin Chen,Hua Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ADAPT框架，通过双提示机制（目标提示+诱饵提示池）将伪鲁棒特征与鲁棒特征解耦，解决对抗提示调优中鲁棒泛化过拟合导致的未见类别性能快速下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗提示调优方法虽能高效增强视觉-语言模型的鲁棒性，但会在已见类别上加剧鲁棒泛化过拟合，导致模型对未见类别的对抗样本性能随训练快速退化。作者经验性地识别出这一退化的根源在于模型倾向于学习伪鲁棒特征（即不可泛化的捷径）。

Method: 提出ADAPT（Adversarial Disentangled Prompt Tuning）框架，遵循"学习不去学什么"的理念。采用双提示机制：包含一个目标提示和解耦提示池。训练时，解耦提示被引导去诱捕多样的伪鲁棒特征，而目标提示被约束在嵌入空间中与解耦提示正交，从而学习真正的鲁棒特征。通过正交损失将鲁棒特征与伪鲁棒特征解耦，并提供理论分析证明正交损失可以界定伪鲁棒特征偏移对未见类别的影响。

Result: 大量实验表明，ADAPT显著提升了目标提示在未见类别上的鲁棒性，有效防止了鲁棒泛化过拟合。理论分析还提供了测试错误的保证。

Conclusion: 通过将伪鲁棒特征"诱捕"到解耦提示中并与目标提示保持正交，ADAPT成功实现了鲁棒特征与伪鲁棒特征的有效分离，从而解决了对抗提示调优中存在的鲁棒泛化过拟合问题，为视觉-语言模型的鲁棒调优提供了新思路。

Abstract: While adversarial prompt tuning can enhance robustness of vision-language models efficiently, we find that existing methods aggravate robust generalization overfitting on seen classes, leading to a rapid degradation in performance against adversarial examples of unseen classes as training progresses. We empirically identify that this degradation stems from the tendency of the model to learn pseudo-robust features (i.e., non-generalizable shortcuts). To mitigate this, we propose ADAPT (Adversarial Disentangled Prompt Tuning), a robust prompt tuning framework following the philosophy of ``Learning What Not to Learn''. Specifically, ADAPT uses a dual-prompt mechanism with a target prompt and a pool of decoy prompts. During training, the decoy prompts are guided to entrap diverse pseudo-robust features, while the target prompt is constrained to be orthogonal to the decoys in the embedding space to learn robust features. By disentangling the robust features from the pseudo-robust features, ADAPT effectively prevents robust generalization overfitting. We further provide an analysis showing that the orthogonal loss bounds the effect of shifts in pseudo-robust features on unseen classes, yielding a testing error guarantee. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that ADAPT substantially improves the robustness of the target prompt on unseen classes. The code is available at https://github.com/cheny02/ADAPT-ACMMM2026.

</details>


### [86] [Scanline-Aware Animatable Gaussian Avatars from Rolling-Shutter Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17314)
*Youxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RS-Avatar，直接从卷帘快门(RS)视频重建清晰、无畸变、可驱动的3D高斯人体化身。核心洞察在于：运动感知化身已在多个亚帧时刻渲染身体，只需将模糊模型中的平均算子替换为逐扫描线合成的卷帘快门算子，即可消除RS畸变。在RS-ZJU基准上，该方法在所有测试对象上优于将帧视为瞬时的训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可驱动人体化身重建普遍假设多视角视频每一帧的所有像素观测到同一身体运动瞬间，但卷帘快门(RS)传感器的行曝光机制导致单帧内头部和脚部之间存在数十毫秒的关节运动差异，每个扫描线对应不同姿态。这种畸变被烘焙进规范表示中，在合成新视角和新姿态时表现为剪切和晃动。更严重的是，多相机阵列中每台相机读取时间表不同，即便几何正确也破坏了驱动重建的多视角一致性。

Method: RS-Avatar的构建极其简洁：利用运动感知化身已有的亚帧时刻渲染能力，将模糊模型中对多个亚帧渲染结果求平均的算子替换为卷帘快门模型中的逐扫描线合成算子。这是对现有管线唯一需要修改的部分，不引入额外模块或复杂度。

Result: 在作者从ZJU-MoCap构建的RS-ZJU基准上，RS-Avatar在所有测试对象上的新视角合成效果均优于将帧视为瞬时（即忽略卷帘快门畸变）的训练基线。对比实验表明，基于相同亚帧机制构建的运动感知模糊模型无法迁移到该任务，其表现甚至低于不感知快门畸变的基线，说明亚帧机制可复用但算子必须专门设计。

Conclusion: 卷帘快门畸变不能通过通用的时序建模或模糊建模来解决，必须使用符合RS物理成像过程的逐扫描线合成算子。RS-Avatar证明了在最小改动下，直接从RS视频恢复清晰的、可驱动的3D化身是可行的，为真实世界手持/消费级多视角视频下的人体重建提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Animatable human avatars are routinely reconstructed from multi-view video under a silent assumption: that every pixel of a frame observes the same instant of the body's motion. Rolling-shutter (RS) sensors expose image rows sequentially, so within one frame the head and the feet of a moving person are separated by tens of milliseconds of articulated motion, and every scanline sees a different pose. Feeding such video to a state-of-the-art avatar bakes the distortion into the canonical representation, where it survives as shear and wobble under novel views and novel poses. Worse, every camera in a rig follows its own readout schedule, so the multi-view consistency that drives the reconstruction is violated even when the geometry is correct. We present RS-Avatar, which reconstructs a sharp, undistorted, animatable 3D Gaussian avatar directly from RS video. The formulation is minimal: a motion-aware avatar already renders the body at several sub-frame instants, and where a blur model averages those renderings, a rolling-shutter model composites them scanline by scanline. Changing that operator is the only modification required. On RS-ZJU, a benchmark we build from ZJU-MoCap, this improves novel-view synthesis over training as if the frames were instantaneous, on every subject. A motion-aware blur model built on the same sub-frame machinery does not transfer, and in fact falls below the shutter-oblivious baseline: the machinery is reusable, the operator is not.

</details>


### [87] [If, Then, Otherwise: Diagnosing Conditional Branching in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17318)
*Seoyoung Lee,Neel P. Bhatt,Pranay Samineni,Cong Liu,S P Sharan,Timothy Barclay,Gregory M. Wagner,Daniel Milan,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu,Atlas Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CondVLN是一个基于场景图（scene-graph）的视觉语言导航（VLN）基准测试，专门用于诊断智能体在有条件分支指令（如"如果条件成立则走A路，否则走B路"）下的表现。该基准包含超过11,500条条件指令，覆盖四个环境，并引入了分支选择准确率和条件成功率两个诊断指标。实验表明，现有SOTA智能体在条件分支中会失败，而一个轻量级的神经符号分支选择模型可将性能提升2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLN评估主要关注沿路线指令导航到固定目标的能力，但现实中的导航指令往往取决于环境状态的条件分支（"如果...则...否则..."）。这类指令要求智能体评估场景证据、选择正确的逻辑分支并执行相应导航行为。现有评估对条件分支执行缺乏控制，难以判断智能体失败究竟是源于感知、语言理解、导航还是逻辑决策。因此需要一个能够分离和诊断这些失败因素的基准。

Method: CondVLN通过程序化生成指令，将分支条件锚定在可验证的3D场景图谓词上，并系统性地控制分支深度、依赖链长度、空间组合、证据可观测性和指令长度等变量。基准覆盖AI2-THOR、Matterport3D、Gibson和ReplicaCAD四个环境，包含超过11,500条条件指令。评估采用标准VLN指标加上两个分支特异性诊断指标：分支选择准确率（Branch Selection Accuracy）和条件成功率（Conditional Success Rate）。此外还提出了一个轻量级神经符号分支选择模型，将条件理解与导航执行分离。

Result: 对四个SOTA VLN智能体（VLN-Zero、NaVid、NaVILA、Open-Nav）的评估表明，条件分支暴露了标准成功率和路径长度指标无法捕捉的失败模式：智能体可以导航得看似合理，但实际选择了与观察到场景条件不一致的分支。神经符号分支选择模型将条件决策从导航执行中解耦，性能提升2倍。

Conclusion: CondVLN提供了一个可复用的测试平台，用于衡量具身智能体是否不仅能够跟随指令，而且能在正确条件下遵循正确的指令。它揭示出现有VLN智能体在条件逻辑决策方面的不足，并展示了将条件接地与导航执行分离的神经符号方法的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation agents are often evaluated on their ability to follow route-like instructions toward a fixed goal. Yet, real navigation instructions often depend on observed states of the environment: if a condition holds, then follow one path, otherwise take another. Such instructions require an agent to evaluate scene evidence, select the correct logical branch, and execute the corresponding navigation behavior. Existing evaluations provide limited control over conditional branch execution, making it difficult to determine whether agents fail because of perception, grounding, navigation, or logical decision-making. We introduce CondVLN, a scene-graph-grounded benchmark for diagnosing conditional branching in vision-language navigation. CondVLN programmatically generates instructions whose branch conditions are grounded in verifiable 3D scene-graph predicates, with controlled variation in branch depth, dependency chain length, spatial composition, evidence observability, and instruction horizon. CondVLN contains over 11,500 generated conditional instructions across AI2-THOR, Matterport3D, Gibson, and ReplicaCAD, and evaluates agents using standard VLN metrics and branch-specific diagnostics: Branch Selection Accuracy and Conditional Success Rate. Evaluating four state-of-the-art VLN agents (VLN-Zero, NaVid, NaVILA, and Open-Nav) shows that conditional branching exposes failures that are not captured by standard success rate or path length alone: agents can navigate plausibly while committing to a branch inconsistent with the observed scene condition. We also present a lightweight neurosymbolic branch-selection model that separates condition grounding from navigation execution, improving performance by 2x. CondVLN provides a reusable testbed for measuring whether embodied agents can not only follow instructions, but follow the right instruction under the right condition.

</details>


### [88] [MS-MFAD : Multimodal large language models for Face Anti-spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17328)
*Xiaoyong Yu,Rongzhen Li,Shuming Shi,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MFAD，一种基于多模态大语言模型的可解释统一人脸防伪检测系统，通过细粒度像素-语义锚定机制激活MLLM内在推理能力，配合仅需每个攻击类别1000个精确掩码的跨攻击语义级标注范式，在有限高质量样本下实现40-50%的域内ACER相对降低，跨域性能退化控制在11.62%/5.23%以内，白盒对抗攻击下准确率仅下降3.2%，推理路径证据可靠性评分4.57/5，满足实时部署要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部生物识别系统面临生成式AI与高保真物理欺骗交织的复合威胁，而现有防御方法存在系统性瓶颈：泛化能力差、推理过程不可审计、依赖大规模低质量数据集。这催生了对兼具可解释性、鲁棒性、以及数据高效性的统一防伪检测框架的迫切需求。

Method: 提出MFAD框架：(1) 通过细粒度像素-语义锚定机制激活多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的内在推理能力，无需外部工具或粗对齐，消除定位幻觉并确保推理路径可审计；(2) 引入跨攻击语义级统一标注范式，每个攻击类别仅标注1000个精确掩码即可生成与欺骗区域严格对应的推理证据链；(3) 基于Qwen-VL基础模型进行监督微调。

Result: 在有限高质量样本下实现40-50%的域内ACER相对降低；跨域性能退化限制在11.62%/5.23%以内，显著优于现有框架；白盒对抗攻击下检测准确率仅下降3.2%；领域从业者对推理路径证据可靠性评分4.57/5；推理延迟满足实时部署需求。

Conclusion: 少量高质量语义标注范式能够有效构建可信、可解释且成本高效的统一人脸防伪检测系统，验证了语义锚定相较大规模短文本数据训练模型的鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Facial biometric recognition systems currently face compound threats intertwining generative AI and high-fidelity physical spoofing. Existing defenses suffer from systemic bottlenecks, including poor generalization, non-auditable reasoning, and reliance on massive, low-quality datasets. To address these challenges, we propose Multimodal Large Language Models (MFAD) for face anti-spoofing detection, an explainable reasoning system for Unified Face Anti-Spoofing Detection (UFAD), accompanied by a semantic-level annotation benchmark. Unlike methods relying on external tools or coarse alignment, MFAD activates the intrinsic reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) via a fine-grained pixel-semantic anchoring mechanism. This eliminates localization hallucinations and ensures auditable reasoning paths. We introduce a cross-attack semantic-level unified annotation paradigm: by annotating only 1,000 precise masks per attack category, we generate reasoning evidence chains strictly corresponding to spoofed regions. Supervised fine-tuning on the Qwen-VL foundation model demonstrates that, using limited high-quality samples, the system achieves a 40-50% relative reduction in in-domain ACER and restricts cross-domain performance degradation to within 11.62%/5.23%, significantly outperforming existing frameworks. Furthermore, under white-box adversarial attacks, detection accuracy drops by only 3.2%, validating the robustness of semantic anchoring compared to models trained on massive short-text data. Domain practitioners rated the evidence reliability of reasoning paths at 4.57/5, with inference latency satisfying real-time deployment requirements. These results confirm that a few-shot, high-quality semantic annotation paradigm is effective for building trustworthy, explainable, and cost-efficient UFAD systems.

</details>


### [89] [Learning latent progression states from spatial heterogeneity in uterine histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17337)
*Qiming He,Yan Liu,Shuang Ge,Fan Yang,Yuxiang Wang,Ieng Man Zhang,Jing Yang,Zihao Jia,Ajin Hu,Yexing Zhang,Zixiu Song,Qiang Huang,Xiaoya Zhao,Zihan Wang,Xianjing Zheng,Yijun Zheng,Liling Lin,Shuxing Liu,Bin Bao,Yue Xie,Tian Guan,Yonghong He,Congrong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaTIE，一个子宫特异性计算病理学框架，利用10,426张子宫H&E全切片图像学习形态学感知表征，将空间组织病理学异质性组织为与肿瘤进展相关的肿瘤状态。SpaTIE在TCGA-UCEC和TCGA-UCS队列中验证，支持诊断、分子和生存相关预测任务，无需时间或分子监督即可从横断面形态推断肿瘤状态轴，并通过多组学整合和虚拟扰动揭示了与染色质调控、拷贝数变异、受体酪氨酸激酶信号等相关的分子程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤进展伴随着组织结构、形态和微环境的变化，但进展相关的异质性通常被压缩为静态的诊断类别。缺乏能够从空间组织病理学中恢复进展相关信息并推断肿瘤状态的框架。

Method: SpaTIE框架：(1)基于10,426张子宫H&E WSI构建形态学感知表征；(2)学习形态学流形以支持多种预测任务；(3)无需时间或分子监督，从横断面形态推断肿瘤状态轴；(4)通过多组学整合（DNA甲基化、CNV、突变、RNA-seq、RPPA）关联推断状态；(5)进展引导的虚拟扰动优先排序分子特征。

Result: SpaTIE学习到的表征形成形态学流形，支持诊断、分子和生存相关预测任务。推断的肿瘤状态在空间上具有连贯性，与临床病理变量和生存结果相关，但不会简单复现分期或诊断标签。多组学分析将状态与染色质调控、拷贝数相关结构变异、RTK信号、细胞粘附、ECM重塑和代谢适应等分子程序联系起来。虚拟扰动进一步优先排序了与形态学状态组织耦合的分子特征。

Conclusion: 子宫组织病理学包含可恢复的进展相关肿瘤状态信息，SpaTIE建立了将空间形态学与多组学信息结合的肿瘤状态发现框架，为癌症进展研究提供了新的计算病理学工具。

Abstract: Tumor progression is accompanied by changes in architecture, morphology and microenvironmental organization, yet progression-associated heterogeneity is usually compressed into static diagnostic categories in histopathology. Here we present SpaTIE, a uterus-specific computational pathology framework that learns morphology-aware representations and organizes spatial histopathological heterogeneity into progression-associated tumor states. SpaTIE was developed using 10,426 uterine hematoxylin and eosin whole-slide images and evaluated in TCGA-UCEC and TCGA-UCS cohorts. The learned representations formed morphology manifolds, supported diagnostic, molecular and survival-related prediction tasks, and localized attention to informative tumor regions. Beyond supervised prediction, SpaTIE inferred tumor-state axes from cross-sectional morphology without temporal or molecular supervision. These morphology-derived states were spatially coherent and showed associations with clinicopathological variables and survival outcomes, while not simply recapitulating staging or diagnostic labels. Integrative multi-omics analyses linked the inferred states to DNA methylation, somatic copy-number variation, mutation, RNA-seq and RPPA profiles, highlighting molecular programs related to chromatin regulation, copy-number-associated structural variation, receptor tyrosine kinase signaling, cell adhesion, extracellular-matrix remodeling and metabolic adaptation. Progression-guided virtual perturbation further prioritized molecular features coupled to the morphology-derived state organization. Together, these findings suggest that uterine histopathology contains recoverable progression-associated tumor-state information and establish SpaTIE as a framework for connecting spatial morphology with multi-omics-informed tumor-state discovery.

</details>


### [90] [Primitive-Driven Compositional Forensic Visual Prompting for Open-World Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17351)
*Fangling Jiang,Qi Li,Bing Liu,Weining Wang,Quilin Huang,Zhenan Sun,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的开放世界人脸反欺骗框架——组合式法证视觉提示学习（Compositional Forensic Visual Prompt Learning），完全在视觉特征空间中操作。基于冻结的ViT基础模型，通过patch级注意力将一组可学习的微法证原语细化为局部法证证据单元，再由类别特定的全局上下文提示提供输入依赖的路由权重，自适应地选择并组合这些原语以进行真/假判别。在九个开放世界协议上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界人脸反欺骗需同时应对协变量偏移（源/目标域成像条件不同）和语义偏移（目标域包含训练中未见过的攻击类型）。现有基于提示的方法通过类别语义或语言引导建模欺骗，擅长表达高层概念，但难以显式捕捉未见攻击中不断演变的细粒度、空间异质的法证证据。作者假设许多未见攻击可由重复出现的视觉线索的新组合来表征，因此提出在纯视觉特征空间中学习组合式提示。

Method: 框架基于冻结的ViT视觉基础模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) patch-aware attention将一组共享的可学习微法证原语（micro-forensic primitives）细化为从图像patch中派生的局部法证证据单元；(2) 类别特定的全局上下文提示产生输入依赖的路由权重，自适应地选择和组合这些原语形成组合式法证视觉提示；(3) 原语不预设语义含义，其专门化和复用通过跨类别的共享参数化和联合优化涌现。

Result: 在九个开放世界协议上进行的大量实验表明，该方法取得了最优性能，展现出强大的跨域泛化能力和对未见攻击的鲁棒适应能力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"未见攻击可由已知视觉线索的新组合表征"这一假设，证明在纯视觉特征空间中通过组合式提示学习能够有效应对开放世界人脸反欺骗中的协变量与语义双重偏移，无需依赖类别语义或语言先验。

Abstract: Open-world face anti-spoofing must address both covariate and semantic shifts: source and target domains differ in imaging conditions, while target domains contain diverse attack types absent from training. Existing prompt-based approaches often express spoofing through category semantics or language guidance, which is effective for modeling high-level concepts but is less suited to explicitly capturing the evolving fine-grained and spatially heterogeneous forensic evidence of unseen attacks. Motivated by the hypothesis that many unseen attacks can be characterized by new combinations of recurring visual cues, we propose a compositional forensic visual prompt learning framework that operates entirely in the visual feature space.Built on a frozen ViT-based vision foundation model, the framework employs patch-aware attention to refine a shared set of learnable micro-forensic primitives into localized forensic evidence units derived from image patches. Class-specific global contextual prompts then provide input-dependent routing weights that adaptively select and compose these primitives into compositional forensic visual prompts for real/spoof discrimination. The primitives are not assigned predefined semantic meanings; instead, their specialization and reuse emerge from shared parameterization and joint optimization across categories.Extensive experiments on nine open-world protocols demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, strong cross-domain generalization, and robust adaptation to unseen attacks.

</details>


### [91] [Continuity-Driven Representation Learning for Industrial Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17362)
*Minjong Kim,Hyun Jun Kim,Jeongrae Kim,Heeseung Shin,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种连续性驱动的表示正则化框架，利用工业检测图像中大量正常主导区域作为密集辅助监督信号，通过多连续性损失和差分损失两种与检测器无关的目标函数来稳定正常区域表示，同时保留缺陷相关的局部不连续性，在多个数据集和六种检测器架构上均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业缺陷检测与自然图像目标检测不同：检测图像在受控条件下拍摄，包含大量正常主导区域和重复结构，缺陷表现为可预测模式的局部扰动。然而传统检测器主要依赖稀疏的边界框监督，导致正常区域表示约束不足，难以充分利用图像中丰富的正常区域结构信息。

Method: 提出连续性驱动的表示正则化框架，包含两个与检测器无关的目标：1) Multi-Continuity Loss，结合一维patch序列预测和二维掩码空间预测；2) Differencing Loss，正则化相邻patch嵌入之间的一阶特征变化和二阶曲率。两个目标均通过边界框派生的区域权重进行加权，以稳定正常区域表示同时保留缺陷相关的不连续信息。

Result: 在工业金属、MEA两个真实工业数据集和公开NEU-DET基准上，覆盖YOLO系列、MambaYOLO和DETR等六种检测器架构，均取得一致性改进。全量数据设置下mAP@0.5:0.95最高提升3.49/5.38/5.03个百分点；在仅用25%训练数据的有限数据条件下，Differencing Loss在NEU-DET上最高提升mAP@0.5达21.07个百分点、mAP@0.5:0.95达8.23个百分点。

Conclusion: 连续性驱动的正则化提供了有效的先验，能够显著提升工业缺陷检测性能，尤其是在标注数据稀缺的情况下收益更为明显，验证了利用正常主导区域作为密集监督信号的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Industrial defect detection differs from natural-image object detection because inspection images are captured under controlled conditions and contain large normal-dominant regions with repetitive structures. Defects therefore appear as localized disruptions of otherwise predictable patterns, while conventional detectors rely mainly on sparse bounding-box supervision, resulting in weakly constrained normal-region representations. We propose a continuity-driven representation regularization framework that exploits normal-dominant regions as dense auxiliary supervision. The framework introduces two detector-agnostic objectives: Multi-Continuity Loss, which combines 1D patch-sequence prediction and 2D masked spatial prediction, and Differencing Loss, which regularizes first-order feature variation and second-order curvature between neighboring patch embeddings. Both objectives are applied with box-derived region weighting to stabilize normal-region representations while preserving defect-related discontinuities.
  Experiments on two real-world industrial datasets and the public NEU-DET benchmark, using six detector architectures including YOLO-family models, MambaYOLO, and DETR, demonstrate consistent improvements over native detector baselines. In the full-data setting, the proposed regularizers improve average mAP@0.5:0.95 by up to 3.49 percentage points on Industrial Metal, 5.38 percentage points on MEA, and 5.03 percentage points on NEU-DET. Under limited-data conditions, the gains become more pronounced, with Differencing Loss achieving improvements of up to 21.07 percentage points in mAP@0.5 and 8.23 percentage points in mAP@0.5:0.95 on NEU-DET using only 25% of the training data. These results suggest that continuity-driven regularization provides an effective prior for improving industrial defect detection, particularly when annotated data are scarce.

</details>


### [92] [GeoWeaver: Accurate Long-Sequence 3D Reconstruction via Hierarchical Geometric Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17389)
*Tinghao Jiang,Sheng Tang,Shengzhe Wei,Juntong Fang,Weiqi Zhang,Junsheng Zhou,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoWeaver是一个面向长序列RGB视频三维重建的统一框架，包含几何先验模型（GPM）和测试时自适应（TTA）两个核心组件。GPM分块预测深度、置信度及相机参数作为可调节几何先验；TTA通过顺序初始化、全局块级Sim(3)对齐和由粗到精的相机位姿/仿射深度/内参细化，联合优化2D重投影与3D一致性残差，解决分块处理带来的尺度漂移、位姿误差和点云错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈模型在深度和位姿预测上表现优异，但内存开销阻碍其直接在长序列上进行联合推理；分块处理虽提高可扩展性，却导致独立预测的块之间存在尺度漂移、位姿误差和点云不对齐的问题。因此需要一个既能保持局部几何精度又能修正全局累积误差的统一框架。

Method: GeoWeaver由两部分组成：(1) 几何先验模型（GPM）预测分块深度、置信度和相机参数作为可调整的几何先验；(2) 测试时自适应（TTA）执行顺序初始化、全局块级Sim(3)对齐、以及相机位姿/仿射深度/内参的由粗到精细化。利用密集对应关系提供相邻、跨块和长距离约束，通过鲁棒的CDF风格目标函数联合优化加权2D重投影与3D一致性残差。

Result: 在多个长序列基准测试上，GeoWeaver显著提升了相机精度、全局一致性和点云质量。消融实验验证了各自适应阶段各自的贡献；将相同TTA过程应用于不同几何先验模型均能一致地改善其轨迹估计，证明框架具有模型无关性。

Conclusion: GeoWeaver通过"分块预测+测试时全局自适应"的范式，在保持局部几何精度的同时有效修正长序列重建中累积的位姿、尺度、深度和标定误差，且不依赖于特定几何先验模型，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Long-sequence 3D reconstruction from RGB videos requires both accurate local geometry and globally consistent camera motion. Feed-forward models provide strong depth and pose predictions, but their memory cost prevents joint inference over long sequences. Chunk-wise processing improves scalability, yet independently predicted chunks often exhibit scale drift, pose errors, and point-cloud misalignment. We present GeoWeaver, a unified framework comprising a Geometric Prior Model (GPM) and Test-Time Adaptation (TTA). The GPM predicts chunk-wise depth, confidence, and camera parameters as adjustable geometric priors. TTA then performs sequential initialization, global chunk-level Sim(3) alignment, and coarse-to-fine refinement of camera poses, affine depth corrections, and intrinsics. Dense correspondences provide adjacent, cross-chunk, and long-range constraints, while a robust CDF-style objective jointly optimizes weighted 2D reprojection and 3D consistency residuals. This design preserves local geometric accuracy while correcting accumulated pose, scale, depth, and calibration errors. Experiments across diverse long-sequence benchmarks demonstrate improved camera accuracy, global consistency, and point-cloud quality. Ablations verify the contribution of each adaptation stage, and applying the same TTA procedure to different geometric prior models consistently improves their trajectory estimates, demonstrating that GeoWeaver is not tied to a specific GPM.

</details>


### [93] [Noisy group neurons with synchronous resetting for high-performance spiking neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17394)
*Yajie Zhai,Yanmei Kang,Meng Li,Zigang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种带噪群体神经元（NGN）模型，结合群体层面的同步重置和神经随机性，并基于平均场动力学将其与反向传播学习框架结合，在多个视觉和神经形态数据集上取得高性能，尤其在CIFAR10-DVS上以10个推理时间步达到87.35%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练深度脉冲神经网络（SNN）仍然面临两大挑战：时空信息丢失和梯度不匹配（gradient mismatching），亟需同时解决这两个问题以提升SNN的表达能力和训练效率。

Method: 提出带噪群体神经元（NGN）模型，其核心机制包括群体水平的同步重置（population-level synchronous resetting）和神经随机性（neural stochasticity），并基于平均场动力学（mean-field dynamics）将其与反向传播学习相结合，形成统一的NGN方法框架。

Result: 通过理论分析和在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、DVS-Gesture、N-Caltech101及CIFAR10-DVS等数据集上的实验验证，NGN方法在CIFAR10-DVS上仅用10个推理时间步即达到87.35%的准确率。

Conclusion: NGN是一种实用的高性能神经形态计算方法，通过引入群体神经元的随机性和同步重置机制，有效缓解了SNN训练中的信息损失与梯度不匹配问题，为实现高性能神经形态计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs), characterized by bio-inspired neuronal dynamics and event-driven communication, have attained significant progress in recent years. Nevertheless, training deep SNNs remains challenging due to spatiotemporal information loss and gradient mismatching. To simultaneously address these issues, we propose a noisy group neuron (NGN) model, which incorporates population-level synchronous resetting and neural stochasticity as fundamental computational mechanisms. We then develop the NGN method as a framework that combines the NGN model with backpropagation learning based on mean-field dynamics. We demonstrate the advantages of the NGN method through theoretical analysis and experimental validation on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet, DVS-Gesture, N-Caltech101, and CIFAR10-DVS. The proposed approach achieves an accuracy of 87.35% on CIFAR10-DVS within 10 inference time steps. These results support NGN as a practical approach to high-performance neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [94] [To Remove or Not to Remove Clouds: A Comparative Analysis and Fusion of Raw SAR and Synthetic NDWI for Overcast Water Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17398)
*Saleh Sakib Ahmed,Sara Nowreen,M. Sohel Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨洪灾期间云层遮蔽光学卫星的问题，对比了两种洪水水体分割策略：直接使用原始SAR数据，或使用深度学习翻译得到的合成NDWI影像。研究发现合成NDWI效果更好，因其翻译过程可滤除雷达噪声；进而提出结合两者的混合框架，融合SAR的物理边界清晰性与合成NDWI的高对比度，性能优于所有单一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洪灾期间云层会遮蔽光学卫星，而SAR虽能穿透云层但其原始数据噪声大、对比度低。现有研究用深度学习将SAR翻译为无云合成光学影像用于水体分割，但存在关键方法学困境：完全阴天时，分割模型应直接处理原始SAR，还是使用翻译后的合成NDWI代理？该问题尚无定论。

Method: 首先通过实验对比直接使用原始SAR与使用合成NDWI进行水体分割的性能，验证翻译过程可作为强大的雷达噪声滤波器。基于此发现，提出Combined Framework混合框架，将原始SAR与合成NDWI同时集成到统一模型中，融合两者特征进行联合分割。

Result: 合成NDWI在分割性能上优于直接使用原始SAR，说明翻译过程的去噪效应有效；结合两者的混合框架通过融合原始SAR的物理边界与合成NDWI的高对比度，在所有评估中一致优于各独立方法。

Conclusion: 研究解决了SAR直接分割与合成NDWI代理之间的方法学争议，证明合成NDWI更优，并提出混合框架实现了最佳性能，为洪水监测中云遮蔽条件下的水体分割提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Persistent clouds blind optical satellites during floods. While Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) penetrates clouds, its raw data is noisy and lacks clear contrast. To mitigate this, recent studies utilize deep learning models to translate SAR into cloud-free synthetic optical imagery for downstream tasks like water body segmentation. However, because raw SAR is the original source for both of these operations, a critical methodological dilemma arises: during complete overcast should segmentation models process the raw SAR directly, or rely on a translated synthetic Normalized Difference Water Index (NDWI) proxy? This study resolves the debate by demonstrating that synthetic NDWI yields better results, as the translation process acts as a powerful filter against radar noise. This raises a natural second question: what if we utilize both? Building on our findings, we introduce a Combined Framework that integrates both raw SAR and synthetic NDWI into a unified model. By fusing the sharp physical boundaries of raw SAR with the high contrast of synthetic NDWI, this hybrid approach consistently outperforms all standalone methods.

</details>


### [95] [MoE-ViE: Mixture of Experts Vision Encoder for Efficient Image and Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17402)
*Bonan Zhang,Shiyu Dong,Quan Hung Tran,Katharina Gschwind,Shuqi Yang,Sijia Chen,Adel Ahmadyan,Seungwhan Moon,Lu Zhang,Ahmed Kirmani,Babak Damavandi,Anuj Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了CLIP风格视觉编码器在MoE架构下的缩放设计，提出了细粒度MoE拓扑、无辅助损失的平衡变体、专用MoE内核以及帧级蒸馏与冻结机制，训练出一系列MoE-ViE模型，在图像和视频任务上均超越同规模及更大规模的稠密编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密视觉编码器的缩放虽然有效但计算成本和推理延迟随之增加，而MoE架构在LLM中已被证明可实现高效缩放，但其在SOTA级别的CLIP风格视觉编码器中的设计空间尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 系统研究不同MoE拓扑，发现细粒度MoE拓扑优于稠密和标准MoE；2) 提出无辅助损失的平衡变体以改善专家利用率；3) 设计专用MoE内核以缓解推理延迟开销；4) 引入帧级蒸馏结合新颖冻结机制，在增强视频能力的同时保持图像知识；5) 训练多尺寸的MoE-ViE系列模型。

Result: 所有尺寸的MoE-ViE均一致优于其稠密对应模型。最大模型以76%的延迟匹配了1.7倍规模SOTA编码器的零样本性能；在对齐LLM后，MoE-ViE在图像和视频基准上超越所有对比编码器，包括激活参数多至5倍的其他模型。

Conclusion: MoE架构是高效缩放视觉编码器的有效方案，细粒度MoE拓扑结合精心设计的训练与推理优化，能够在降低计算和延迟成本的同时，在图像和视频多模态任务上取得SOTA水平的表现。

Abstract: Vision encoders are a critical component of vision-language models, and scaling their capacity effectively improves performance. However, dense scaling increases compute cost and inference latency. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer a compelling alternative, having enabled efficient scaling in LLMs, yet the MoE design space for CLIP-style vision encoders remains underexplored at State-of-the-Art (SOTA) levels. In this work, we systematically study MoE designs for vision encoder scaling and find that fine-grained MoE topologies yield substantial gains over both dense and standard MoE counterparts. We further propose an auxiliary-loss-free balancing variant for better expert utilization, and design a specialized MoE kernel to mitigate inference latency overhead. To enhance video capabilities while preserving image knowledge, we introduce frame-level distillation paired with a novel freezing mechanism. We pretrain a series of Mixture-of-Experts Vision Encoders (MoE-ViE) across a range of sizes, all consistently outperforming their dense counterparts. Our largest model matches the zero-shot performance of a SOTA encoder 1.7x its size at 76% of its latency. When aligned with an LLM, MoE-ViE surpasses all compared encoders on image and video benchmarks, including those with up to 5x more activated parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/moe_vie.

</details>


### [96] [REChart: Reasoning-Efficient Chart Editing with Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17414)
*Yuanbang Liu,Chenxi Ruan,Yihan Hou,Qiong Luo,Wei Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REChart，一个两阶段训练框架，用于改善多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在图表编辑任务中的表现。研究发现推理长度与图表编辑性能之间存在"倒U型"关系，过度推理会导致"过度思考"问题。REChart通过第一阶段的过程级监督（200k高质量推理轨迹的SFT）和第二阶段的强化学习（忠诚度奖励+效率奖励）来同时提升编辑保真度和推理效率，在ChartEdit和ChartMIMIC基准上达到开源模型SOTA，并将推理token使用量减少79.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表编辑任务需要从参考图表图像中推断并修改可视化代码，这对MLLM的细粒度视觉推理、指令跟随和可执行代码合成能力提出挑战。虽然大型推理模型（LRMs）借助扩展的思维链推理适合处理此类复杂多模态任务，但初步研究发现推理长度与性能呈"倒U型"关系——过度推理会导致模型向幻觉视觉细节漂移或陷入冗余推理循环（即"overthinking"问题）。现有方法缺乏对中间推理过程的有效监督，无法平衡推理深度与编辑质量。

Method: REChart采用两阶段训练框架。(1) 第一阶段：从大规模图像-指令-代码池中合成200k高质量推理轨迹，使用角色专业化的智能体工作流Reason-Score-Refine，对图表代码进行迭代评分和精化，最终用于监督微调（SFT）。(2) 第二阶段：通过强化学习优化模型，使用两个互补的奖励函数——忠诚度奖励（评估代码正确性、视觉保真度和结构一致性）和效率奖励（为每个rollout分配随机思考预算，截断推理过程，并根据最终推理段对输出的贡献给予信用分配）。

Result: 在ChartEdit和ChartMIMIC基准上，REChart在同等规模的开源模型中达到图表编辑的SOTA性能，同时缓解了过度思考问题。与基础模型相比，在最大思考预算为16,384个token的条件下，平均推理token使用量减少了79.0%。

Conclusion: REChart通过结合过程级监督和双奖励强化学习，有效解决了图表编辑中因过度推理导致的性能退化问题，在保持高编辑保真度的同时大幅提升推理效率，验证了对推理过程进行精细控制和管理是提升MLLM在复杂多模态任务上表现的关键方向。

Abstract: Chart editing requires inferring and modifying visualization code from a reference chart image based on an editing instruction, challenging fine-grained visual reasoning, instruction following, and executable code synthesis capabilities of MLLMs. Large reasoning models (LRMs) with extended Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning are suitable for tackling such complex multimodal tasks. However, our preliminary study reveals an ``inverted-U'' relationship between reasoning length and chart-editing performance: Excessive reasoning often leads to ``overthinking,'' where models drift toward hallucinated visual details or get stuck in redundant reasoning loops. To address the gap, we introduce REChart, a two-stage training framework that provides process-level supervision over intermediate reasoning steps, improving both editing fidelity and reasoning efficiency. First, we synthesize 200k high-quality reasoning trajectories for supervised fine-tuning from a large image-instruction-code pool, using a role-specialized agentic Reason-Score-Refine workflow that iteratively refine the chart code toward higher quality. Second, we optimize the model via reinforcement learning with two complementary rewards: a \emph{fidelity} reward evaluating code correctness, visual fidelity, and structural consistency, and an \emph{efficiency} reward that assigns each rollout a random thinking budget, truncates the reasoning process, and credits the final reasoning segment according to its contribution to the output. On the ChartEdit and ChartMIMIC benchmarks, our model achieves state-of-the-art chart-editing performance among open-source models of comparable scale, while mitigating overthinking and reducing average reasoning token usage by 79.0\% under a maximum thinking budget of 16,384 tokens compared with the base model.

</details>


### [97] [Spectral Gradient Orthogonalization Improves Differentially Private Training at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17415)
*Sabari Shanmugam,Nick Barnes,Kerry Taylor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于极分解（polar decomposition）的谱梯度正交化后处理技术，在差分隐私训练中仅通过将添加的各向同性高斯噪声投影到信号主导的低秩子空间来恢复方向性信号，无需额外隐私成本。该方法在中等至高SNR区间（如大批量、高容量模型）显著提升准确率（WRN-28-10上+20.9%，ResNet-18上+14.9%），但受相变阈值支配——低SNR时正交化会适得其反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DP-SGD对裁剪后的梯度添加各向同性高斯噪声，每个奇异方向受到同等扰动。然而视觉模型中的空间相关性将梯度能量集中在低秩子空间中，大量噪声落在几乎无信号的维度上，导致隐私预算被浪费在无关方向上。作者旨在从含噪梯度的低秩结构中恢复方向性信号，以零额外隐私成本提升DP训练的效用。

Method: 引入极分解对含噪梯度矩阵进行正交化的后处理步骤。具体来说：1) 对含噪梯度进行极分解，提取其正交因子以重新定向梯度方向；2) 识别出该变换的效用由相变阈值决定，该阈值由梯度的谱间隙（spectral gap）支配；3) 在大批量训练（B=4096）等足够高的SNR条件下，奇异向量可被可靠恢复，正交化有效。作者还将其与时间去噪（temporal denoising）结合，并与DP-Adam对比微调稳定性。

Result: 谱正交化在WRN-28-10（B=4096）上比DP-SGD提升+20.9%，ResNet-18上提升+14.9%，同时将跨运行方差降低2-3倍。在微调场景中匹配DP-Adam的稳定性且保持一阶内存占用。结合谱与时间去噪在CIFAR-10（epsilon=4）上达到50.3%的准确率，为所有测试配置中最高。但增益仅限中等至高SNR区间（大批量、高容量模型）；小批量或低SNR场景中DP-SGD或时间去噪更优。

Conclusion: 谱梯度正交化是一种零隐私成本的梯度方向恢复技术，其有效性受谱SNR相变支配。它在大批量训练高容量视觉模型时提供显著且稳定的提升，但在低SNR场景应避免使用。该工作揭示了DP训练中梯度低秩结构与噪声之间的交互，为未来在DP优化器中利用谱结构信息提供了方向。

Abstract: Differentially private training adds isotropic Gaussian noise to clipped gradients, corrupting every singular direction equally. In vision models, where spatial correlation concentrates gradient energy into a low-rank subspace, most of this noise falls in directions that carry little signal. Spectral gradient orthogonalization via polar decomposition is introduced as a post-processing step that recovers directional signal from the noisy gradient's low-rank structure at zero additional privacy cost. A phase transition governs the utility of this approach: orthogonalization improves accuracy only when the per-direction spectral signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) suffices for singular vector recovery; in low-SNR regimes, the directional bias of the gradient is replaced by a nearly random orthogonal update, and the transformation is harmful. The recovery threshold is determined by the spectral gap of the gradient and is surpassed at large batch sizes. Empirically, the benefit scales with model capacity: spectral orthogonalization achieves a +20.9% improvement over DP-SGD on WRN-28-10 (B = 4096) and +14.9% on ResNet-18, while reducing inter-run variance by a factor of two to three. In the fine-tuning regime, spectral orthogonalization matches the stability of DP-Adam while maintaining a first-order memory footprint. Combining spectral with temporal denoising yields 50.3% on CIFAR-10 (epsilon = 4), the highest accuracy in any tested configuration. These gains are specific to moderate-to-high-SNR regimes such as large-batch training of higher-capacity models. Small-batch or low-SNR settings are better served by DP-SGD or temporal denoising.

</details>


### [98] [SPVC: Structured and Panoptic Video Fixing for Cross-Dataset Driving Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17420)
*Gen Li,Shu Han,Yun Xi Qiao,Hua Chen,Xuyang Dai,Bohan Li,Hao Zhao,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPVC框架，一种结构化和全景的视频修复框架，用于跨数据集驾驶场景渲染。该框架利用显式空间条件（相机位姿、3D边界框、HD地图）引导修复过程，同时校正背景渲染伪影和前景车辆编辑伪影，在视频序列级别进行修复，并支持单个共享网络跨多个驾驶数据集训练和应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景重建与渲染（尤其基于3D Gaussian Splatting）在自动驾驶模拟中至关重要，但在外推的ego轨迹和场景编辑下，渲染视图会出现模糊结构、时间闪烁和前后景错位等问题。现有的细化方法通常针对特定设置设计，如图像级新视角修复或对象编辑校正，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: SPVC框架遵循四个设计原则：(1)结构化修复——使用相机位姿、3D边界框和HD地图等显式空间条件引导修复过程以减少不可控的幻觉；(2)全景修复——同时校正背景渲染伪影（如扭曲的道路、建筑物和车道）和前景车辆编辑伪影（如物体外观不一致）；(3)视频修复——在驾驶序列上操作而非孤立帧，利用时间线索校正伪影；(4)跨数据集修复——单个共享网络在多个驾驶数据集上训练和应用。具体实现上，通过模拟欠约束3DGS渲染和前景车辆插入伪影来构建成对的降质-干净训练数据，训练一个两阶段可控视频扩散模型，先处理视频级外观，再用结构化控制细化场景布局。

Result: SPVC通过构建成对的降质-干净训练数据，训练两阶段可控视频扩散模型，实现了跨多个驾驶数据集的统一修复能力。该框架能同时处理背景渲染伪影和前景车辆编辑伪影，并在视频序列级别利用时间线索进行修复。

Conclusion: SPVC提出了一个统一的、结构化且全景的视频修复框架，解决了驾驶场景3DGS渲染在轨迹外推和场景编辑下的退化问题，通过显式结构化控制和两阶段视频扩散模型实现了跨数据集的鲁棒修复，减少了为每个数据集或场景单独训练修复器的需求。

Abstract: Driving scene reconstruction and rendering, especially with 3D Gaussian Splatting, has become an important component of autonomous driving simulation. However, rendered views often degrade under extrapolated ego trajectories and scene edits, producing blurry structures, temporal flicker, and foreground-background misalignment. Existing refinement methods are commonly designed for a specific setting, such as image-level novel-view repair or object-editing correction. In this paper, we introduce SPVC, a structured and panoptic video fixing framework for cross-dataset driving scene rendering. The name summarizes four design principles. (1) Structured fixing denotes the use of explicit spatial conditions, including camera pose, 3D bounding boxes, and HD maps, to guide the repair process and reduce uncontrolled hallucination. (2) Panoptic fixing refers to correcting both background rendering artifacts, such as distorted roads, buildings, and lanes, and foreground vehicle artifacts introduced by scene editing, such as inconsistent object appearance. (3) Video fixing means that the model operates on driving sequences rather than isolated frames, allowing temporal cues to be used during artifact correction. (4) Cross-dataset fixing means that a single shared network is trained and applied across multiple driving datasets, reducing the need for dataset-specific or scene-specific fixers. Concretely, we construct paired degraded-clean training data by simulating under-constrained 3DGS rendering and foreground vehicle insertion artifacts, and train a two-stage controllable video diffusion model that first addresses video-level appearance and then refines scene layout with structured controls.

</details>


### [99] [TEAMS: Text-prompted spatiotEmporal dual-heAd Mamba Snake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17421)
*Ruicheng Zhang,Jianhui Lei,Kaiwen Shen,Haowei Guo,Jun Zhou,Bin Chen,Mengtang Li,Shen Zhao,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TEAMS是一种结合视觉-语言Mamba与Snake的全新实例分割框架，通过时空Snake演化策略、轮廓形态感知Mamba和文本提示协作双头Snake三个创新模块，有效解决了复杂形态变化、细粒度器官细节捕获和基准检测错误修正等挑战，在五个医学影像数据集上超越了现有语义分割与snake分割方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的deep snake实例分割方法虽能克服像素级误分类问题（如空洞和锯齿边缘），但在处理复杂形态变化、捕获精细器官细节以及修正基础检测错误方面仍存在明显不足。作者旨在通过引入状态空间模型和文本提示等多模态线索来突破这些局限。

Method: 提出TEAMS框架，包含三大核心创新：(1)时空Snake演化策略(SSES)，在状态空间模型中同时捕捉蛇轮廓上的双向空间依赖和演化步骤间的时序动态；(2)轮廓形态感知Mamba(CMAM)，量化局部轮廓形态以调制Mamba2 SSD双形式中的结构化注意力掩码，扩展Mamba对输入序列元素相对重要性的感知能力；(3)文本提示协作双头Snake(TCDHS)，融合文本提示线索并将演化后的轮廓信息传递给基础检测头。

Result: 在涵盖不同器官和影像模态的五个数据集上进行了全面评估，TEAMS在所有指标上均优于现有语义分割和deep snake分割方法，例如在脊柱数据集上实现了6.9%的相对mDice提升和9.1%的相对mBF提升。

Conclusion: TEAMS展现出作为多样化医学图像分割场景中可靠工具的潜力，证明了结合状态空间建模、文本提示和多头协作的有效性。该工作为医学图像实例分割提供了新的方法范式，特别是在处理复杂形态和精细结构方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Deep snake is a promising family of instance segmentation methods that accurately predicts object-level contours, thereby overcoming common pixel-level misclassification issues such as mask cavities and jagged edges in semantic segmentation approaches. However, existing deep snake methods face challenges in handling complex morphological variations, accurately capturing fine-grained organ details, and correcting base detection errors. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a cohesive Text-prompted spatiotEmporal dual-heAd Mamba Snake (TEAMS), a novel vision-language Mamba snake framework with three key innovations: (1) A Spatiotemporal Snake Evolution Strategy (SSES) is introduced to tackle complex morphological variations by capturing bidirectional spatial dependencies along the snake contour and temporal dynamics across evolution steps in a state space model. (2) A Contour Morphology-Aware Mamba (CMAM) is proposed to quantify local contour morphologies to modulate the structured attention mask in the Mamba2 SSD dual form, which extends Mamba's capability to perceive the relative importance of its input sequence elements for better delineation of fine-grained organ details. (3) A Text-prompted Collaborative Dual-Head Snake (TCDHS) is designed to incorporate cues from textual prompts and transfer the evolved contour information to the base detection head, which enhances the deep snake workflow and mitigates wrong detections. Comprehensive evaluations on five datasets covering different organs and imaging modalities demonstrate that TEAMS outperforms existing semantic and deep snake segmentation methods (e.g., relative mDice/mBF improvements of 6.9%/9.1% in a spinal dataset), underscoring its potential as a reliable tool across diverse medical image segmentation scenarios.

</details>


### [100] [TF-CADE: Foreground-Concentrated Text-Video Alignment for Zero-Shot Temporal Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17422)
*Yearang Lee,Ho-Joong Kim,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TF-CADE，一种零样本时序动作检测方法，通过文本-前景集中对齐来增强动作类别的语义区分能力。包含两个核心模块：Action Concentrate Aggregation (ACA)将文本信息与动作相关前景区域显式对齐，以及Certainty-based Confidence Re-weighting (CCR)在推理时抑制无关动作类别，在分布内和跨数据集泛化场景均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本时序动作检测方法虽然通过改进架构层面的文本-视频对齐取得了一定效果，但仍难以捕捉动作类别之间的语义差异，导致产生与文本无关的预测结果。

Method: 提出Text-Foreground Concentrated Alignment框架(TF-CADE)，包含两个关键组件：(1) Action Concentrate Aggregation (ACA)，通过提取动作集中分数，将时序上信息丰富的视频片段聚合成前景加权视频嵌入，实现前景集中对齐以增强文本与视频特征的语义一致性和类间判别性；(2) Certainty-based Confidence Re-weighting (CCR)策略，利用前景感知的相似度细化每个片段的置信度分数，在推理时有效抑制无关动作类别。

Result: 广泛的实验评估表明TF-CADE在分布内设置下达到了最先进的性能，并且在跨数据集泛化到未见动作类别方面也表现出色。

Conclusion: 通过将文本信息与动作相关前景区域显式对齐，TF-CADE有效解决了现有方法在语义区分方面的不足，同时提升了分布内性能和跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Temporal Action Detection (ZSTAD) aims to lo- calize and recognize action instances from unseen action categories in untrimmed videos. Although existing meth- ods have shown effectiveness by advancing architectural text-video alignment, they still struggle with capturing se- mantic distinctions between action classes, resulting in text- irrelevant predictions. To address this issue, we propose a Text-Foreground Concentrated Alignment for zero-shot temporal action DEtector (TF-CADE) that explicitly aligns textual information with action-relevant foreground regions. Specifically, we introduce Action Concentrate Aggregation (ACA), which extracts action concentrate scores to aggregate temporally informative video segments into a foreground- weighted video embedding. This foreground concentrated alignment enhances the semantic consistency between text and video features and improves inter-class discriminabil- ity. In addition, a Certainty-based Confidence Re-weighting (CCR) strategy refines per-snippet confidence scores by lever- aging foreground-aware similarity, effectively suppressing irrelevant action classes during inference. Extensive evalua- tions show that our TF-CADE not only achieves state-of-the- art performance under in-distribution settings but also excels in cross-dataset generalization to unseen action classes.

</details>


### [101] [GSToken: Geometry-Structured Gaussian Tokens for Compact 3D Medical Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17425)
*Xiaoduo Li,Quan Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将高斯标记（Gaussian tokens）引入多模态脑肿瘤分割任务，每个标记不仅携带语义特征，还学习3D中心、各向异性尺度和方向，为表示提供显式几何支撑。同时提出冻结标记效用评估协议，在严格匹配条件下公平比较不同分词器的信息保留能力。实验表明GSToken在冻结探测下一致且显著优于容量匹配的自适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的3D多模态MRI分割方法在将体素压缩为token序列时，无论是固定patch编码还是TokenLearner式的可学习注意力池化，都丢弃了显式的空间形状信息，导致token无法传达病灶形态或空间范围。此外，端到端评估将分词器的信息保留与下游解码器的重建能力混淆，且缺乏统一的容量契约，使得不同方法的性能差异难以归因。

Method: 1) 提出GSToken（Gaussian tokens）：每个token由一个语义特征加上可学习的3D中心、各向异性尺度和方向组成，以极小的参数开销为表示赋予显式几何支持。2) 提出冻结token效用评估协议：训练后的分词器被冻结，其输出被转换为固定容量的序列化契约，由共享的轻量Transformer探针在严格匹配条件下独立测量各分词器保留的信息量。3) 使用多seed配对统计检验评估性能。

Result: GSToken在冻结探测下一致且显著优于容量匹配的自适应基线（如TokenLearner），在肿瘤全部子区域以及表面、距离指标上均展现出统一的优势。

Conclusion: 在token中显式编码空间几何能够显著提升体素表示的信息密度，为紧凑的3D医学图像表示和下游读取提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: Effective segmentation of multi-modal MRI is central to improving neural network accuracy in brain tumor recognition. Existing methods typically compress 3D volumes into token sequences via fixed patch encoding or learned attention pooling (e.g., TokenLearner). However, these compression schemes discard explicit spatial shape information; the resulting tokens convey no notion of lesion morphology or spatial extent. Meanwhile, end-to-end evaluation entangles a tokenizer's information retention with the reconstruction capacity of the downstream decoder, and the lack of a unified capacity contract across methods makes performance differences difficult to attribute. In this paper, we introduce Gaussian tokens to multi-modal brain tumor segmentation for the first time: each token carries not only a semantic feature but also a learned 3D center, anisotropic scale, and orientation, endowing the representation with explicit geometric support at negligible parameter cost. We further propose a frozen-token utility evaluation protocol: the trained tokenizer is frozen, its output is cast into a fixed-capacity serialized contract, and a shared lightweight Transformer probe independently measures each tokenizer's retained information under strictly matched conditions. Multi-seed paired statistical testing shows that GSToken consistently and substantially outperforms capacity-matched adaptive baselines under frozen probing, with uniform advantages across all tumor sub-regions, surface, and distance metrics. These results demonstrate that explicitly encoding spatial geometry within tokens significantly improves the information density of volumetric representations, offering a new design principle for compact 3D medical image representation and downstream reading.

</details>


### [102] [SemComp-Bench: Benchmarking Semantic Task Completion in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17426)
*Keyu Tu,Zhuowei Chen,Mengqi Huang,Yuxin Wang,Jiahao Zhu,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义任务完成视频生成"（Semantic Task Completion Video Generation），这是一个以结果为导向的视频生成任务，要求同时满足预期结果达成与语义对齐。作者构建了SemComp-Data评估数据集（覆盖六个领域）和SemComp-Bench评估协议（基于VLM回答结构化二元问题，报告OA分数与GR分数），实验表明现有视频生成模型在实现预期结果并保持参考图像的语义一致性方面仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成任务多以中间过程或外观一致性为导向，缺乏以最终结果是否达成为核心目标的评估框架。作者旨在提出一种结果导向的视频生成范式，以更好地匹配实际应用中"完成某项任务"的语义需求，并建立系统性的评估方法。

Method: (1) 提出语义任务完成视频生成任务定义，包含结果达成与语义对齐两个成功条件；(2) 构建SemComp-Data，通过四阶段可扩展的筛选流水线将原始视频转化为标准化数据实例，涵盖六个领域，每个实例包含参考图像、详细指令、简要指令和结果中心视频片段；(3) 提出SemComp-Bench评估协议，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）回答结构化二元问题，报告OA分数（Outcome Achievement）和GR分数（Generation Reliability）。

Result: 在代表性视频生成模型上的实验表明，在实现预期结果的同时保持与参考图像的任务相关语义对齐仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 语义任务完成视频生成是一个具有挑战性的新任务，现有的视频生成模型尚不能很好地同时满足结果达成和语义对齐的双重要求，该任务及配套数据与评估基准为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic Task Completion Video Generation, an outcome-oriented video generation task. Under this formulation, success requires both achievement of the intended outcome and semantic grounding. Semantic grounding characterizes the correspondence between the reference image and the generated outcome in terms of high-level semantics relevant to the task. Evaluation focuses on the generated outcome and requires neither the presentation of a complete sequence of intermediate task steps nor conventional appearance consistency with the reference image. To support systematic evaluation, we construct SemComp-Data, an evaluation dataset covering six domains. Each instance comprises a reference image, a detailed instruction, a brief instruction, and an outcome-centric video clip. A scalable four-stage curation pipeline converts raw videos into standardized SemComp-Data instances. We further introduce SemComp-Bench, an evaluation protocol that uses a vision-language model (VLM) to answer structured binary questions. SemComp-Bench reports the OA Score and the GR Score for Outcome Achievement and Generation Reliability, respectively. Experiments on representative video generation models show that achieving intended outcomes while maintaining task-relevant semantic grounding in reference images remains challenging.

</details>


### [103] [Counterfactual Anatomy-guided Spatial-Temporal Decoding for Annotation-Free Hallucination Mitigation in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17427)
*Yifan Lu,Adinath Dukre,Abhijit Das,Ziyun Zou,Haolin Yang,Yutong Xie,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAST是一个推理阶段、无需人工标注的框架，通过自动解剖区域发现（基于医学分割）+反事实干预选择致因区域，并结合分类器无关引导（空间注意力矫正）与逐步时间对比（生成动态调控）的统一对比解码，在SLAKE和MIMIC-CXR上显著减少Med-VLM的幻觉并提升空间接地能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Med-VLMs在医学视觉问答中虽表现优异但易产生幻觉（生成缺乏图像证据支持的临床语句）。现有解码阶段的幻觉缓解方法缺乏解剖意识，或过度依赖ground truth标注，限制了实际适用性。因此需要一种无需标注、具解剖感知的推理时幻觉缓解方案。

Method: CAST框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 通过广泛医学分割自动发现与查询相关的解剖区域；(2) 利用反事实干预（遮挡导致答案似然下降）选择紧凑且因果信息丰富的区域；(3) 在该区域引导下执行统一对比解码，融合classifier-free guidance以纠正空间注意力，并结合stepwise temporal contrast调节生成动态。

Result: 在SLAKE和MIMIC-CXR数据集上、跨三个Med-VLM模型验证，CAST一致优于强基线，并超越依赖ground truth的解码策略。结果表明紧凑、自动选择的区域无需专家标注即可提供高效的对比引导，同时提升空间接地性和降低幻觉。

Conclusion: CAST证明了无需人工标注的、致因性解剖区域选择能够为Med-VLM提供有效且可泛化的幻觉缓解方案，为临床场景下实际部署医学视觉语言模型提供了实用解码框架。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (Med-VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on medical visual question answering, yet they remain prone to hallucination, generating clinically unsupported statements that are insufficiently grounded in image evidence. Mitigation methods applied during decoding offer a practical solution, but they typically lack anatomical awareness or rely heavily on ground truth annotations, which limits their applicability. We propose Counterfactual Anatomy-guided Spatial-Temporal decoding (CAST), a framework that operates entirely during inference and requires no manual annotations for anatomically grounded hallucination mitigation. CAST automatically discovers anatomical regions relevant to the given query through broad medical segmentation. It then selects a compact, causally informative area using counterfactual intervention based on the drop in answer likelihood under occlusion. Guided by this chosen region, CAST performs a unified contrastive decoding process, combining classifier-free guidance to correct spatial attention with stepwise temporal contrast to regulate generation dynamics. Experiments on the SLAKE and MIMIC-CXR datasets across three Med-VLMs demonstrate that CAST consistently outperforms strong baselines and surpasses decoding strategies reliant on ground truth. Our results indicate that compact, automatically selected regions provide highly effective contrastive guidance without expert annotations, offering a practical and generalizable solution for improving spatial grounding and reducing hallucinations. Code is available at https://github.com/csyifan/CAST.

</details>


### [104] [NGS-Marker: Robust Native Watermarking for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17447)
*Hao Qin,Yukai Sun,Luyuan Chen,Mengxu Lu,Feng Zhang,Ming Kong,Zhenhong Du,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NGS-Marker是一个针对3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的原生水印框架，通过联合训练的水印注入器和消息解码器，以及基于梯度的渐进式注入策略实现全场景覆盖，从而有效防御局部侵权（对手提取并重用部分高斯子集），并支持混合保护和多模态水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS水印技术主要依赖预训练解码器保护渲染图像，无法有效保护底层3D高斯图元，尤其对局部侵权（仅重用部分高斯子集）防御效果不佳。

Method: NGS-Marker采用联合训练的水印注入器和消息解码器框架，配合基于梯度的渐进式注入策略确保水印覆盖整个场景，从而支持从任意局部区域解码所有权信息。此外扩展了结合原生与间接水印的混合保护机制，并支持多模态水印。

Result: 大量实验证明NGS-Marker能有效防御局部侵权攻击，同时为实际部署提供了灵活的实用支持。

Conclusion: NGS-Marker为3DGS提供了一种更鲁棒和灵活的原生版权保护方案，弥补了现有方法在局部侵权防御方面的不足，兼具混合保护能力和多模态扩展性。

Abstract: With the rapid development and adoption of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), the need for effective copyright protection has become increasingly critical. Existing watermarking techniques for 3DGS mainly focus on protecting rendered images via pre-trained decoders, leaving the underlying 3D Gaussian primitives vulnerable to misuse. In particular, they are ineffective against Partial Infringement, where an adversary extracts and reuses only a subset of Gaussians. In this paper, we propose NGS-Marker, a novel native watermarking framework for 3DGS. It integrates a jointly trained watermark injector and message decoder, and employs a gradientbased progressive injection strategy to ensure full-scene coverage. This enables robust ownership decoding from any local region. We further extend NGS-Marker with hybrid protection (combining native and indirect watermarks) and support for multimodal watermarking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NGS-Marker effectively defends against partial infringement while offering practical flexibility for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [105] [S$^3$AM: A Single-Stream SAM with Reliability-Calibrated Frequency Adapter for Multi-modal Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17475)
*Ruichao Hou,Boyue Xu,Tongwei Ren,Dongming Zhou,Gangshan Wu,Jinde Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单流SAM适配框架SSSAM，通过可靠性校准的频率适配器将辅助模态的高频信息注入主干网络，实现多模态显著目标检测，仅需12.20M可训练参数（占总参数5.4%）即在RGB-D、RGB-T和RGB-NIR基准上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAM适配的MSOD方法多采用双流编码器或辅助提示生成器，导致计算冗余；虽然单流方案可降低开销，但早期融合可能将噪声或错位的高频辅助信息传播到主干网络中，影响检测性能。因此需要一种既能避免重复基础模型计算、又能显式控制辅助频率注入的单流框架。

Method: 提出单流SAM适配框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 混合频率专家模块，利用平稳小波变换分解各模态并聚合跨模态频率信息；(2) 可靠性校准频率适配器，通过双门控校准机制选择性传播校准残差，同时控制注入强度和跨模态可靠性；(3) 超网络引导的语义-结构解码器，结合SAM主干提取的语义掩膜特征与基于Mamba的结构细节恢复。

Result: 在RGB-D、RGB-T和RGB-NIR三个多模态显著目标检测基准上，所提框架以仅12.20M可训练参数（占总参数5.4%）取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性校准的频率适配机制，单流SAM框架能够在避免计算冗余的同时，显式控制辅助模态高频信息的注入，在极低可训练参数下实现高效的多模态显著目标检测。

Abstract: Vision foundation models have recently advanced multi-modal salient object detection (MSOD) through parameter-efficient tuning and prompt learning. However, existing Segment Anything Model (SAM)-adapted MSOD methods often rely on dual-stream encoders or auxiliary prompt generators, leading to redundant computation. Although a single-stream alternative can reduce this cost, early fusion may also propagate noisy or misaligned auxiliary high-frequency cues through the backbone. In this paper, we propose a novel single-stream framework that integrates reliability-calibrated frequency adaptation into the adopted SAM backbone for MSOD. It avoids duplicated foundation backbones while explicitly controlling auxiliary frequency injection. Specifically, we design a mixture of frequency experts module, which uses the stationary wavelet transform to decompose each modality and aggregate cross-modal frequency information. We further introduce a reliability-calibrated frequency adapter with a dual-gate calibration mechanism, which selectively propagates the calibrated residual across transformer stages while jointly controlling its injection strength and cross-modal reliability. A hypernetwork-guided semantic-structural decoder then combines semantic mask features from the adopted backbone with Mamba-based structural detail recovery. Comprehensive experiments on RGB-D, RGB-T, and RGB-NIR salient object detection benchmarks validate that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance with only 12.20M trainable parameters, accounting for 5.4\% of the total parameters. The code will be available at https://github.com/xuboyue1999/SSSAM.

</details>


### [106] [NeuroPath: Brain-Inspired Dual-Pathway Graph Convolutional Networks for Skeleton-Based Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17487)
*Kanglei Zhou,Ruizhi Cai,Hubert P. H. Shum,Frederick W. B. Li,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NeuroPath的双路径图卷积网络，受人类视觉感知中腹侧和背侧通路启发，将空间和时间信息分离建模并协同融合，从而解决现有STGCN中隐式耦合时空信息导致的跨骨骼模态性能不均衡问题，在Kinetics Skeleton 400、NTU RGB+D 60和NTU RGB+D 120三个基准上取得了持续的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间-时间图卷积网络（STGCN）通过隐式的空间-时间表征建模骨骼结构，已取得不错效果，但经验研究发现不同骨骼模态之间存在明显的性能不平衡，说明隐式耦合时空信息限制了结构和运动互补线索的充分挖掘。

Method: 提出了NeuroPath双路径图卷积网络，具体包含三个关键组件：(1) 变换单元将输入转换为通路特异的骨骼表征，使每条通路专注于人类运动的互补方面；(2) 分组图卷积块动态识别关键身体部位并建模其时空依赖关系，捕获协调的关节行为及其相互关系；(3) 跨通路动态融合模块整合通路间的互补模态信息，促进动作的高层语义解释。

Result: 在Kinetics Skeleton 400、NTU RGB+D 60和NTU RGB+D 120三个大规模基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明在各项指标上均获得一致性的性能提升，验证了双通路时空建模对于骨骼动作识别的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过分离且协同地建模空间和时间信息，双通路架构能够更充分地表征骨骼动作的结构与运动互补线索，有效克服了隐式时空耦合带来的模态性能不平衡问题，为骨骼动作识别提供了一种新的有效范式。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition aims to recognize human actions from sequences of human joint coordinates. Most existing Spatial-Temporal Graph Convolutional Networks (STGCNs) have achieved promising results by modeling skeletal structures with implicit spatial-temporal representations. However, our empirical study reveals a clear performance imbalance across different skeletal modalities, indicating that implicitly coupling spatial and temporal information limits the full exploitation of complementary structural and motion cues. Inspired by the ventral and dorsal pathways in human perception, we propose Dual-Pathway Graph Convolutional Networks (NeuroPath), which adopt a dual-pathway architecture for separate yet collaborative modeling of spatial and temporal information. Specifically, transformation units first convert the input into pathway-specific skeletal representations, allowing each pathway to focus on complementary aspects of human motion. To further capture coordinated joint behaviors and their interrelationships, we introduce a group graph convolution block that dynamically identifies key body parts and models their spatial-temporal dependencies. In addition, inter-pathway dynamic fusion modules integrate complementary inter-modal information across pathways, facilitating higher-level semantic interpretation of actions. Extensive experiments on Kinetics Skeleton 400, NTU RGB+D 60, and NTU RGB+D 120 demonstrate consistent performance improvements, validating the effectiveness of dual-pathway spatial-temporal modeling for skeleton-based action recognition.

</details>


### [107] [When More Foundation Models Means Less: Diagnosing and Addressing Multi-View Fusion Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17490)
*Yibo Liu,Bowen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KAGES方法，将大规模编码器池下的多视图融合视为视图组合选择问题，通过基于核中心对齐的贪心选择策略，仅需少量预计算即可选出与任务对齐的紧凑视图子集，在多个识别基准上优于全融合和其他选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型中心将多视图融合变成了一个选择问题：面对大规模的异构编码器池，应该融合哪些视图、融合多少个？已有的融合方法通常假设融合更多编码器总是更好，但实际中下游性能与融合编码器数量呈非单调关系——后加入的视图可能冗余或与任务不对齐，导致准确率饱和甚至下降。因此需要一种方法在无需训练下游分类器的情况下，选择紧凑且任务对齐的视图子集。

Method: 形式化视图组合选择问题并提出KAGES（Kernel-Alignment Greedy Encoder Selector），一种标签感知的方法。核心思路是对冻结的编码器按其边际核中心对齐（centred kernel-target alignment）增益进行排序并贪心选择。整个过程在预训练下游分类器之前完成，每个候选编码器评估复杂度为O(n²)，与编码器维度无关。在单调性和正子模性比率条件下，该方法可提供条件化的(1-e^(-γ))前缀保证。

Result: 在五种识别场景下及低样本、更大池和全数据协议中，KAGES相比全融合在平均AULC上分别提升3.9、5.8和3.3个百分点，并优于DPP和设施位置选择方法。图像检索沿KAGES顺序表现出后期任务相关的饱和现象，峰后下降现象也在冻结大语言模型融合中得到复现。

Conclusion: 有效的大规模编码器池融合关键在于选择紧凑、任务对齐的视图子集，而非不加区分地融合更多编码器。KAGES为此提供了高效且理论上可保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation-model hubs turn multi-view fusion into a selection problem: from a large heterogeneous encoder pool, which views should be fused, and how many? We show that downstream performance is non-monotonic in the number of fused encoders; later views can be redundant or task-misaligned, causing accuracy to saturate or decline. We formalise this setting as view-set composition and propose KAGES (Kernel-Alignment Greedy Encoder Selector), a label-aware method that orders frozen encoders by their marginal gain in centred kernel-target alignment. KAGES requires no downstream classifier training during selection, evaluates each candidate in $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ time independent of encoder dimension, and admits a conditional $(1-e^{-γ})$ prefix-wise guarantee under monotonicity and a positive submodularity ratio. Across five recognition regimes and low-shot, larger-pool, and full-data protocols, KAGES improves average AULC over full fusion by 3.9, 5.8, and 3.3 points, respectively, and exceeds DPP and facility-location selection in average AULC. Image retrieval exhibits later, task-dependent saturation along the KAGES ordering, while peak-then-decline reproduces in frozen-LLM fusion. These results show that effective large-pool fusion depends on selecting a compact, task-aligned set of views rather than indiscriminately fusing more encoders.

</details>


### [108] [SE-MoLoRA: Shared-Expert LoRA Adapters for Domain-Specific Photographic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17514)
*Bishwash Khanal,Anlan Zhang,Sasu Tarkoma,Tommi Mikkonen,Abhishek Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SE-MoLoRA，一种模块化参数高效适配框架，用于领域特定的摄影评估。通过共享LoRA专家和按路由分配的专业适配器，将通用摄影知识与构图、光线、技术质量等专业判断相分离，在损失评估生成上显著优于单体LoRA，且使用更少活跃参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型虽然能流畅描述图像，但常常无法提供可操作的摄影批评，因为语义内容和美学判断纠缠在一起。现有方法需要训练多个完整模型或使用单一LoRA，缺乏对专业摄影评估的可控性和特异性。

Method: SE-MoLoRA框架包含：(1) 一个始终活跃的rank-64共享LoRA专家，捕获广义摄影词汇；(2) 针对构图、光线、技术质量三个领域的rank-32专业LoRA适配器，通过路由机制选择；(3) 轻量级查询路由器选择相关专家；(4) 正交正则化惩罚鼓励共享与专业表征解耦。训练数据通过对Reddit Photo Critique Dataset蒸馏获得领域标注的批评样本。

Result: 在保留的批评生成评估中，SE-MoLoRA将BERTScore-F1从0.2317提升至0.4215（对比单体LoRA），在84.6%的成对比较中被偏好，同时使用比单独专业模型更少的活跃参数。SVD消融研究表明共享-专业分解和正交正则化能减少专家重叠。

Conclusion: 模块化适配能提升多模态摄影批评的可控性和特异性，证明共享-专业分离的LoRA架构在领域特定视觉-语言任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models can describe images fluently, but they often fail to provide actionable photographic critique because semantic content and aesthetic judgment remain entangled. We propose SE-MoLoRA, a modular parameter-efficient adaptation framework for domain-specific photographic assessment. The method separates general photographic knowledge from specialist residual judgments using an always-active shared LoRA expert and routed adapters for composition, lighting, and technical quality. A lightweight query router selects the relevant specialist, enabling targeted critique without training separate full models. A rank-64 shared adapter captures broad photographic vocabulary, while rank-32 specialists learn domain-specific residuals with an orthogonal regularization penalty that encourages disentangled representations. Training data is obtained by distilling the Reddit Photo Critique Dataset into domain-labeled critique samples. On held-out critique generation, SE-MoLoRA improves BERTScore-F1 from 0.2317 to 0.4215 over monolithic LoRA and is preferred in 84.6\% of pairwise comparisons, while using fewer active parameters than separate specialist models. SVD-based ablation study shows that shared-specialist decomposition and orthogonal regularization reduce expert overlap. These results demonstrate that modular adaptation improves controllability and specificity in multimodal photographic critique.

</details>


### [109] [Looking Beyond the Scale: Do Surgical Skill Models Learn Transferable Representations Across Assessment Rubrics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17519)
