Abstract: Radiology report generation has matured almost entirely on 2D chest radiographs, where the default route to better reports is a larger backbone or a pre-training one on medical data. We revisit that assumption on 3D multi-sequence brain MRI, a volumetric multi-disease regime, and find that the model is not the lever. Zero-shot medical and radiology vision-language models transfer poorly to brain MRI, with chest radiograph specialists failing most conspicuously, and five backbones fine-tuned identically across three model families and an order of magnitude in scale differ only marginally. What determines the quality of the report is the information injected into the prompt. We delegate perception to upstream 3D segmentation and classification, serialize their outputs into a structured fact sentence, and prompt a LoRA-adapted vision-language model with it; we call this \textbf{PerFact}. In a controlled study that fixes the backbone, data split, target reports, and adaptation while varying only the injected grounding, perception-derived facts outperform retrieved prior reports, retrieval becomes redundant once facts are present, and end-to-end predicted facts remain effective without any ground-truth annotation at inference. The residual gap between predicted and oracle facts is explained by the granularity of the facts rather than by the generator. Closed-ended visual question answering comes at no measurable cost to report quality, though the grounding source has little effect on it. On 3D brain MRI, grounding information, not model choice, is the dominant controllable factor in report quality.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Instrument Motion: Recognizing Tissue Tension Toward Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17935)
*Marko Haralovi,Zhiqi Miao,Alexander Machiel Bont,Jiapan Guo,Frans van Workum,Estefania Talavera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出微创手术中组织张力识别这一新的视频理解任务，构建了首个专家标注的组织张力数据集SurgTension，并提出了基于稀疏点轨迹的轻量级框架TensionTRAC，在性能上与强预训练视频主干网络相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微创手术技能评估依赖人工专家评审，耗时、主观且难以规模化。虽然已有手术视频理解方法涵盖器械分割、手术阶段识别和动作识别，但缺乏对组织处理细节（组织张力）的显式捕捉，而组织张力是手术质量的关键指标。

Method: 提出TensionTRAC，一个轻量级的基于轨迹的框架，通过稀疏点轨迹建模组织张力。该方法使用紧凑的轨迹编码器，将组织张力识别建模为视频理解任务。

Result: TensionTRAC在SurgTension基准上取得了与强预训练视频主干网络相当的性能，同时保持轻量级特性，展示了基于轨迹编码在手术视频理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次定义并系统地研究了手术中组织张力识别任务，提供了标准化的基准数据集和有效的方法，为客观、可扩展的手术技能评估提供了新方向。

Abstract: Surgical performance assessment in minimally invasive surgery largely relies on manual expert review, making it time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale. While existing surgical video understanding methods address tasks such as instrument segmentation, surgical phase recognition, and action recognition, they do not explicitly capture fine-grained tissue handling, a key indicator of surgical quality. To address this gap, we introduce tissue tension recognition, a new clinically motivated video understanding task for laparoscopic and robot-assisted rectal cancer surgery. To support this task, we construct SurgTension, the first expert-annotated tissue tension dataset, providing a benchmark for objective tissue tension recognition. We further propose TensionTRAC, a lightweight trajectory-based framework that models tissue tension from sparse point trajectories. Using a compact trajectory encoder, TensionTRAC achieves competitive performance against strong pretrained video backbones.

</details>


### [142] [Cross-Domain Generalization in Machine Unlearning via Label-Conditioned Energy Magnitude Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17942)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Syed Bilal Ahsan,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究了在类别遗忘任务中，遗忘一个类别如何影响模型其余部分。使用标签条件能量模型（EBM），通过提升被遗忘类别图像-标签对的能量实现遗忘，并引入传播项将遗忘信号按相似度扩散到相似样本。在DomainNet和CIFAR-10上验证了遗忘效果及对非目标类别的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法大多将被遗忘的概念视为孤立事件，忽略了遗忘操作对模型其他部分的影响。作者希望在一个可观测的框架下（能量模型）量化遗忘一个类别后模型其余部分的变化，以理解遗忘的传播效应。

Method: 采用标签条件能量模型（EBM），每个类别有独立的能量分配。遗忘某个类别通过提高其图像-标签对的能量实现，训练目标包含：遗忘项（forget term）、对预训练模型的保留锚点（retain anchor）、全局边际（global margin）、以及防止能量无限增长的能量正则化项。此外提出传播项（propagation term），利用DINOv2特征相似度将遗忘信号加权传递到保留样本中，使与遗忘类别相似的样本也受到影响，而无关样本不受干扰。

Result: 在DomainNet子集（四个视觉域）上分别遗忘tiger、lion、scissors：遗忘sketch域中的类别同时能在real、clipart、painting域中被擦除，lion和scissors的遗忘误差达98%和99%，且遗忘效应传导到最相似类别。在CIFAR-10上关闭传播项逐类遗忘十个类别：遗忘完全（100%），其余九个类别平均保留98.5%的遗忘前准确率。

Conclusion: 基于标签条件EBM的遗忘方法可以高效完成类别遗忘，遗忘效果可跨域传播，同时将遗忘对非目标类别的影响最小化（约1.5%的准确率损失），验证了传播机制在控制遗忘范围中的有效性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific data from a trained model. However, most methods treat the forgotten concept as isolated. In this paper, we study what happens to the rest of the model when a class is forgotten, using a label-conditioned energy-based model (EBM) that assigns per-class energies, making the effect directly observable. We forget a class by raising the energy of its image-label pairs, training with a forget term, a retain anchor to the pretrained model, a global margin, and an energy regularizer that stops the energy magnitudes from growing without limit. A propagation term applies the same forget signal to retain samples, weighted by each sample's DINOv2 similarity to the forget class, so forgetting reaches images that resemble it and leaves the rest untouched. We evaluate on two benchmark datasets: 1) On a subset of DomainNet across four visual domains, we forget tiger, lion, and scissors one at a time. Forgetting a class in the sketch domain also erases it from real, clipart, and painting, with forgetting error reaching 98% and 99% for lion and scissors, and the effect carrying over to the most similar class. 2) On CIFAR-10, we turn off the propagation term and forget each of the ten classes on its own. Forgetting is complete (100%), while the other nine classes retain 98.5% of their pre-unlearning accuracy on average.

</details>


### [143] [SFMformer: A Spatial-Frequency Modulation Transformer for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17966)
*Chih-Hsiang Yang,Chia-Min Lin,Ching-Yu Tsai,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMformer，通过将稀疏注意力网络的改进目标拆分为"选择质量"和"聚合质量"两个可分离的环节，在渐进式聚焦注意力的输入端配以双分支空间增强、输出端配以小波域调制，实现两者的协同增益（联合增益超过单项增益之和），在五个基准、三个放大因子上的30个PSNR/SSIM指标中28项排名第一，且参数低于100万，并在树莓派5上验证了实际部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力机制已成为轻量级图像超分最有效的Transformer核心，但现有工作往往将其视为一个整体来改进。作者观察到稀疏层与稠密层不同：top-k选择阶段丢弃的token无法在下游恢复，因此网络改进目标应区分为"选择质量"（决定哪些token存活）与"聚合质量"（决定如何利用被选中的token），二者是可分离的优化目标。

Method: SFMformer由两部分模块构成：一是放在渐进式聚焦注意力（progressive focused attention）输入端的双分支空间增强模块，负责提升token选择质量；二是放在注意力输出端的小波域调制模块，负责提升特征聚合质量。两个模块分别作用在注意力的前后，对应"选择质量"与"聚合质量"两个可分离目标。此外，作者提出频谱调制每个block仅启用一次（而非每层一次），将成本降至约六分之一。

Result: 在15个基准尺度对上单独和联合测量两个模块，发现联合增益并非简单的加性叠加：9个尺度对上联合增益超过单独增益之和；弱模块的单独贡献能预测这种差异的符号（r=-0.72），即当两个模块缓解不同约束时相互增益，缓解同一约束时效果重叠。SFMformer在5个基准、3个放大因子的30个PSNR/SSIM条目中28项排名第一，所有尺度参数均低于100万，并在树莓派5上完成了实际部署验证。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力网络的改进应当将选择质量和聚合质量视为两个独立且可叠加的目标，二者仅在缓解不同约束时产生正协同。SFMformer的设计验证了这一观点，在保持轻量（低于百万参数）的同时取得领先性能，且通过低算力平台部署证明了其实际可用性。作者还诚实地报告了配对不生效的案例，为后续研究提供了边界条件。

Abstract: Sparse attention mechanisms, which score all token pairs but propagate only the strongest, now underpin the most efficient Transformers for lightweight image super-resolution. This paper observes that sparsification changes what it means to improve such a network. A dense attention layer has one place where representation quality matters: the aggregation of attended features. A sparse layer has two, because the top-k operator first decides which tokens survive and only then decides what to do with them, and a token discarded at the selection stage cannot be recovered downstream. Selection quality and aggregation quality are therefore separable targets, addressed by modules placed before and after the attention respectively. We test this by pairing a dual-branch spatial enhancement on the input of a progressive focused attention with a wavelet-domain modulation on its output, forming SFMformer. Measuring each module alone and jointly over all fifteen benchmark-scale pairs, we find their gains are not additive: the joint gain exceeds the sum of the individual gains on nine pairs, and the sign of the discrepancy is predicted by how much the weaker module contributes on its own (r = -0.72), so the two compound when they relieve different constraints and overlap when they relieve the same one. Enabling spectral modulation once per block rather than once per layer retains the effect at roughly one-sixth of its cost, keeping the model below one million parameters at every scale. SFMformer ranks first on 28 of 30 PSNR/SSIM entries across five benchmarks and three upscaling factors. We report the cases where the pairing does not help, and deploy the model on a Raspberry Pi 5 to confirm the design is practical under tight resource budgets.

</details>


### [144] [LinCa: Accelerating Diffusion Models via Learnable Decomposed Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17973)
*Jinshan Liu,Haoran Qin,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Jiahui Hu,Zhengan Yan,Yukun Xie,Kerui Shen,Jinkui Ren,Yuqi Lin,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinCa是一种基于可学习可逆网络的特征缓存框架，通过将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性属性的子组件，并对各组件采用差异化预测顺序，实现了扩散模型的高加速比采样。仅需不到0.2%的额外参数，在FLUX、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo上以5-7倍加速保持近无损质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像和视频生成中取得显著成功，但迭代采样的高计算成本是实际部署的关键瓶颈。特征缓存虽已成为有前景的加速范式，但现有无训练方法采用统一的预测策略，无法适应异构的特征动态，在高加速比下造成严重的质量退化。

Method: LinCa提出Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct统一流水线：通过轻量可逆网络将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性属性的子组件，对各组件匹配差异化预测顺序；严格的可逆性保证无损地重建回原始特征空间；为不同模型和时间步片段训练独立的预测器以适应异构特征动态。

Result: 在FLUX、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo上的实验表明，LinCa仅增加不到0.2%的额外参数，显著优于现有方法，并在5-7倍加速下保持近无损质量。

Conclusion: LinCa通过可学习可逆网络实现特征分解与差异化预测，有效解决了现有特征缓存方法在加速时无法适配异构特征动态的问题，在极低参数开销下实现了高加速比与近无损质量的兼顾。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image and video generation, yet the high computational cost of iterative sampling remains a critical bottleneck for practical deployment. Feature caching has emerged as a promising acceleration paradigm by reusing or predicting intermediate features across timesteps. However, existing training-free methods apply uniform prediction strategies that cannot adapt to the heterogeneous feature dynamics, causing significant quality degradation under high acceleration ratios. We propose LinCa, a feature caching framework based on learnable invertible networks. LinCa decomposes cached features into sub-components with distinct continuity properties via a lightweight invertible network and applies differentiated prediction orders matched to each component. The strict invertibility guarantees lossless reconstruction back to the original feature space, forming a unified Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct pipeline. By training separate predictors for different models and timestep segments, LinCa adapts to heterogeneous feature dynamics. Experiments on FLUX, Qwen-Image, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LinCa, with less than 0.2% additional parameters, significantly outperforms existing methods and maintains near-lossless quality at 5-7x speedup. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/LinCa

</details>


### [145] [Dual Co-Train: Cross-Dataset Ultrasound Tongue Segmentation Under Extreme Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17983)
*Alisher Myrgyyassov,Zhen Song,Bruce Xiao Wang,Yu Sun,Min Ney Wong,Yihao Zhou,Yongping Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个源无关域自适应框架，用于超声舌体分割任务。该方法基于轻量级UltraUNet骨干，仅用5张标注源图像预训练后，在无标注目标域上通过迭代精炼伪标签、轮廓质量控制模块和分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成数据，实现闭环自适应。在8个数据集、12个源-目标迁移对上优于基线（包括有监督方法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声舌体分割在跨数据集域偏移下仍具挑战性，存在标注稀缺、探头差异和采集噪声等问题导致模型泛化能力下降。现有方法依赖源域数据或大量标注，难以在无源数据访问且目标域无标注的实际场景中应用。

Method: 1) 轻量级UltraUNet骨干网络；2) 从仅5张标注源图像预训练得到欠拟合源模型；3) 在完全无标注目标域上迭代精炼伪标签；4) 基于轮廓的质量控制模块过滤不可靠掩码；5) 分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成图像-掩码对；6) 学生模型在干净伪标签、带一致性正则化的噪声伪标签及合成样本的混合数据上进行闭环训练。

Result: 在8个超声舌体成像数据集的12个源-目标迁移对上评估，并进行了源数据规模缩放实验和消融研究。在所有比较中，所提框架在分割重叠率和轮廓精度上均优于基线方法，包括有监督基线。

Conclusion: 任务特定的伪标签精炼和合成目标风格增强能显著提升超声舌体成像的源无关自适应性能，表明在源数据不可访问且目标域无标注场景下，该框架是有效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Ultrasound tongue contour segmentation remains challenging under cross-dataset domain shift, where limited annotations, probe variability, and acquisition noise often degrade model generalization. We present a source-free domain adaptation framework for robust ultrasound tongue segmentation built on a lightweight UltraUNet backbone. Starting from a checkpoint pretrained on only five labeled source images, simulating an underfitted constrained source model, the proposed method adapts to a fully-unlabeled target domain by iteratively refining pseudo-labels, filtering unreliable masks with a contour-based quality-control module, and generating target-style synthetic image-mask pairs through a segmentation-guided conditional GAN. The student model is then trained on a mixture of clean pseudo-labeled target images, noisy pseudo-labels with consistency regularization, and synthetic samples, enabling closed-loop adaptation without access to source data. We evaluate the method on 12 source-target transfer pairs across eight ultrasound tongue imaging datasets, and conduct source-size scaling experiments and ablation studies. Across all comparisons, the proposed framework improves segmentation overlap and contour accuracy over the baselines, including supervised ones. These results suggest that task-specific pseudo-label refinement and synthetic target-style augmentation can substantially improve source-free adaptation for ultrasound tongue imaging.

</details>


### [146] [GS-Voxel: Fitting-Free Structured Latents for Large-Scale 3DGS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17988)
*Ming Qian,Zijian Wang,Minchao Sun,Jincheng Xiong,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Chi Wang,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GS-Voxel，一种免拟合的结构化隐空间框架，将无序且稀疏的预优化3DGS航空重建确定性转换为稀疏活性体素，并通过因子化VAE编码为可扩展的3D隐变量，结合流模型实现大规模航拍3D高斯场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可扩展潜在3D生成器通常工作在结构化张量上，而预优化的3D高斯散列（3DGS）重建是无序、空间不规则且基本元数量差异极大的，难以直接作为结构化生成模型的输入，因此需要一种能将预优化3DGS重建转换为适合生成模型的统一结构化表示的方法。

Method: GS-Voxel框架包含三个关键部分：(1) 确定性转换模块，将兼容的预优化3DGS重建转为稀疏活性体素，无需逐场景优化，保留次体素位置和渲染属性；(2) GS专用因子化VAE，分别编码体素几何和局部高斯属性为稀疏3D隐变量，其容量随占用体素数增长而非受固定全局基本元数量限制；(3) 在GS-Voxel隐空间中训练图像条件流模型（flow models）用于生成航拍3DGS场景，并通过重叠感知的分块（tiled）推理实现超出单个训练裁剪范围的大面积场景合成。

Result: GS-Voxel成功为预优化的航拍3DGS重建提供了结构化隐表示，隐容量随占用体素数增长而扩展，并支持基于卫星图的超单裁剪范围的大面积场景生成。

Conclusion: GS-Voxel通过免拟合的结构化隐空间设计，弥合了无序3DGS重建与可扩展生成模型之间的鸿沟，为大面积航拍3D场景生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Many scalable latent 3D generators operate on structured tensors, whereas pre-optimized 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions are unordered, spatially irregular, and vary widely in primitive count. We present GS-Voxel, a fitting-free structured latent framework, and evaluate it for large-scale aerial 3D Gaussian scene generation. GS-Voxel deterministically converts a compatible pre-optimized 3DGS reconstruction into sparse active voxels without additional per-scene optimization, retaining the sub-voxel positions and rendering attributes of the selected primitives. A GS-specific factorized VAE then separately encodes voxel geometry and local Gaussian attributes into sparse 3D latents whose size grows with the number of occupied voxels rather than being limited by a fixed scene-wide primitive count. We train image-conditioned flow models in the GS-Voxel latent space to generate aerial 3DGS scenes. A key application enabled by GS-Voxel is large-area scene generation: overlap-aware tiled inference extends synthesis beyond a single training crop conditioned on satellite-view images. Our results show that GS-Voxel provides structured latents for pre-optimized aerial 3DGS reconstructions, with latent capacity that grows with the number of occupied voxels.

</details>


### [147] [AViTS: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Token Selection for Efficient Dynamic-Resolution Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17995)
*Haoran Qin,Zhengan Yan,Shikang Zheng,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Yuqi Lin,Chang Zou,JinShan Liu,Peiliang Cai,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AViTS是一种用于动态分辨率扩散Transformer（DiTs）的自适应时空Token选择框架，通过融合潜在文本注意力（空间重要性）与跨扩散时间步的Token级特征变化（时间重要性），实现选择性上采样，优先细化关键Token而延迟次要Token，从而减少高分辨率冗余计算并改善质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiTs）由于迭代采样成本高昂；动态分辨率采样虽能降低早期成本，但均匀上采样所有潜在Token会产生冗余计算并损害细节一致性。现有的部分上采样策略依赖局部潜在结构线索或单步统计量，难以联合捕捉Token-文本语义相关性及跨扩散步骤的Token级表示动态。

Method: AViTS通过两个维度建模Token重要性：(1) 空间重要性——利用潜在文本注意力衡量Token与文本语义的相关性；(2) 时间重要性——度量Token特征在扩散时间步间的变化。两者融合后形成时空重要性感知的选择性上采样策略：为关键Token优先执行分辨率细化，延迟次要Token的上采样。

Result: 在FLUX上实现最高6.34倍加速，在Qwen-Image-Edit和FLUX.1-Kontext-dev上实现近9倍FLOPs缩减；该方法与蒸馏、量化和特征缓存正交，结合蒸馏模型可达到14.76倍加速。

Conclusion: AViTS证明了结合空间语义相关性与时间动态变化的自适应Token选择能有效改善动态分辨率DiTs的质量-效率平衡，且方法与现有加速技术（蒸馏、量化、缓存）高度互补。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve high-quality generation but are costly due to iterative sampling. Dynamic-resolution sampling reduces early-stage cost by denoising at low resolution; however, uniformly upsampling all latent tokens at resolution transitions incurs redundant computation and may degrade fine-detail consistency. Existing partial upsampling strategies typically rely on local latent structure cues or single-step statistics, making it difficult to jointly capture token-text semantic relevance and token-wise representation dynamics across diffusion steps. We propose AViTS, an adaptive spatiotemporal token selection framework for dynamic-resolution DiTs. AViTS models spatial importance via latent-text attention and temporal importance via token-level feature variation across diffusion timesteps, and fuses them to enable spatiotemporal importance-aware selective upsampling: it prioritizes resolution refinement for critical tokens while deferring less important ones, thereby reducing redundant high-resolution computation and improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. AViTS achieves up to 6.34x on FLUX and nearly 9x FLOPs reduction on Qwen-Image-Edit and FLUX.1-Kontext-dev, orthogonal to distillation, quantization, and feature caching, and reaching 14.76x with distilled models. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/AViTS

</details>


### [148] [Memory Tree Guided Key Frame Querying for Efficient 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18009)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Fu-Chen Chen,Li-Wu Tsao,Cheng-Han Lee,Che-Chun Su,Lu Xia,Ronghui Peng,Jenq-Neng Hwang,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemTree3D，一种基于记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式，用于具身场景下的高效3D问答。通过利用相机6自由度位姿实时在线构建紧凑且可复用的3D场景表示，使大语言模型无需重新处理整个视频流即可高效查询和检索与问题相关的关键帧。在OpenEQA基准上，GPT-4o的LLM-Match提升了17.4%，LLaVA-OneVision-7B提升了5.8%，优于现有视觉搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身场景下VLM推理受限于有限的计算和内存资源，现有方法采用视觉搜索关键帧检索方法，但需要在每个用户查询中对数千个视频帧进行视觉搜索，效率低下。

Method: 提出记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式。其核心是MemTree3D，一种紧凑且可复用的3D场景表示：1）利用相机6-DoF位姿支持实时在线构建；2）捕获多级3D场景信息；3）使LLM能够通过评分机制高效查询和检索问题相关的关键帧，无需重新处理整个视频流。

Result: 在OpenEQA基准上，该方法使GPT-4o的LLM-Match提升17.4%，LLaVA-OneVision-7B提升5.8%，性能优于现有视觉搜索方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MemTree3D通过构建紧凑可复用的3D场景记忆树，无需逐帧视觉搜索即可高效完成关键帧选择，显著提升具身场景3D问答的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Answering questions accurately and efficiently in embodied scenarios presents significant challenges due to limited computational and memory resources for Vision Language Model (VLM) inference. Existing methods adopt visual search key frame retrieval method to select critical question-related key frames for VLM input. However, visual search methods are inefficient because they require visual search among thousands of video frames for each individual user query. In this work, we propose a memory tree guided key frame selection paradigm for efficient 3D question answering in embodied scenarios. Our method leverages a compact and reusable 3D scene representation, termed MemTree3D, which supports real-time online construction leveraging camera 6-DoF poses. MemTree3D captures multi-level 3D scene information, enabling a Large Language Model to efficiently query and retrieve question-relevant key frames through our scoring-based frame selection without reprocessing the entire video stream. On OpenEQA, our method improves the LLM-Match of GPT-4o by 17.4%, LLaVA-OneVision-7B by 5.8%, outperforms existing visual search methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/MemTree3D

</details>


### [149] [Automated ACL Footprint Identification Using 3D Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18012)
*Ruida Cheng,Ali Uneri,Gabriel Gibson,Frances T. Sheehan,Barry Boden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索了两种3D深度学习模型用于直接从3D磁共振图像中识别前交叉韧带（ACL）股骨足迹中心，以解决ACL重建术中股骨隧道错位导致的失败问题。基于4970个膝关节数据集，图像增强模型平均误差2.1mm优于基于网格的图卷积模型（2.8mm），表明3D深度学习为ACL足迹定位提供了可行的临床方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACL重建失败的主要原因之一是股骨隧道错位，而准确的ACL股骨足迹识别对于精确隧道放置、恢复膝关节自然力学及预防移植物失败至关重要。现有AI研究主要集中于ACL分割和撕裂分类，对足迹中心识别缺乏深入研究，因此需要探索基于深度学习的ACL足迹定位方法。

Method: 开发了两种3D深度学习架构：(1) 基于3D图卷积神经网络的几何模型，应用于3D股骨网格； (2) 基于3D MR图像的landmark增强识别模型。使用公开数据库中4883个右膝和3087个左膝图像集，80%用于模型训练，20%用于测试。

Result: 两种模型均表现优异，但基于图像的方法优于基于模型的方法（平均误差2.1mm vs 2.8mm）。

Conclusion: 3D深度学习为ACL足迹定位提供了可行的临床方法，有望提高ACL重建足迹的精确性。

Abstract: One of the most common reasons for anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) reconstruction failure is femoral tunnel malpositioning (ACL footprint center and tunnel orientation). Such failures may lead to the development of meniscal pathology and osteoarthritis. Accurate ACL femoral footprint identification is therefore essential for precise tunnel placement, restoration of the native knee joint mechanics, post-surgical knee joint health and prevention of graft failure. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) bring new opportunities to improve image-guided orthopedic surgery. However, at present, existing AI research focuses primarily on ACL segmentation and rupture classification based on pre- and post-operative magnetic resonance (MR) images. Identification of the ACL footprint center using deep learning methods has not been thoroughly researched. Thus, the purpose of this study is to explore 3D deep learning models for ACL femoral footprint identification directly from 3D MR images. Two comprehensive 3D deep learning architectures were developed: a 3D graph convolutional neural network-based geometric model applied to 3D femoral meshes; and a 3D landmark-enhanced identification model based on 3D MR images. A total of 4883 right and 3087 left knee image sets were used from a publicly available database. Eighty percent (80%) were applied to model generation, and twenty percent (20%) were preserved for model testing. Both models achieved excellent performance; however, the image-based method outperformed the model-based method (average error of 2.1mm vs 2.8 mm). Thus, 3D deep learning provides a feasible clinical approach for ACL footprint localization and has the potential to improve ACL reconstruction footprint accuracy.

</details>


### [150] [Initialization-Free Bundle Adjustment Revisited: A Controlled Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18028)
*Simon Weber,Mateo de Mayo,Je Hyeong Hong,Carl Olsson,Daniel Cremers,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的评估框架（C++实现的OSE公式与Blender数据集生成器）对免初始化BA进行实验研究，揭示了一个被忽视的"优化-重建差距"：低OSE目标值不能保证有效的度量重建，提出初始先验、地标观测密度和度量升级稳定性是决定重建成功的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免初始化BA方法（基于OSE与VarPro）表现出良好的优化行为，但已有评估仅关注优化成功与否，未回答低OSE目标值是否能产生有效的度量3D重建这一关键问题。

Method: 构建统一评估框架：采用C++实现现有OSE公式，结合基于Blender的数据集生成器以提供精确的真值、受控的相机配置和观测密度；在此基础上对免初始化BA进行系统的实验比较与分析。

Result: 实验发现存在优化-重建差距：OSE值相近的射影解在度量升级后可能导致差异显著的欧氏重建；初始先验、地标观测密度和度量升级稳定性是决定重建成功的关键因素，表明免初始化BA的主要挑战不仅是最小化OSE目标，还要获得能可靠度量升级的射影重建。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基准、实现和分析为免初始化BA的未来研究建立了更强的实验基础，将该问题定位为计算机视觉中尚未充分探索的研究方向。

Abstract: Initialization-free bundle adjustment (InitFree BA) aims to recover camera poses and scene structure directly from image observations, avoiding the geometric initialization stages of conventional structure-from-motion pipelines. Recent methods based on Object-Space Error (OSE) formulations and Variable Projection (VarPro) show encouraging optimization behavior from random camera configurations. However, existing evaluations primarily measure optimization success, leaving unclear whether a low OSE objective yields a valid metric 3D reconstruction. We revisit InitFree BA experimentally through a unified evaluation framework combining a C++ implementation of existing OSE formulations with a Blender-based dataset generator providing exact ground truth and controlled camera configurations and observation densities. Our experiments reveal a previously overlooked optimization--reconstruction gap: projective solutions with similarly low OSE values can lead to substantially different Euclidean reconstructions after metric upgrade. We identify initialization priors, landmark observation density, and metric-upgrade stability as key factors governing reconstruction success. Overall, our results suggest that the main challenge of InitFree BA is not merely minimizing OSE objectives, but obtaining projective reconstructions that admit reliable metric upgrade. We believe that the proposed benchmark, implementation, and analysis establish stronger experimental foundations for future research on initialization-free bundle adjustment, a problem largely unexplored within the computer vision community. Project page is available at https://github.com/simonwebertum/InitFreeBA.git.

</details>


### [151] [Deep Academic Survey: Stateful Agentic Closed-Loop Paradigm for Academic Survey Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18034)
*Zhikai Xu,Zhucun Xue,Teng Hu,Yabiao Wang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAS，一个基于状态（stateful）的智能代理（agentic）框架，用于生成面向出版的学术综述。其核心思想是将可复用的论文分析与特定主题的综述构建相分离，通过显式的文献、组织、写作和定稿状态机，结合候选驱动的分类规划、反向论文到章节路由以及层级化主张与引用规划，实现综述生成。同时引入DAS-2M元数据湖（约两百万篇论文）和DAS-Bench/DAS-Eval基准评估体系。实验显示DAS在四个维度均取得最高平均分（4.34分），显著优于最强竞争者（4.03分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术综述在组织快速增长的科学文献中至关重要，但现有Deep Research和自动综述生成系统通常只覆盖流程的某个环节，缺乏共享、可修订的状态来协调论文理解、文献组织、证据驱动的撰写和手稿验证，导致各环节割裂、难以可靠地生成符合出版质量的综述。

Method: DAS采用状态化智能代理框架：1) 将可复用的论文分析与特定主题的综述构建解耦；2) 基于DAS-2M动态元数据湖（约两百万篇论文的综述化表征）；3) 代理通过显式维护文献（literature）、组织（organization）、写作（writing）和定稿（finalization）四种状态；4) 采用候选驱动的分类规划、反向论文到章节路由、层级化主张与引用规划；5) 语义审查仅重新激活受影响的写作状态进行修复和重新评估，形成范围明确的闭环与确定性验证。评估采用DAS-Bench（30主题基准）和DAS-Eval（16条标准，覆盖引文质量、分类学综合、层级话语和手稿组装可靠性四个维度）。

Result: 在所有30个主题上，DAS在全部四个维度取得最高平均分，综合得分4.34，优于最强竞争者Autosurvey的4.03；在匹配的21个CS主题子集上保持相同排序。盲审专家评估中，DAS在27/30个主题上优于Naive RAG，在19/21个CS主题上优于AutoSurvey。

Conclusion: DAS通过引入可修订的共享状态和范围化闭环修复机制，有效协调了综述生成全流程，在学术综述生成的引文质量、分类综合、层级话语和组装可靠性等关键维度上均超越现有方法，证明状态化代理框架对面向出版的学术综述生成具有显著优势。

Abstract: Academic surveys play a central role in organizing rapidly expanding scholarly literature, yet their construction requires extensive paper analysis, coherent knowledge organization, fine-grained citation support, and reliable manuscript assembly. Existing Deep Research and automated survey generation systems address parts of this process, but typically do not coordinate paper understanding, literature organization, evidence-grounded drafting, and manuscript validation through a shared, revisable state. We introduce DAS, a stateful agentic framework for generating publication-oriented academic surveys. Its key idea is to separate reusable paper analysis from topic-specific manuscript construction. DAS builds on DAS-2M, a dynamically updated metadata lake containing survey-oriented representations of approximately two million papers. Its agents maintain explicit literature, organization, writing, and finalization states through candidate-grounded taxonomy planning, reverse paper-to-section routing, and hierarchical claim and citation planning. Semantic review reactivates only the affected writing states for repair and reevaluation, forming a scoped closed loop with deterministic validation. We further introduce DAS-Bench, a 30-topic benchmark, together with DAS-Eval, which assesses scholarly citation quality, taxonomic synthesis, hierarchical discourse, and manuscript assembly reliability through 16 criteria. Among systems evaluated on all 30 topics, DAS achieves the highest average in all four dimensions, with an overall score of 4.34 compared with 4.03 for the strongest competitor, and the same ordering is preserved on the matched 21-topic CS subset. Blinded expert evaluation further prefers DAS to Naive RAG on 27 of 30 topics and to AutoSurvey on 19 of 21 shared CS topics. The project page is available at https://zhikaixu24.github.io/projects/DAS/.

</details>


### [152] [Plug-and-Play Traffic Element Awareness for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18035)
*Zongzheng Zhang,Jijun Wang,Saining Zhang,Shuo Wang,Yiru Wang,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Yuwen Heng,Hao Sun,Anqing Jiang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了交通元素（交通灯、路标等）感知对端到端自动驾驶性能的影响。作者通过为多个公开驾驶数据集补充全面的交通元素标注，构建了统一研究基础设施，并提出一种最小化、通用的即插即用集成设计，将交通元素信号嵌入现有端到端驾驶管线。该设计在感知-预测-规划、VLA、回归式规划器、扩散式策略及轨迹评分等多类现代范式和多个数据集上均一致提升驾驶性能，并在挑战性的NAVSIM-v2基准上刷新了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶研究主要关注动态交通参与者（车辆、行人），而交通灯和路标等交通元素对驾驶决策同样关键，却缺乏系统性量化研究。主要挑战在于公开数据集缺少结构化的交通元素标注，且现代驾驶系统架构和训练范式差异大，难以统一评估。

Method: 作者构建统一研究基础设施，为多个公开数据集补充全面的交通元素标注；采用最小且通用的集成设计，以即插即拔方式将交通元素信号注入现有管线，仅需极少的架构修改；在感知-预测-规划、VLA、回归规划器、扩散策略和轨迹评分等五类主流范式中进行评估。

Result: 在所有测试范式和数据集（nuScenes、NAVSIM-v1/v2、Bench2Drive）上，该简单集成一致提升了驾驶性能；在NAVSIM-v2基准上显著改进现有最先进架构和数据管线，创造新SOTA。

Conclusion: 交通元素感知为端到端驾驶系统提供了鲁棒且可泛化的信号，证明了交通元素的显式注入是一种通用有效的改进方向，填补了该领域系统量化研究的空白。

Abstract: Traffic elements such as traffic lights and road signs play a fundamental role in human driving decisions and should naturally influence end-to-end driving performance. However, existing end-to-end driving research predominantly focuses on dynamic road participants (e.g., vehicles and pedestrians), while the role of traffic elements remains largely unexplored. The community still lacks a systematic study quantifying their impact, largely because public datasets rarely provide structured traffic-element annotations and modern driving systems vary widely in architecture and training paradigm. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of traffic element awareness for end-to-end autonomous driving. We construct a unified research infrastructure by augmenting multiple public driving datasets with comprehensive traffic-element annotations. To support diverse model families, we adopt a minimal and universal integration design that incorporates traffic-element signals into existing pipelines in a plug-and-play manner with negligible architectural modification. We evaluate this design across modern paradigms, including perception-prediction-planning pipelines, vision-language-action models (VLA), regression-based planners, diffusion-based policies, and trajectory-scoring frameworks, on nuScenes, NAVSIM-v1, NAVSIM-v2, and Bench2Drive. Across all paradigms and datasets, this simple integration consistently improves driving performance, demonstrating that traffic element awareness provides a robust and generalizable signal for end-to-end driving systems. Notably, on the challenging NAVSIM-v2 benchmark, our approach significantly improves state-of-the-art architectures and data pipelines, establishing a new state of the art.

</details>


### [153] [EDITBRIDGE: Towards Faithful and Efficient Ultra-High-Resolution Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18063)
*Jiayi Song,Shijie Huang,Fangtai Wu,Yubo Huang,Zhenxiong Tan,Songhua Liu,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditBridge 提出了一种扩散桥接框架，通过从低分辨率编辑结果到高分辨率版本的结构化数据到数据转换，实现高效超高分辨率图像编辑，支持最高4K分辨率，且比现有方法提升3.6-8.4倍速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像编辑在专业工作流中需求日益增长，但现有基于扩散的模型由于二次注意力复杂度和巨大内存需求，被限制在1K以下分辨率。常见的两阶段方案（低分辨率编辑+独立超分辨率）存在信息发散（幻觉细节与原始高分辨率源矛盾）和纹理退化（过度平滑或过度锐化伪影）两大关键问题。

Method: EditBridge采用扩散桥接框架，将细化过程建模为从低分辨率编辑结果到高分辨率对应物的结构化数据到数据转换，并显式以原始高分辨率源为条件以保留真实细节。为高效融合高分辨率源引导，引入先验引导的块级稀疏注意力机制，利用第一阶段编辑的语义对应关系，将跨图像交互限制在空间对齐区域，显著降低计算开销。

Result: 实验表明EditBridge在高达4K分辨率下实现了高保真编辑和优越的感知质量，在2K分辨率下提供3.6-8.4倍加速，并能在61秒内完成实际4K编辑任务。

Conclusion: EditBridge通过扩散桥接与稀疏注意力机制有效解决了高分辨率编辑中的信息发散和纹理退化问题，在效率和保真度之间取得了显著平衡，为超高分辨率图像编辑提供了实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: High-resolution image editing is increasingly demanded in professional workflows, yet existing diffusion-based models remain constrained to resolutions below 1K due to quadratic attention complexity and prohibitive memory requirements. A prevalent workaround employs a two-stage pipeline: editing at low resolution followed by independent super-resolution. However, this approach suffers from two critical issues: information divergence, where hallucinated details contradict the original high-resolution (HR) source, and texture degradation, manifesting as over-smoothed or over-sharpened artifacts. We propose EditBridge, a diffusion bridge framework for efficient ultra high-resolution editing. Unlike conventional diffusion that regenerates from noise, we formulate refinement as structured data-to-data translation from the low-resolution (LR) edited result to its HR counterpart, explicitly conditioned on the original HR source to preserve authentic details. To efficiently incorporate HR source guidance, we introduce a prior-guided block-wise sparse attention mechanism that exploits semantic correspondence from first-stage editing to constrain cross-image interactions to spatially aligned regions, significantly reducing computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EditBridge achieves high-fidelity editing with superior perceptual quality at resolutions up to 4K, delivering 3.6--8.4$\times$ speedup at 2K and enabling practical 4K editing in 61 seconds.

</details>


### [154] [From Corpora to Co-Evolving Capabilities: Capability-Centric Data Design for Generalist Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18076)
*Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Taihang Hu,Jun Zheng,Qing Jin,Qinye Zhou,Zhengtao Wu,Yongchao Du,Zuan Gao,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Yuhang Yu,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能力驱动的数据基础设施（capability-driven data infrastructure），将各任务专属的数据构建与能力对齐的课程调度相结合，通过三个互操作的数据引擎（文本-图像对齐、图像间变换、图像-知识关联）和字幕专家协同构建多粒度监督数据，并以多阶段课程联合演化任务组成、视觉概念分布、数据质量和图像分辨率，最终训练出3B和6B两种规模的多模态扩散模型，在CPI-Bench及多种T2I和编辑场景中展现出广泛的视觉覆盖与跨能力迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像生成流水线通常是针对单一任务数据集进行孤立优化，缺乏对不同生成能力之间依赖关系的系统性组织。核心挑战不仅在于如何策展各任务专属语料，更在于如何根据生成能力的依赖结构来组织异构监督信号。

Method: 提出一个能力驱动的数据基础设施，包含三个专用但互操作的数据引擎——分别构建文本-图像对齐、图像间变换、图像-知识关联的互补关系型监督；同时引入字幕专家实现T2I与编辑监督在跨任务和跨粒度上的对齐。采用多阶段课程，按照能力习得的依赖顺序联合演化任务组成、视觉概念分布、数据质量与图像分辨率；通过能力感知评估（目标检索、专家构建、缺口感知重采样）闭环反馈。大规模下构建了440M图像T2I语料、120M编辑对和27M+图像-实体对，并据此从零训练3B和6B两个规模的多模态扩散模型。

Result: 在CPI-Bench上进行了定量评估，并在多样的文本-图像生成和编辑场景中完成了定性评估。实验结果表明该框架具备广泛的视觉覆盖、多样的渲染能力以及跨生成能力的有效迁移。

Conclusion: 能力驱动的数据基础设施能够有效组织异构监督信号，依据能力依赖关系安排课程训练，从而在大规模图像生成和编辑任务中实现卓越的性能表现与跨能力迁移。

Abstract: Large-scale image generation has benefited from advances in data scale, quality, rebalancing, and recaptioning, yet conventional pipelines typically optimize task-specific datasets in isolation. A central challenge is not only how to curate each task-specific corpus, but also how to organize heterogeneous supervision according to the dependencies among generative capabilities. We present a \textbf{capability-driven data infrastructure} that couples capability-specific supervision construction with capability-aligned curriculum scheduling. Its three specialized yet interoperable data engines build complementary relational supervision for text-image grounding, inter-image transformation, and image-knowledge association, while caption experts align T2I and editing supervision across tasks and granularities. A multi-stage curriculum jointly evolves task composition, visual-concept distribution, data quality, and image resolution along the dependency order of capability acquisition, with capability-aware evaluation closing the loop through targeted retrieval, expert construction, and gap-aware resampling. At scale, the framework curates a 440M-image T2I corpus, 120M editing pairs, and over 27M image-entity pairs. With this infrastructure, we train multimodal diffusion models at two scales from scratch, with 3B and 6B sizes respectively. We conduct quantitative evaluation on CPI-Bench, along with qualitative evaluations across diverse text-to-image and editing scenarios. Experimental results present broad visual coverage, versatile rendering, and effective transfer across generative capabilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Quantum-Safe Web Service Architecture Using Time-Based One-Time Passwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16961)
*Abel C. H. Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种量子安全的Web服务架构，通过后量子密码（PQC）实现TLS/HTTPS传输安全，并使用HMAC驱动的TOTP进行Web服务安全管理。实验评估了SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256和SM3四种哈希算法在不同硬件条件下的计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性密码（OTP）已被广泛应用于多因素认证，但在自动化连接和系统间互操作的场景中，TOTP需要被用于建立安全连接和访问Web服务。随着量子计算的发展，现有的密码学体系面临威胁，因此需要探索量子安全的Web服务架构。

Method: 采用基于后量子密码（PQC）的TLS和HTTPS实现传输安全管理，通过构建HMAC驱动的TOTP实现Web服务安全管理。在实验环境中，对比评估SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256和SM3四种哈希算法在不同硬件资源条件下的计算性能。

Result: 通过在实验环境中对SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256和SM3进行性能对比测试，分析了四种算法在不同硬件资源条件下所需的计算时间，为未来Web服务部署提供参考依据。

Conclusion: 该量子安全的Web服务架构结合了PQC加密与HMAC驱动的TOTP机制，能够同时保障传输安全和服务安全。四种哈希算法的性能对比结果为在不同硬件条件下的Web服务部署提供了选型参考。

Abstract: One-Time Passwords (OTPs) have become a common option for multi-factor authentication in several applications. For instance, during website login processes, OTPs are often used in conjunction with traditional text-based usernames and passwords to verify whether the access request originates from a legitimate human user rather than an automated agent. However, in scenarios involving automated connections and system-to-system interoperability, Time-Based One-Time Passwords (TOTPs) may be required to establish secure connections and access Web Services (WSs). Therefore, this study focuses on exploring the development of a quantum-safe web service architecture. The proposed approach achieves transmission security management by implementing Transport Layer Security (TLS) and HyperText Transfer Protocol Secure (HTTPS) based on Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Furthermore, web service security management is realized through the construction of keyed-Hash Message Authentication Code (HMAC)-driven TOTPs. Within the experimental environment, this study evaluates and compares the computational performance of the Secure Hash Algorithm-2 (SHA-2), SHA-3, Ascon-Hash256, and SM3. The required computation time under different hardware resource conditions is analyzed for future web service deployment.

</details>


### [156] [Probing the Prefill: Detecting Code Vulnerabilities via Latent Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16970)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探索了一种轻量级的漏洞检测方法：直接从冻结的代码生成LLM的隐藏激活中提取信号，训练MLP探针来预测C/C++代码的漏洞状态。在四个基准上的平均F1为41.7%，探针参数仅为基础模型的0.2%。在Devign上最佳探针（Qwen3.5-9B，68.8% F1）匹配了已发布微调分类器的SOTA（67.9%），但在更难、更不平衡的基准上仍落后于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成的防御手段（静态分析器、微调分类器、LLM裁判）都是事后的——它们只检查完成的代码，忽略了生成模型自身的内部状态。作者想问一个更直接的问题：LLM在读取代码作为上下文时，其隐藏激活中是否已经携带了关于该代码漏洞状态的信息？这有望实现轻量级、模型原生的漏洞筛查。

Method: 从四个LLM（Granite-4.1-8B、Qwen3.5-9B、Qwen3.6-27B、Gemma-4-12B，覆盖三个模型家族）中提取最后一个prefill token激活，训练MLP探针（13.4-16.0M参数，不足基座模型0.2%），在四个C/C++函数级基准（Devign、Big-Vul、Draper VDISC、PrimeVul）上评估漏洞分类性能。

Result: 平均F1为41.7%。在Devign上，Qwen3.5-9B的最佳探针达到68.8% F1，匹敌微调分类器SOTA（67.9%）；但在Big-Vul、Draper VDISC、PrimeVul等更难、更不平衡的数据集上，探针与SOTA差距显著。

Conclusion: 这是早期证据表明编码LLM对任意代码的表示本身携带有用于判断漏洞状态的信息，为轻量级、模型原生的漏洞筛查提供了动机和方向。作者认为该方法在简单基准上可匹敌SOTA，但在复杂场景下仍需改进。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation is now embedded in mission-critical pipelines, but defenses against vulnerable output remain post-hoc -- static analyzers, fine-tuned classifiers, or an LLM judge that screen completed code, ignoring the generating model's own internal state. We test a narrower, directly measurable question: when an LLM reads a piece of C/C++ code as context, do its hidden activations already carry a signal about that code's vulnerability status? We extract last prefill token activations from four LLMs (Granite-4.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3.6-27B, Gemma-4-12B) across three model families and train MLP probes on these activations. We evaluate them on four function-level C/C++ benchmarks (Devign, Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul). Our probes achieve 41.7\% average F1 using 13.4--16.0M-parameter probes -- under 0.2\% of base-model size. On Devign, the best probe (Qwen3.5-9B, 68.8\% F1) matches the published fine-tuned-classifier SOTA (67.9\%) despite reading only a frozen, general-purpose LLM's activations; on the harder, more imbalanced benchmarks (Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul) probes trail SOTA substantially. This is early evidence that a coding LLM's own representation of arbitrary code is informative about that code's vulnerability status, motivating further work toward lightweight, model-native vulnerability screening.

</details>


### [157] [Remote-Timer-as-a-Service: Efficient Microarchitectural Leakage in the Cloud with Remote Timers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17043)
*Martin Schwarzl,Haocheng Xiao,Albert Pedersen,Sam Ainsworth,Nigel Topham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了对Cloudflare Workers生产环境中动态进程隔离（DyPrIs）机制的突破性攻击。研究者利用微架构放大技术发现多种在沙箱环境中测量时间的方法，证明现有计时器限制措施不足，成功在真实生产环境中执行了远程Spectre攻击，以99.16%的准确率、最高12bit/s的速度泄露了同驻恶意worker的JWT令牌，性能比现有攻击提升数个数量级（从2bit/min提升至12bit/s）。攻击披露后Cloudflare通过集成V8沙箱、改进DyPrIs检测和部署基于MPK的进程内隔离来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloudflare Workers为了降低启动延迟，移除了多租户间的进程隔离边界，改用语言级隔离，这引入了Spectre攻击风险。虽然此前已部署DyPrIs等缓解措施，但研究者怀疑这些措施在生产环境中的实际有效性不足，试图验证并突破这些安全防护。

Method: 研究者采用微架构放大技术，在生产环境中发现了多种测量时间的方法（包括时序放大和远程计时器），突破了Cloudflare Workers对计时器的冻结和粗化限制。利用这些技术，他们构造了一个完整的远程Spectre攻击链，通过在受控worker中利用推测执行漏洞，跨租户泄露同驻受害者worker的内存数据（JWT令牌）。

Result: 成功在Cloudflare Workers生产环境中执行了端到端的远程Spectre攻击，以99.16%的准确率和最高12bit/s的速率泄露了JWT令牌，比现有攻击（2bit/min）提升了数个数量级。Cloudflare随后通过集成V8沙箱（限制瞬时访问64位指针）、改进DyPrIs检测能力、部署MPK-based进程内隔离等方式修复了该漏洞。

Conclusion: 该研究证明基于语言级隔离的边缘计算架构即便部署了DyPrIs等缓解措施，仍然存在Spectre攻击的实际风险；单纯的计时器限制（冻结和粗化）不足以防御侧信道攻击，需要结合硬件辅助的隔离机制（如V8沙箱和MPK）才能有效缓解。

Abstract: Edge computing solutions have become a crucial part of the industry, delivering fast, flexible and scalable applications close to the end users, with typical use cases including dynamic content creation, image resizing and chatbots. Cloudflare Workers is one such framework, which handles millions of HTTP requests per second worldwide. To reduce start-up latency, Cloudflare Workers removes process-isolation boundaries between multiple tenants and leverages language-level isolation. This architecture poses the risk of Spectre attacks. To mitigate these, Cloudflare Workers previously introduced several countermeasures such as restricted timer measurements, no shared memory, no multithreading and Dynamic Process Isolation (DyPrIs), detecting potential attacks and process-isolating potentially malicious scripts.
  We demonstrate that the production implementation of DyPrIs was insufficient. We adopt microarchitectural amplification techniques and discover various possibilities to measure time in the production environment of Cloudflare Workers. Given these techniques, we show that freezing and coarsening timers in the Cloudflare Workers security model is insufficient. Leveraging both timing amplification and remote timers, we demonstrate a remote Spectre attack that leaks a JWT token from a co-located victim worker in the Cloudflare Workers production environment. We outperform the existing attack by orders of magnitude, going from 2 bit/min to up to 12 bit/s at an accuracy of 99.16%, posing an immediate risk to customer data. Following our end-to-end attack, Cloudflare Workers mitigated it in a coordinated effort by integrating the V8 Sandbox limiting transient access to 64-bit pointers, improving the detection capabilities of DyPrIs, and deploying hardware-assisted MPK-based in-process isolation to confine each tenant heap under a dedicated memory-protection key.

</details>


### [158] [CUSTOS: Toward Forensic-Ready Zero Trust at the Capture-Containment Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17068)
*Avinash Srinivasan,John Paramadilok*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CUSTOS，一种面向取证的零信任参考架构，以取证管理点(FMP)为核心协调分层采集、身份与策略关联的重建、遥测编排及受控调查访问。实验表明：网关端始终在线的决策记录以1.9-3.0%吞吐代价实现哈希链取证；反应式检查点（约65ms）优于防御者路由的驱逐，但无法对抗非排序的直接SIGKILL（约9ms）；在容器中并发捕获与SIGKILL的取证成功率0/1000，而通过FMP屏障排序则达到1000/1000；身份导向遥测可比网络导向填充更多决策记录模式（64-75%对18-30%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任架构以持续验证取代隐式信任，但其核心特性——mTLS、临时工作负载、身份中心控制和自动化补救——在安全增强的同时降低了载荷可见性、削弱了基于IP的属性归因，并缩短了获取易失性证据的时间窗口，导致数字取证面临系统性挑战。

Method: 提出CUSTOS参考架构，核心是取证管理点(FMP)，负责协调分层证据采集、身份与策略关联的取证重建、遥测编排以及受ZT控制的调查访问。通过组合组件级原型进行评估：实时执行网关上的决策哈希链记录、独立的运行时与编排器实验、单节点Kubernetes竞态检查点实验及公测数据集上的模式填充分析。

Result: 始终在线的决策记录在进程内策略引擎上仅造成1.9-3.0%吞吐开销；反应式检查点约65ms，优于秒级防御者路由驱逐，但败于约9ms的非排序SIGKILL；在真实容器中并发采集+SIGKILL取证恢复0/1000，而FMP屏障排序后恢复1000/1000；身份导向遥测填充决策模式64-75%，远超网络导向的18-30%。

Conclusion: 取证就绪的零信任架构需要同时具备始终在线的证据底数和有界的反应式捕获能力；研究同时明确了易失性证据在哪些场景下仍不可恢复，即取证性"碎片化"效应（forensic shredder effect）的边界。

Abstract: Zero Trust (ZT) replaces implicit trust with continuous verification, but mutual TLS, ephemeral workloads, identity-centric control, and automated remediation reduce payload visibility, weaken IP-based attribution, and shrink the window for acquiring volatile evidence. We propose CUSTOS, a forensic-ready ZT reference architecture centered on a Forensic Management Point (FMP) that coordinates tiered capture, identity- and policy-linked reconstruction, telemetry orchestration, and ZT-controlled investigative access. We evaluate a composed, component-level prototype using a live enforcement gateway plus separate runtime and orchestrator experiments. An always-on decision record is captured and hash-chained on the gateway at a 1.9-3.0\% throughput cost on in-process policy engines, preserving decision provenance outside the monitored workload under stated trust assumptions. Reactive checkpointing (about 65 ms) precedes seconds-scale defender-routed eviction but loses to unsequenced direct SIGKILL (about 9 ms), in-kernel enforcement, and adversarial self-destruction, producing the forensic shredder effect. On a real container, concurrent capture and SIGKILL recovered the planted secret in 0/1000 trials; sequencing SIGKILL behind the FMP barrier recovered it in 1000/1000. The primary integrated single-node Kubernetes race checkpoints an FMP-controlled process; container-memory capture is evaluated separately and was unavailable in the managed-Kubernetes configuration. Across five public benchmark datasets and a synthetic schema reference, identity-oriented telemetry populates 64-75\% of the decision-record schema against 18-30\% for network-oriented, while rate limiting bounds the full-memory admission ceiling. These results show that forensic-ready ZT requires both an always-on evidentiary floor and bounded reactive capture, while identifying where volatile evidence remains unrecoverable.

</details>


### [159] [SentryBus: A Multi-Vantage Observability Model and Validated Instrument for I2C Sensor-Interface Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17082)
*Sandesh More,Elton Batista,Karla Daley,Sneha Sudhakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究传感器接口操纵的检测边界问题。提出SentryBus系统，通过被动监控宿主侧I2C总线上的事务时序、读写序列等行为特征，建模正常采集模式，实现对传感器接口中间人攻击的检测。实验表明该方法在保持采集调度和载荷内容的前提下将内联拦截器限制在采集服务时间的0.842%，并在6304秒内保持稳定采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器驱动系统（医疗物联网设备、无人机、信息物理系统）通常信任到达嵌入式处理器的测量值。攻击者可在传感器与处理器之间的数字接口上注入看似合理的伪造值，被固件接受并报告为正常遥测数据。现有防御缺乏对这一攻击面的系统检测手段，因此需要探索被动宿主侧监视能否区分合法采集与受操纵采集。

Method: 提出SentryBus系统，基于I2C传感器总线对采集行为进行建模，特征包括：事务时序、读写序列、传输长度、地址行为、寄存器与FIFO状态访问、原始数据转换。攻击者被建模为四类：内联拦截器、并行控制器、传感器替换、被攻陷主机。采用双端测试平台同时捕获两条总线、主机内存和遥测数据，检测器仅消费宿主侧总线，其余视角作为真值。

Result: 三项实测结果：(1) 内联拦截器被限制在采集服务时间的0.842%，同时保持采集调度和载荷内容；(2) 干净采集在遥测视角下稳定持续6304秒，无时钟回退；(3) 负结果证明数据转换特征编码了会话特定的信号统计量，不能跨采集会话迁移。此外，仪器特性表明低成本分析仪可在无内核可见错误的情况下截断捕获。

Conclusion: 传感器接口操纵在采集路径上留下可从不同视角观察的痕迹，被动宿主侧监视存在可量化的边界，超过该边界后操纵与合法采集不可区分。由于受控攻击试验尚未完成，当前无法声称检测率。结果表明基于事务级行为的被动监视是检测传感器接口攻击的可行方向，但攻击评估仍需后续工作。

Abstract: Sensor-driven systems in medical Internet of Things devices, drones, and cyber-physical systems commonly trust a measurement once it reaches the embedded processor. An adversary on the digital interface between sensor and processor can supply a plausible value that correct firmware accepts and reports as ordinary telemetry. The hypothesis is that sensor interface manipulation leaves observable evidence on the acquisition path, that the evidence appears at different vantages depending on attacker position, and that a passive host-side monitor therefore has a measurable boundary beyond which manipulation becomes indistinguishable from legitimate acquisition. SentryBus models acquisition behavior on the I2C sensor bus using transaction timing, read and write sequences, transfer lengths, address behavior, register and FIFO state access, and raw data transitions. The adversary is modeled as an inline interposer, parallel controller, sensor replacement, or compromised host, because a commodity target-only sensor cannot initiate transfers or stretch, reorder, or delay bus transactions. A dual sided testbed captures both busses, host memory, and telemetry, and the detector consumes the host facing bus alone while the remaining vantages serve as ground truth. A physiological instantiation reports three measured results: an inline interposer bounded at 0.842 percent of acquisition service time while preserving acquisition schedule and payload content, clean acquisition stability sustained over 6304 seconds at the telemetry vantage with no clock regression, and a negative result establishing that data-transition features encode session specific signal statistics and do not transfer across capture sessions. Instrument characterization shows that a low-cost analyzer can truncate captures without kernel visible error. Controlled attack trials are still outstanding, so no detection rate is claimed.

</details>


### [160] [Structured Driving-State Narratives for Small Language Model-Based GNSS Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17092)
*Abyad Enan,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的框架，用于检测和分类自动驾驶车辆中的GNSS欺骗攻击。该框架将GNSS与其他传感源导出的独立驾驶状态转换为结构化语义叙述，供SLM进行欺骗检测和攻击分类。实验表明，SLM在检测性能上与微调的大型语言模型（LLM）相当（平均准确率96.99%，精确率99.05%，召回率95.59%，F1分数97.18%），但在推理延迟和GPU内存占用方面显著更优，适合部署在资源受限的车载计算平台上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆高度依赖GNSS定位，但GNSS信号容易被欺骗攻击，导致车辆获得看似合理实则错误的定位信息，构成严重的安全威胁。现有检测方法大多依赖复杂的机器学习模型或大型语言模型，计算和内存开销大，难以在资源受限的车载平台实时部署。因此需要一个高效、轻量级且准确的GNSS欺骗检测与分类方案。

Method: 该框架构建了一个SLM（小语言模型）为基础的检测与分类系统。具体方法为：(1) 将来自GNSS和其他独立传感源（如惯性测量单元等）的独立驾驶状态数据转换为结构化的语义叙述（structured semantic narratives）；(2) 将这些叙述输入SLM进行GNSS欺骗攻击检测和攻击类型分类；(3) 设计了五类分类任务：无攻击、超调攻击、停止攻击、逐向攻击和错误转向攻击；(4) 与基于相同训练数据微调的LLM进行性能对比，并在地理上未见过的现场数据（南卡罗来纳州克莱姆森采集）上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) SLM在检测性能上与LLM相当，平均准确率96.99%、精确率99.05%、召回率95.59%、F1分数97.18%；(2) SLM在推理延迟和GPU内存占用（微调和推理阶段均）方面明显优于LLM；(3) 在地理位置不同的现场数据上验证了框架的泛化有效性；(4) 该框架能够实现实时GNSS欺骗检测与分类，同时计算和内存开销较低。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，小语言模型能够以接近大型语言模型的检测精度实现GNSS欺骗攻击的实时检测与分类，同时在计算效率和资源利用方面具有显著优势。该框架适用于资源受限的自动驾驶车载计算平台，为GNSS安全防护提供了一种轻量化、可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) depend on reliable Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) positioning. However, spoofed GNSS signals can induce plausible but incorrect vehicle states. This study develops a small language model (SLM)-based framework for detecting and classifying GNSS spoofing attacks by comparing vehicle behaviors independently derived from GNSS and other sensing sources. The framework converts independent driving states from GNSS and other sensing sources into structured semantic narratives that are provided to an SLM for spoofing detection and attack classification. The performance of the SLM-based framework is compared with large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned on identical training data and evaluated on the same test set. The evaluation considers five classes: no attack, overshoot attack, stopped attack, turn-by-turn attack, and wrong-turn attack. The framework is also evaluated with geographically unseen field data collected in Clemson, South Carolina, United States. Experimental results indicate that the evaluated SLMs achieve performance similar to the LLMs, achieving an average accuracy of 96.99%, precision of 99.05%, recall of 95.59%, and F1-score of 97.18%. In terms of computational efficiency and resource utilization, the SLMs demonstrate advantages over the LLMs by requiring lower inference latency and less GPU memory during both fine-tuning and inference. Evaluation using field data collected in a geographically distinct location further demonstrated its efficacy. The presented framework can detect and classify GNSS spoofing attacks in real-time while requiring relatively low computational and memory resources, and is therefore suitable for deployment on resource-constrained vehicular computing platforms.

</details>


### [161] [Digital Twin-Based Intrusion Detection for Vehicle Powertrain CAN Bus Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17093)
*Araf Rahman,M Sabbir Salek,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于数字孪生（DT）的汽车CAN总线入侵检测系统，使用共享编码器LSTM对17个解码后的动力传动系统信号进行联合建模，通过预测与观测行为之间的残差来检测保持正常通信模式的隐蔽载荷操纵攻击。在四个攻击场景（平台、连续漂移、冒充和挡位冒充）的评估中，DT系统以94.6%和89.2%的检测率超越了传统基线方法，但39.6%的误报率表明仍需改进鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有汽车CAN入侵检测系统主要针对消息时序、频率或序列的异常进行检测，无法识别那些保持正常通信属性但操纵载荷的攻击。虽然数字孪生已被用于模拟CAN流量和生成攻击场景以评估IDS，但其在入侵检测本身的应用尚未被探索。

Method: 提出了一种基于数字孪生的IDS架构，采用共享编码器LSTM对真实Hyundai/Kia CAN日志中的17个解码信号进行联合建模，在24步时间窗口内预测七个数值信号和两个分类挡位信号。通过残差超过校准阈值来标记异常时间步，并采用自适应滚动机制防止持续污染对孪生模型输入历史的破坏。评估了四种攻击类型，并与范围和合理性基线方法进行对比。

Result: DT系统在所有攻击类型上均优于基线方法：连续漂移攻击检测率达94.6%，冒充攻击检测率达89.2%，而基线方法几乎无法检测到任何伪造载荷攻击。但误报率高达39.6%，表明在持续攻击下系统的鲁棒性仍需改进。

Conclusion: 学习耦合的车辆动力学特性能够有效检测保持正常CAN通信模式的隐蔽载荷操纵攻击，DT-based IDS为联网和自动化车辆的行为驱动型网络安全提供了有前景的方向，但需要进一步降低误报率以增强实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Existing automotive intrusion detection systems (IDSs) for the Controller Area Network (CAN) largely target discrepancies in message timing, frequency, or sequencing and cannot detect attacks that preserve these properties while manipulating the payload. Digital twins (DTs) have been used to emulate CAN traffic and generate attack scenarios for IDS evaluation, but their use for intrusion detection remains unexplored. This study develops a DT-based IDS that jointly models physical relationships among decoded powertrain signals and identifies attacks through residuals between predicted and observed behavior. A shared-encoder LSTM DT was trained on 17 decoded signals from a real Hyundai/Kia CAN log to jointly predict seven numeric and two categorical gear signals over a 24-step window. A timestep is flagged when a residual exceeds a calibrated threshold, while adaptive rollout protects the twin's input history from sustained contamination. Four attacks (plateau, continuous drift, masquerade, and gear masquerade) were evaluated against the twin and a range-and-plausibility baseline. The DT outperformed the baseline across all attacks, achieving detection rates of 94.6% for continuous drift and 89.2% for masquerade, while the baseline detected almost none of the fabricated payload attacks. These results demonstrate that learning coupled vehicle dynamics enables detection of stealthy payload manipulations that preserve normal CAN communication patterns. False positive rates reached 39.6%, highlighting the need for improved robustness under sustained attacks. The DT-based IDS shows promise for detecting stealthy payload-level CAN attacks that preserve normal communication patterns, supporting behavior-based cybersecurity for connected and automated vehicles.

</details>


### [162] [Protocol-Embedded Compliance for Privacy-Preserving, Non-Custodial Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17145)
*Santiago De Simone,Geoffrey Goodell,Georgios Samakovitis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"托管式账户模型是数字支付合规性（如AML、审计）的必要条件"这一传统观点，提出非托管、类现金的数字资产同样能够实现强大的可审计性和合规性，且安全性更高。作者基于USO资产模型提出了一个参考架构和核心协议规则，将合规数据作为加密签名的证明嵌入资产状态中，在协议层面而非机构托管层面执行合规约束，并论证该模型能与现有支付系统成功对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，支付基础设施领域的主流共识认为，托管式、基于账户的模型是保证交易完整性、可审计性和可验证性的必要条件；监管合规（尤其是AML）必须依赖可识别的实体来存储凭证、执行KYC并充当合规的"单一真相来源"。本文旨在推翻这一假设，论证非托管、现金式数字资产同样可以承载这些能力，从而在隐私与合规之间找到更好的平衡点。

Method: 作者提出一个参考架构和核心协议规则，用于数字价值交换系统。具体方法包括：(1) 基于Goodell等人的不可伪造、有状态、不经意（USO）资产模型；(2) 将监管合规数据以加密签名的证明形式直接嵌入资产状态，由独立实体签发；(3) 仅在满足合规谓词且结果签名纳入资产状态时，转移才有效；(4) 通过协议层面的合规执行取代机构托管或基于身份账户控制；(5) 论证该架构可与现有支付系统连接，实现与遗留金融基础设施的集成。

Result: 作者通过协议设计与架构论证表明，非托管数字资产可以在协议层面实现合规性执行，而不依赖托管机构或身份账户体系。该模型支持资产创建、转移和赎回的合法性条件定义，并能使独立实体验证交易是否合规。研究结论认为该模型能够成功与现有支付系统接口，使非托管、合规验证的交易能够集成到遗留金融基础设施中。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：托管并非数字支付合规性的必要条件。通过将合规证明嵌入资产状态的协议设计，可以在保持用户隐私的同时实现强大的可审计性和监管合规性，且该方法在安全性上更具优势。该模型可与现有支付系统互操作，为"非托管+合规验证"的数字交易融入传统金融体系提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Received wisdom on payments infrastructure strongly supports the custodial, account-based model as a necessity for transaction integrity, auditability and verification; the set of fundamental primitives for regulated digital money exchange, the argument goes, necessitates designated identifiable entities that store and process credentials, perform KYC, and ultimately act as the 'single version of the truth' for compliance remediation and, most important, AML. In this paper, we propose this is not the case, by arguing that non-custodial, cash-like digital assets can embody such capabilities, in an arguably more secure manner.
  To that end, we present a reference architecture and core protocol rules for digital-value-exchange systems that preserve meaningful user privacy while enabling strong auditability. The protocol defines the conditions under which digital asset creation, transfer, and redemption are valid. The architecture specifies the allocation of actors, roles and components through which these rules operate, enabling independent verification of transaction compliance with applicable norms. Building upon the Unforgeable, Stateful, Oblivious (USO) asset model of Goodell et al., regulatory compliance data are embedded directly into the asset state as cryptographically signed attestations issued by independent entities. A transfer is valid only upon satisfaction of applicable compliance predicates and inclusion of the resulting signature within the asset state. Compliance enforcement is thus performed at the protocol level rather than through institutional custody or identity-based account control. We conclude that our proposed model can successfully interface with existing payment systems, making it possible to integrate non-custodial, compliance-verified transactions with legacy financial infrastructure.

</details>


### [163] [Picture the Epsilon: Pursuing Identity-Level Privacy Guarantees for Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17147)
*Arman Zareian Jahromi,Vishnu Bondalakunta,Mohammad Akbar Bin Shah,Naimul Haque,Shuangqing Wei,George T. Amariucai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对四种针对预训练黑盒人脸生成器的身份级差分隐私审计方法进行了比较研究，在FaceFusion和InstantID上的实验表明，在高身份可区分性场景下，四种方法的一致结论是存在显著的身份可区分性，但各自报告不同的epsilon估计值，无法支持可靠的排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸生成器的输出与源图像之间的视觉不相似性有时被当作隐私的证据，但对此进行形式化的身份级差分隐私审计需要选择多种不同的途径将嵌入空间观测转换为对差分隐私参数epsilon的估计或界限。现有研究缺乏对这些审计方法的系统比较，尤其是在统一的审计框架下。

Method: 提出并比较四种适用于预训练黑盒人脸生成器的审计方法：(1) 基于每身份敏感性的高斯机制解读（GaussMech）；(2) 基于逐维度核密度对数比并通过基本组合聚合的KDE-LR；(3) 通过最大均值差异与全变差距离推导纯DP epsilon的解析总体下界（MMD-TV）；(4) 基于交叉验证分类器出折ROC的假设检验评估（ROC-HT）。对每种方法明确其假设、超参数依赖、有限样本局限及epsilon估计有效的作用域。

Result: 在FaceFusion和InstantID上使用多种身份编码器和参考数据集进行实验，四种审计一致揭示显著的身份可区分性，但报告的epsilon估计值因各方法的假设和有限样本处理不同而差异明显。在高可区分性体制下，实验结果不支持对四种方法进行可靠排名。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的身份级审计框架，阐明各方法假设和有限样本处理如何塑造差分隐私估计结果，并指出对部分隐私机制进行评估是自然的下一步研究方向。

Abstract: Image-to-image face generators are widely used, and visual dissimilarity between their outputs and source images is sometimes treated as evidence of privacy. Auditing whether these systems satisfy formal identity-level (epsilon, delta)-differential privacy requires choosing among several distinct routes for converting embedding-space observations into estimates or bounds on the differential privacy parameter epsilon. We present a comparative study of four such audits applicable to pre-trained, black-box face generators: a Gaussian-mechanism reading of per-identity sensitivity (GaussMech); a per-dimension kernel-density log-ratio aggregated by basic composition (KDE-LR); an analytical population-level lower bound on pure-DP epsilon derived from the maximum mean discrepancy via the total variation distance (MMD-TV); and a hypothesis-testing evaluation of a cross-validated classifier's out-of-fold ROC (ROC-HT). For each method we make explicit its assumptions, hyperparameter dependence, finite-sample limitations, and the regime in which its epsilon estimate is informative. Applied to FaceFusion and InstantID across multiple identity encoders and reference datasets, the audits consistently reveal substantial identity distinguishability while reporting markedly different epsilon estimates that reflect each method's distinct assumptions and finite-sample treatment. In this high-distinguishability regime, the experiments do not support a reliable ranking of the four methods. Their relative trade-offs should be evaluated on partially private mechanisms, which we identify as the natural next study. The resulting framework places these audits in a shared identity-level audit setting and clarifies how their assumptions and finite-sample treatments shape the resulting differential privacy estimates.

</details>


### [164] [Authorization Before Context: A Model-Neutral Audience Boundary Against Cross-Audience Memory Leakage in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17148)
*Sibo Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"授权先于上下文"（authorization before context）机制，在个人语言智能体的"记忆→上下文"转换阶段应用一个反单调的受众成员规则：每一条记忆条目携带记录时的受众，当前观众集合从频道元数据中读取（模糊时回退为公开），仅当所有当前观众都属于该条目的原始受众时才将其纳入上下文。该规则通过排除而非模型行为来保证：记录给较窄受众的内容绝不会到达较广泛受众，且被投毒的记忆无法自行扩大受众范围。在合成Contextual-Integrity测试集上，该边界构造的上下文未放入任何禁止事实，而未经范围限定的基线则必然包含此类事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人语言智能体从一个受众学到事实后，可能在为另一受众组装提示时将其放入上下文，这一"记忆到上下文"环节存在攻击面：模糊或不一致的频道、跨受众窥探以及被投毒的记忆，都可能导致系统组装出与查询相关但对当前观众未授权的上下文。现有方案依赖模型行为来保证隐私，缺乏一个模型无关的确定性边界。

Method: 提出单一的反单调受众成员规则，在记忆到上下文转换时应用。每条记忆条目附带其记录时的受众；当前观众集合从频道元数据读取，模糊时回退为公开；仅当每个当前观众都已属于条目的受众时，该条目才被接纳。这是模型无关的不变式，确保禁止的事实必须在模型被调用之前就从确切组装的上下文中缺席。

Result: 在合成Contextual-Integrity测试集上，该边界组装的上下文未放入任何禁止事实，而无范围限定的基线按构造必然包含此类事实；进一步审计表明每条读取路径都安全关闭（fail closed）。证据是初步和合成的。

Conclusion: 该工作证明可以通过在上下文组装边界上施加一个简单的反单调受众规则，以排除机制而非依赖模型行为来保证跨频道信息隔离，从而为个人语言智能体提供可验证的隐私保障基础；但结论仍需要更充分的真实场景验证。

Abstract: A personal language agent learns a fact from one audience and may later place it in the prompt it assembles for another. This memory-to-context step is an attack surface: ambiguous or inconsistent channels, cross-audience prying, and poisoned memory can each cause the system to assemble context containing a fact relevant to the query yet unauthorized for the current viewers. We introduce authorization before context: a single, anti-monotone audience-membership rule applied at the memory-to-context transition. Each item carries the audience present when it was recorded; the current viewer set is read from channel metadata and falls back to public when ambiguous; and the item is admitted only when every current viewer already belonged to its audience. We prove that this rule gives every participant cross-channel recall while ensuring, by exclusion rather than by model behavior, that nothing recorded for a narrower audience reaches a broader one and that poisoned memory cannot widen its own audience. The boundary is a model-neutral invariant on the exact assembled context: a forbidden fact must be absent before the model is called. On a synthetic Contextual-Integrity suite, no forbidden fact entered the context our boundary assembled, whereas unscoped baselines included such facts by construction; we further audit that every read path fails closed. The evidence is preliminary and synthetic.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond the Hype: Evaluating LLM Integration and Practical Limitations in Security Operation Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17154)
*Elnaz Rabieinejad,Ali Dehghantanha,Fattane Zarrinkalam,Sarina Dastgerdy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对20名SOC从业者的半结构化访谈，揭示了LLM在安全运营中心（SOC）中的实际使用现状：从业者认可LLM在低风险、可快速验证任务（如日志摘要和草拟初步调查线索）中带来的时间节省，但普遍将LLM输出视为初步草稿而非决策级结论；在高风险的安防决策中，由于输出不可靠和推理不透明，信任度有限。研究提出了一个成熟度评估框架用于衡量LLM集成就绪度，并规划了强调可审计性和透明解释机制的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM正被越来越多地引入SOC以支持文本密集型分析工作，但实践者对幻觉、推理不透明以及验证成本等关键运营问题存在严重担忧。然而，目前缺乏基于一线从业者实际经验的系统性理解，需要深入探究SOC从业者如何感知和使用LLM，以及这些感知如何影响LLM在安全流程中的安全采纳。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对20名SOC从业者进行半结构化访谈，受访者涵盖基层分析师、SOC经理和工具开发者三个层级。通过归纳分析访谈记录，提炼出从业者对LLM在时间节省、信任度、验证规范等方面的共识性认知，并基于访谈发现构建成熟度评估框架。

Result: 研究发现：（1）从业者报告LLM在低风险且可快速验证的任务（如日志总结、草拟初步调查线索）中能节省时间；（2）LLM输出被一致地定位为初步草稿和建议，而非决策级结论；（3）由于输出不可靠和模型推理不清晰，从业者对LLM在高风险安全决策中的信任有限；（4）从业者主要依赖临时性的验证规范和持续人工监督，而非标准化缓解程序。

Conclusion: 基于访谈证据，论文提出了一个衡量LLM集成就绪度的成熟度评估框架，并勾勒了强调可审计性和透明解释机制的研究议程，以促进LLM在SOC工作流中的更安全采纳。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored within Security Operation Centers (SOCs) to support text-heavy analytical work such as alert contextualization, incident summarization, and drafting investigative artifacts. Despite this interest, practitioners describe critical operational concerns, most notably hallucinations (plausible but incorrect outputs), opaque reasoning, and the verification effort required to safely use model-generated content in security workflows. In this paper, we present findings from semi-structured interviews with 20 SOC practitioners spanning frontline analysts, SOC managers, and tool developers. Participants report perceived time savings for low-stakes tasks that are quickly verifiable (e.g., summarizing logs or drafting initial investigative leads), but they consistently frame LLM outputs as preliminary drafts and suggestions rather than decision-grade conclusions. Participants also describe limited trust in LLMs for high-stakes security decisions due to unreliable outputs and unclear model reasoning, and they report relying primarily on ad-hoc verification norms and continuous human oversight rather than standardized mitigation procedures. Based on these interview-grounded accounts, we introduce a maturity rubric to characterize readiness for LLM integration and outline a research agenda emphasizing auditability and transparent explanation mechanisms to support safer adoption in SOC workflows.

</details>


### [166] [The Acknowledgment Point Is the System: Durable Policy-Decision Receipts for AI Audit Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17176)
*Neeraj Kumar Singh Beshane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍RuntimeGuard-AI研究原型，一个围绕持久化与信任边界约束重构的AI审计记录系统。它将策略决策绑定到策略源码，在调用者选择的同步边界提交隐私最小化记录，并返回Ed25519签名回执明确指示边界是否完成。系统通过签名Merkle纪元支持审计验证，并在Apple M4 Pro上实现了可测量的持久性-延迟权衡（缓冲模式下27,193 req/s，全同步模式242 req/s），同时明确承认了其局限（不证明模型执行、不防分叉、不具法律合规性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI审计记录的耐用性和信任边界必须是显式的。返回有守卫的决策若在持久化写入前完成可最小化延迟，但无法保证证据能在立即崩溃后幸存。这一核心矛盾驱动作者重新构建RuntimeGuard-AI，以明确刻画持久性与延迟之间的权衡。

Method: 系统将每个确定性策略决策绑定到精确的策略源码；在调用者选择的同步点提交隐私最小化记录；返回Ed25519签名的回执，明确声明同步边界是否完成。重启后引擎验证帧记录、清单、分片放置、序列连续性和重放身份。独立的认证路径将已提交记录分组成链式签名Merkle纪元，审计者通过外部获得的密钥进行验证。

Result: 在Apple M4 Pro上，4个工作线程、2,048字节提示下，缓冲签名的证据达到27,193请求/秒，中位延迟141.9微秒；每条记录全数据全同步时吞吐降至约242请求/秒，中位延迟升至16.0毫秒；封存100,000条记录签名纪元耗时97.0毫秒。

Conclusion: 结果是经过测量的持久性-延迟权衡，而非"免费"的异步审计路径。原型不证明模型执行、不能防止被攻陷的签名者分叉历史，也不建立法律合规性，这些是明确的边界与后续方向。

Abstract: An AI audit record is useful only if its durability and trust boundary are explicit. Returning a guarded decision before any durable write minimizes latency, but it cannot guarantee that evidence survives an immediate crash. We rebuild RuntimeGuard-AI around this constraint. The resulting research prototype binds each deterministic policy decision to the exact policy source, commits a privacy-minimizing record at a caller-selected synchronization boundary, and returns an Ed25519-signed receipt that states whether that boundary completed. After restart, the engine validates framed records, manifests, shard placement, sequence continuity, and replay identity. A separate attestation path groups committed records into chained, signed Merkle epochs that an auditor verifies with an externally obtained key. On an Apple M4 Pro at four worker threads and 2,048-byte prompts, buffered signed evidence reaches 27,193 requests/s with 141.9 microseconds median latency. Per-record data and full synchronization reduce throughput to approximately 242 requests/s and raise median latency to 16.0 ms. Sealing a 100,000-record signed epoch takes 97.0 ms. The result is a measured durability-latency trade-off, not a "free" asynchronous audit path. The prototype does not prove model execution, prevent a compromised signer from forking history, or establish legal conformity.

</details>


### [167] [PACE: Policy-Attested Contract Execution for Safe AI Agents in Decentralized Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17220)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Richard Williamson,Hemanth Hm,Prakhar Mehrotra,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE（Policy-Attested Contract Execution），一个位于LLM智能体和链上执行之间的交易级授权框架。PACE通过类型化交易意图、确定性策略验证器以及带签名策略决策记录（PDR）将批准的意图、策略和模拟报告以密码学方式绑定到最终提交执行的精确字节上，并具备重放和过期保护。在确定性沙箱评估中实现0.00不安全执行率和0.00误报率，与未防护基线的0.80形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体正在成为DeFi操作的接口，但它们依赖LLM进行交易规划，继承了LLM对提示注入的脆弱性，且缺乏将验证者批准与最终链上提交的精确交易绑定的机制，导致安全隐患。

Method: PACE引入三层设计：类型化交易意图、确定性策略验证器、以及带签名的策略决策记录（PDR）将批准意图、策略和模拟报告密码学绑定到执行字节，并配备重放与过期保护。通过Solidity智能账户在链上强制执行PDR签名。评估采用40个任务覆盖四类攻击加良性用例，共2800次试验、10个种子，并包含消融研究及三模型实时LLM评估。

Result: PACE在确定性沙箱中达到0.00不安全执行率和0.00误报率，未防护基线为0.80。链上气体开销为29,826-31,822 gas。消融实验显示许可策略设置（+57.5个百分点）和touched-contract允许列表（+12.5个百分点）是主要安全组件。

Conclusion: 作者将PACE的声明界定为可复现基准内的逻辑级安全，而非部署就绪的DeFi安全保障。补充研究（三模型实时LLM评估、主网分叉测试）与确定性基准分离，不替代其结论。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents are emerging as interfaces for decentralized finance (DeFi) actions such as swaps, lending operations, and yield management. Because these agents rely on large language models (LLMs) to plan transactions, they inherit the LLM's susceptibility to prompt injection and lack of mechanisms to bind a verifier's approval to the exact transaction ultimately submitted on-chain. We present PACE (Policy-Attested Contract Execution), a transaction-level authorization framework that interposes between an LLM-based agent and on-chain execution. PACE introduces typed transaction intents, a deterministic policy verifier, and signed Policy Decision Records (PDRs) that cryptographically bind the approved intent, policy, and simulation report to the exact execution bytes, with replay and expiration protection. A Solidity smart account enforces PDR signatures on-chain with a measured overhead of 29,826-31,822 gas. We evaluate PACE against six baselines on 40 tasks spanning four attack categories plus benign utility (2,800 trials, 10 seeds). In our deterministic sandbox, PACE achieves a 0.00 unsafe execution rate and 0.00 false-positive rate on benign tasks, compared to 0.80 for the unguarded baseline. Ablation studies identify permissive policy settings (+57.5 pp) and the touched-contract allowlist (+12.5 pp) as the dominant safety components. To test whether the same deterministic floor holds for real model outputs, the artifact additionally provides a three-model live-LLM evaluation over the full task suite with repeated runs. A mainnet-fork harness is included for archive-RPC deployments, but fork results are reported only when the corresponding artifacts are generated. These auxiliary studies are separate from, and never substitute for, the deterministic benchmark. We frame our claims as logic-level safety within a reproducible benchmark rather than deployment-ready DeFi security.

</details>


### [168] [COMIC: Reference-Aware Safety Gating for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17234)
*Md Abdullahil Oaphy,Anhao Xiang,Zongxing Xie,Huayue Gu,Chenyu Wang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文识别了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中的引用依赖型安全失效模式——即提示词和图像各自无害，但当模型将看似良性的操作（如摘要、翻译、跟随）绑定到特定视觉目标时才产生不安全行为。作者提出COMIC（Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier），一个引用感知的生成前安全门控机制，通过推断操作与引用类型、构建候选目标、解析指代并评估操作-目标对的安全性来防御此类越狱攻击。实验表明COMIC在多个开源MLLM、局部化及广泛多模态越狱基准和良性引用敏感场景中均提升了鲁棒性，同时保持良性效用和实际效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态防御主要对整个提示-图像对进行整体审查，但真正的安全相关单元是接地（grounding）过程中产生的操作-目标对。当有害语义被局部化、仅在地接后被激活且依赖于视觉引用解析时，现有防御机制性能显著退化，这暴露了结构性弱点。

Method: COMIC作为生成前的安全门控机制，包含四个步骤：(1) 推断请求的操作类型和引用类型；(2) 从OCR和开放词汇提议中构建候选目标；(3) 将指代解析到具体的视觉目标；(4) 对显式的操作-目标对进行安全评估。为保守处理歧义，COMIC结合最大风险聚合与质量感知路由来决定放行或阻止请求。

Result: 在多个开源MLLM、局部化及广泛多模态越狱基准和良性引用敏感场景上的评估表明，COMIC一致性地提升了鲁棒性，同时保持了良性效用和实际效率。

Conclusion: 多模态安全无法仅靠整体审查来可靠执行，必须建模请求的操作、该操作所作用的视觉目标以及该接地的置信度。这项发现为多模态安全防御提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interact with screenshots, scanned documents, diagrams, and other visually grounded inputs. This shift introduces a new safety risk: in many multimodal jailbreaks, neither the prompt nor the image is harmful in isolation. Unsafe behavior emerges only when the model binds an apparently benign operation, such as summarizing, translating, or following, to a localized visual target. This reveals a structural weakness in current multimodal defenses, which largely moderate the prompt-image pair as a whole even though the true security-relevant unit is the grounded operation-target pair produced during dereference. In this work, we identify and analyze this reference-dependent failure mode and show that existing defenses degrade when harmful semantics are localized, activated only after grounding, and dependent on visual reference resolution. To address this problem, we propose COMIC (Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier), a reference-aware pre-generation safety gate for MLLMs. COMIC first infers the requested operation and reference type, constructs candidate targets from OCR and open-vocabulary proposals, grounds plausible referents, and evaluates safety over explicit operation-target pairs. To handle ambiguity conservatively, COMIC combines max-risk aggregation with quality-aware routing before deciding whether to forward or block a request. We evaluate COMIC across multiple open-source MLLMs, localized and broader multimodal jailbreak benchmarks, and benign reference-sensitive settings. The results show that COMIC consistently improves robustness while preserving benign utility and practical efficiency. More broadly, our findings suggest that multimodal safety cannot be enforced reliably without modeling the requested operation, the visual target to which it applies, and the confidence of that grounding.

</details>


### [169] [ADAPTD: Adaptive Detection and Proactive Threat Defense for Autonomous APT attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17251)
*Yeongwoo Kim,Quanyan Zhu,György Dán*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPTD框架，一个通信与计算高效的决策理论框架，通过紧凑kill chain、即时阻断机制和预测性驱逐策略实现跨子网络的高级持续性威胁（APT）横向移动检测与防御，在多种威胁场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击者越来越多地使用复杂技术在分段企业网络中横向传播。及时检测和防御依赖于跨子网络协调，但维持全局态势感知会产生大量通信开销。为管理这一权衡，需要灵活的监控和适应性遏制机制。

Method: ADAPTD框架包含三个核心组件：(i)紧凑kill chains用于识别多样化攻击向量；(ii)即时阻断机制实现及时遏制；(iii)预测性驱逐策略恢复系统安全。采用去中心化信念更新方案替代全局通信，结合两种防御行动（阻断与驱逐）进行决策理论优化。

Result: 实验验证表明：(1)去中心化信念更新方案优于最先进的扩散HMM；(2)相比基于transformer的检测，ADAPTD大幅减少了误驱逐；(3)在噪声环境下，自适应阻断能有效遏制攻击者同时最小化不必要的中断；(4)消融研究证实结合两种防御行动能显著降低防御者的总成本。

Conclusion: ADAPTD通过去中心化的信念更新和组合式防御行动（即时阻断+预测性驱逐），在降低通信开销的同时实现了高效的APT横向移动检测与遏制，为分段企业网络安全提供了有效的决策理论解决方案。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) actors increasingly employ sophisticated techniques to propagate laterally through segmented enterprise networks. Timely detection and defense depend on cross-subnetwork coordination, yet maintaining global situational awareness generates substantial communication overhead. To manage this tradeoff, flexible monitoring and adaptable containment are imperative. This paper presents ADAPTD, a communication- and computation-efficient, decision-theoretic framework integrating: (i) compact kill chains for identifying diverse attack vectors, (ii) an immediate blocking mechanism for timely containment, and (iii) a predictive eviction strategy to restore system security. Our experiments validate ADAPTD's effectiveness across diverse threat scenarios. First, our decentralized belief update scheme outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion HMM. Second, ADAPTD substantially reduces false evictions compared to transformer-based detection. Third, under noisy environments, adaptive blocking contains attackers while minimizing unnecessary disruption. Lastly, the ablation study confirms that combining two defensive actions significantly reduces the defender's total cost.

</details>


### [170] [When Agents Act on Web3: An Attack-Surface Survey of MCP, Skills, and Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17275)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Nour Diallo,Wujie Xiong,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文调查了AI代理在公开区块链上执行工具调用时的安全威胁。区块链执行层的四个特性（不可逆性、签名权限、持续自主性和序列级组合）从根本上改变了威胁模型，将通常可恢复的代理安全故障转化为永久性损失。作者提出了攻击面分类法和Web3风险映射矩阵，并发现现有防御措施严重不足（仅阻止不到30%的攻击，模型级安全拒绝不到3%），最后据此推导出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理正从仅读取信息转向实际修改外部状态（MCP生态中部署工具中修改状态的占比从27%升至65%）。当这些代理在公开区块链上行使权限时，攻击后果由区块链执行层决定，而非传统软件假设。现有MCP安全文献碎片化，缺乏系统性的威胁模型分析和防御评估，需要一份综合调查来厘清这一新兴风险领域的全景。

Method: 本文采用调查方法：首先论证区块链执行层的四个关键属性（不可逆性、签名权威、持续自主性、序列级组合）如何从质上改变威胁模型；其次将碎片化的MCP安全文献组织为攻击面分类学；然后构建Web3风险映射矩阵，将每类攻击与放大影响、责任放大器、代表性缓解措施和残余缺口关联；最后综合防御手段并进行定量评估。

Result: 测量结果显示防御改进但不足：已测量的防护措施只能阻止不到30%的攻击，模型级安全机制拒绝率低于3%。作者将工作与相邻调查进行了位置对比，并从矩阵的开放单元格中推导出研究议程。

Conclusion: 区块链执行层使AI代理安全从"可恢复故障"变为"持续性不可逆损失"，现有防御体系在数量和有效性上均远未达标，迫切需要基于区块链原生机制的新型防护方案，并从风险矩阵的空白处建立系统的研究路线。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act rather than merely read: across the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem, the share of deployed tools that modify external state has risen from 27% to 65% of tool use. When agents exercise this authority on public blockchains through MCP, skills, and tool calling, the consequences of an attack are governed by the blockchain execution layer rather than by conventional software assumptions. This survey argues that four properties of that layer (irreversibility, signing authority, continuous autonomy, and sequence-level composition) qualitatively change the threat model, turning the recoverable failures of generic agent security into a standing, irreversible loss. We organize the fragmented MCP-security literature into an attack-surface taxonomy, then contribute a Web3 risk-mapping matrix that ties each attack class to its amplified impact, the responsible amplifiers, a representative mitigation, and the residual gap. We synthesize defenses, including emerging blockchain-based mechanisms, and find them improving but insufficient: measured protections stop fewer than 30% of attacks, and model-level safety refuses fewer than 3%. We close by positioning the work against adjacent surveys and deriving a research agenda from the matrix's open cells.

</details>


### [171] [FlowShield: cryptocurrency anti-money laundering with transaction semantics parsing and fund flow tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17355)
*Qishuang Fu,Andreas Deppeler,Joseph K. Liu,Yixin Liu,Shirui Pan,Qin Wang,Weiqing Wang,Tsz Hon Yuen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlowShield，一个面向加密货币反洗钱（Crypto AML）的框架，通过行为级语义恢复、资金流子图重构和文本-结构融合机制，实现交易级洗钱检测并生成面向调查人员的可读可疑活动报告（SARs）。同时开源了首个公开的多链洗钱数据集 BybitML，平均 F1 分数达 98.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密货币反洗钱方法面临三大挑战：①将复杂洗钱行为过度简化，难以捕捉资金快速碎片化漂移的多元语义；②过度依赖拓扑中心性信号，忽略行为语义；③仅输出孤立的检测标签，缺乏可解释性和可读性。此外，多链场景下缺乏公开数据集，数据稀缺问题突出。
