Method: FlowShield 包含四个核心组件：①从可观测关系中恢复行为级语义，使洗钱意图显式化；②从三个互补视角重构资金流子图，用于追踪价值来源与再分配；③采用文本-结构融合机制，将大语言模型（LLM）编码的语义信息与图卷积网络（GCN）编码的结构信息进行交互融合；④生成可读的可疑活动报告（SARs），为调查人员提供简洁摘要和可解释的红色预警标识。

Result: 在 BybitML 和两个公开洗钱数据集上，FlowShield 取得最佳综合性能，平均 F1 分数达 98.0%。行为分析和 SAR 分析表明，FlowShield 能够揭示多样化的洗钱策略，并为调查复杂多跳资金流动生成可读报告。

Conclusion: FlowShield 通过结合 LLM 语义编码与 GCN 结构编码的融合机制，以及资金流子图重构，有效解决了现有 Crypto AML 方法中语义简化、拓扑依赖和输出单一的问题，实现了高性能的检测与可解释的报告生成，为多链洗钱检测提供了新的思路和开源基准。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency anti-money laundering (Crypto AML) is increasingly challenged by sophisticated laundering behaviors that rapidly fragment stolen assets through diverse semantics and across multiple blockchains. Existing Crypto AML methods often simplify transaction semantics, rely on topology-centric signals, or output isolated detection labels. In this paper, we present \textsc{FlowShield}, a Crypto AML framework for transaction-level laundering detection and investigator-facing report generation. \textsc{FlowShield} first recovers behavior-level semantics from observable relations, making laundering intents explicit. To trace value provenance and redistribution, \textsc{FlowShield} reconstructs fund-flow subgraphs from three complementary perspectives. It then employs a text--structure fusion mechanism, enabling the interplay between large language model (LLM)-encoded semantics and flow texts with graph convolutional network (GCN)-encoded structure. Beyond mere detection, \textsc{FlowShield} further generates readable suspicious activity reports (SARs), offering investigators concise summaries and explainable red flags. To address the data scarcity in multi-chain detection, we construct and open-source \textit{BybitML}, the first public multi-chain laundering dataset. We evaluate \textsc{FlowShield} on \textit{BybitML} and two public laundering datasets and experimental results demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} achieves the best overall performance, with an average F1 score of 98.0\%. Further behavior and SAR analyses demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} can reveal diverse laundering strategies and produce readable reports for investigating complex multi-hop fund flows.

</details>


### [172] [Fair ASR: Re-Evaluating Black-Box Jailbreaks under Shared Target-Call Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17360)
*Zhida He,Xiaoyu Wen,Han Qi,Ziyuan Zhou,Peng Yu,Jiajia Li,Chaochao Lu,Qiaosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fair-ASR评估协议，以目标调用次数作为黑盒越狱攻击的统一比较轴，克服了现有仅用ASR和FLOPs评估的公平性问题；重新评估11种攻击后发现排名随预算显著变化，并据此设计出高效的ReCode攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击评估大多仅依赖ASR，忽略了其对攻击预算的依赖性，导致不同方法之间的比较不公平；而现有的计算感知评估将异构资源折算为FLOPs，对黑盒模型难以估计且无法反映资源特定约束。

Method: 提出Fair-ASR协议，以共享的目标调用预算B为基准，将目标调用次数作为可直接观测且与模型无关的比较轴，同时单独追踪攻击者调用次数以分析效率。基于此协议重新评估11种代表性攻击，并进一步提出ReCode——一种组合式、预算高效的攻击方法，将去敏重写与Fair-ASR识别的两种低成本原语相结合。

Result: 在Fair-ASR协议下，攻击排名在不同目标调用预算间发生显著变化；简单的随机扰动和手工模板在相同的目标访问权限下仍极具竞争力；没有一种LLM驱动的方法在目标和攻击者调用两方面都高效。ReCode在20次目标调用预算下对GPT-5实现85%的ASR，平均每次请求仅需7.19次攻击者调用。

Conclusion: Fair-ASR为黑盒越狱攻击提供了更公平、可比较的评估基础，揭示了现有评估指标的局限性和流行方法的效率缺陷；ReCode证明了基于公平评估洞察设计的组合式攻击可以在有限预算下实现高成功率和双维度的调用效率。

Abstract: Reliable jailbreak evaluation is essential for assessing LLM safety, but most existing studies rely solely on attack success rate (ASR) without accounting for its dependence on attack budgets, resulting in unfair comparisons across methods. Existing compute-aware evaluations reduce heterogeneous resources into FLOPs, which is difficult to estimate for black-box models and fails to capture resource-specific constraints. To provide a comparable evaluation basis, we introduce Fair-ASR, an evaluation protocol for black-box jailbreak attacks under shared target-call budgets B, using target calls as a directly observable and method-agnostic comparison axis while tracking attacker calls separately for efficiency analysis. We re-evaluate 11 representative attacks under the Fair-ASR protocol and find that attack rankings change substantially across target-call budgets, simple stochastic perturbations and hand-crafted templates remain highly competitive under equal target access, and no evaluated LLM-driven method is efficient in both target and attacker calls. Motivated by this efficiency gap, we introduce ReCode, a compositional budget-efficient attack that combines desensitization rewriting with two effective low-cost primitives identified by Fair-ASR. Under a budget of 20 target calls, ReCode achieves 85% ASR on GPT-5 while requiring only 7.19 attacker calls per request on average, showing strong efficiency in both target and attacker calls.

</details>


### [173] [Trusted Workflow Relays:Cross-Tenant Email Abuse and Composable Red Team Initial-Access Primitives in Multi-Tenant Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17361)
*Priyank Nigam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"受信工作流中继"(trusted workflow relay)概念：云应用中认证用户可通过提供商托管邮件身份跨租户发送未经应用层授权的内容，类似经典SMTP开放中继但故障上移到应用层。基于三个已披露并修复的跨租户通知工作流漏洞，给出测试矩阵、MITRE ATT&CK映射，并提出租户绑定、类型化模板、对象级授权等缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云应用通过提供商运营的邮件身份发送通知以提升投递率，但这将"提供通知参数的参与者"与"发起消息的服务主体"分离，导致应用层授权无法约束谁可以触发提供商向谁发送什么内容。这个问题类似经典SMTP开放中继，但已上移到更高栈层，现有SPF/DKIM/DMARC等邮件认证机制无法解决。

Method: 对三个已负责任披露并修复的跨租户通知工作流进行案例研究：(1)后端请求绕过UI长度限制，原始HTML/CSS进入消息，攻击者可隐藏服务控制文本；(2)缺少收件人租户验证且攻击者可控subject和HTML字段；(3)审批应用存在弱访问控制、顺序对象标识符、缺少动作授权和不完整token验证。定义"受信工作流中继"概念，提出通知管线测试矩阵，映射到MITRE ATT&CK技术，并关联设备码钓鱼(RFC 8628)。

Result: 识别出三类跨租户通知漏洞，论证了应用级"发送授权谓词"为假时仍可能投递服务认证的邮件。建立测试矩阵用于评估通知管线，证明SPF/DKIM/DMARC无法建立应用级发送授权，将原语映射到无附件钓鱼的ATT&CK技术，并与设备码钓鱼建立关联。

Conclusion: 提出五类缓解控制：租户绑定、类型化模板、对象级授权、token受众验证和身份遥测。核心结论是：即使消息在邮件认证层被验证为来自可信服务，也不能推断应用级发送是被授权的，需要将授权检查嵌入通知管线本身。

Abstract: Cloud applications routinely send notifications through provider-operated mail identities, which improves deliverability but separates the actor who supplies notification parameters from the service principal that originates the message. In three responsibly disclosed and remediated cross-tenant notification workflows, an authenticated actor could reach recipients across tenant boundaries and, to varying degrees, control content that a trusted provider service delivered. In the first, backend requests bypassed a UI length limit, raw HTML and CSS survived into the delivered message, attacker links rendered, and CSS could hide service-controlled text; iframes and non-web URI schemes were rejected. The second combined missing recipient-tenant validation with attacker-controlled subject and HTML fields. The third, an approval application, added weak access control, sequential object identifiers, missing action authorization, and incomplete token validation, composing notification abuse with authorization failures.
  The pattern is analogous to a classical unauthenticated SMTP open relay, but the failure has moved up the stack: the actor is authenticated and the provider is the legitimate sender, yet application-layer authorization still fails to constrain who may cause it to send what to whom. We define a trusted workflow relay as a delivered, service-authentic message for which the application-level send-authorization predicate is false. We give a test matrix for notification pipelines, map the primitive to MITRE ATT&CK techniques for attachment-free phishing, and link it to device-code phishing (RFC 8628). SPF, DKIM, and DMARC can authenticate a message yet cannot establish that an application-level send was authorized. We conclude with controls for tenant binding, typed templates, object-level authorization, token audience validation, and identity telemetry.

</details>


### [174] [FESC: Remodeling Long-Context Private Inference with Encrypted State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17442)
*Yufan Zhu,Chao Jin,Khin Mi Mi Aung,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FESC是一个混合FHE-MPC系统，用于私有长上下文选择性SSM推理。它通过因子化扫描-收缩技术保持输入依赖转换紧凑，实现在单GPU上首次原生端到端执行L≥1,024的私有长文档推理。在L=2,048时，12层Mamba-base模型以77.3分钟完成推理，峰值内存32.7GB，并保持接近明文精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有私有推理系统针对Transformer注意力进行优化，但其二次方token-pair计算量随序列长度增长成为瓶颈。选择性SSM提供线性时间递归，但直接加密实现面临线性乘法深度、序列级状态驻留或密集FHE-MPC转换等问题。需要一个高效且隐私保护的长上下文推理方案。

Method: 提出FESC，一种混合FHE-MPC系统：(1) 因子化扫描-收缩（factorized scan-contract）机制，在转换边界保持输入依赖转换紧凑，无需密集展开即可组合，按需流式传输状态块，并在转换前收缩输出；(2) GPU优化的CKKS内核用于线性计算；(3) MPC协议实现SiLU、softplus、指数和RMSNorm等非线性操作；(4) 采用近似感知微调提升精度；(5) 在Mamba-2架构上实例化实现。

Result: FESC是首个在单GPU上原生端到端执行L≥1,024的私有长文档推理系统。在L=2,048时，12层Mamba-base模型在单张A100 GPU上以77.3分钟完成推理，峰值内存占用32.7GB，在长文档任务上保持接近明文的精度。

Conclusion: FESC通过因子化扫描-收缩方法解决了私有SSM推理中的关键瓶颈，证明了混合FHE-MPC系统能够高效支持长上下文私有推理，为大规模私有序列建模提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Processing long, sensitive documents with machine-learning models requires efficient, privacy-preserving long-context inference. Prior private inference systems optimize or distribute encrypted Transformer attention, but its quadratic token-pair work remains the bottleneck as sequence length grows. Selective state-space models (SSMs) offer linear-time recurrence, yet direct encrypted implementation incurs linear multiplicative depth, sequence-wide state residency, or dense FHE-MPC conversion. We present Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract (FESC), a hybrid FHE-MPC system for private long-context selective SSM inference. Its factorized scan-contract keeps input-dependent transitions compact across conversion boundaries, composes them without dense expansion, streams state chunks on demand, and contracts outputs before conversion. We demonstrate interface compatibility of the scan-contract implementation across invariant and selective SSM architectures. For our Mamba-2 instantiation, we design GPU-optimized CKKS kernels for linear computations, MPC protocols for SiLU, softplus, exponential, and RMSNorm, with approximation-aware fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FESC is the first private long-document inference system to complete native end-to-end execution at $L \geq 1{,}024$ on a single GPU. At $L = 2{,}048$, a 12-layer Mamba-base model completes inference in 77.3 minutes on one A100 GPU with a peak memory footprint of 32.7 GB, while maintaining near-plaintext accuracy on the evaluated long-document tasks.

</details>


### [175] [Decomposition Attacks Across Unlinkable Identities: Limits of Stateful Defenses for LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17445)
*Bowen Sun,Zhengyue Zhao,Xiaogeng Liu,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在攻击者可不受限制地创建新身份、在各个地方组合答案的设定下，分解攻击是否仍可被阻止。核心发现是：如果没有可靠的请求分组信号、身份关联或答案使用控制机制，防御分解攻击在实用层面是不可行的。实验表明，即使拥有精确请求-操作映射的特权策略，也无法在可接受的误拒预算内阻止分解攻击；只有引入身份关联、新身份成本或答案使用控制等额外机制才能实现有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型服务大多采用无状态防御（仅判断当前请求），而分解攻击通过将有害任务拆分为单独无害的请求来绕过此类防御。虽然理论上状态监控器可通过分组请求来防御，但攻击者可以使用不可关联的身份并在外部组合答案，导致缺乏可靠的分组信号。作者旨在回答：在这种无分组信号的设定下，分解攻击是否仍能被阻止。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与大规模实验相结合的方法。理论上，作者针对无重试的固定攻击策略证明了可实现的安全性与效用权衡完全取决于良性请求的分组方式：持久且可识别的分组允许有效防御，而全新且不可区分的分组则不行；当攻击者可重试并从Allow/Block决策中学习时，这一有效工作点会消失。实验上，作者在91个可执行任务和11,393个能力匹配的良性请求上测试了十种防御策略（包括一种具有精确请求到操作映射的特权策略），并设置了1%的良性请求拒绝上限和0.5%的背景流量拒绝上限。

Result: 实验结果显示，在设定的预算约束下，所有十种测试策略要么无法阻止攻击，要么超出预算。在防御未见过的任务族上，攻击成功率在一次尝试后至少为99%，两次尝试后达到100%。这证明在没有额外分组证据或机制的情况下，现有策略无法在实用场景下防御分解攻击。

Conclusion: 有效的分解攻击防御需要额外的证据或与分组相关的机制，包括：可靠的身份关联、为新身份引入成本、或对答案使用的控制。仅依靠状态监控器或精确的请求-操作映射是不够的。

Abstract: Most large language model services use stateless defenses, which judge only the current request, to refuse harmful tasks. Decomposition attacks exploit this limitation by splitting a harmful task into individually permissible requests and combining their answers. Defending against them therefore requires a stateful monitor that considers requests together. If it can group all requests for one attacker task, it can stop the attack. However, attackers can use unlinkable identities and combine answers elsewhere, leaving no reliable grouping signal. We ask whether decomposition attacks can still be stopped under this setting. For a fixed attack strategy without retries, we prove that the achievable security and utility tradeoff depends entirely on how benign requests for the same capabilities are grouped. Persistent, recognizable groups permit a useful defense; fresh, indistinguishable groups do not. When attackers can retry and learn from Allow/Block decisions, this useful operating point disappears: the feedback reveals what passes but not whether a block was correct. Experiments on 91 executable tasks and 11,393 capability-matched benign requests support these results. Under a 1% denial cap for these requests and a 0.5% cap for unrelated background traffic, all ten tested policies, including one privileged policy with an exact request-to-operation map, either fail to stop attacks or exceed the budget. On defense-unseen task families, attack success is at least 99% after one attempt and 100% after two. Effective defenses therefore require additional evidence or mechanisms tied to grouping, such as reliable identity linkage, costs for fresh identities, or control over answer use.

</details>


### [176] [KeyPooling: Measuring Where LLM API Relay Paths Collapse Prompt Cache Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17485)
*Bowen Sun,Yixi Cai,Xiaogeng Liu,Zhengyue Zhao,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出KeyPooling测量方法，系统追踪LLM API中继中用户身份如何从缓存查找和写入中透传，发现五个主流开源网关在默认配置下均未将客户绑定到上游凭据，导致共享凭据下跨客户缓存读取漏洞。在OpenRouter生产环境中，80.5%的token量受影响，12/28个标签存在跨账户读取。作者提出防御契约：每个客户必须进入供应商强制的域，或基于认证身份的命名空间必须在每次最终缓存查找和写入中存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在选定端点展示了缓存共享问题，但未系统地识别究竟是哪个凭据、池、适配器或嵌套跳转控制最终的缓存身份。中继客户被映射到同一缓存身份时可互相观察缓存状态，这是一个跨客户数据泄露的严重安全问题。

Method: 提出KeyPooling测量方法：(1) 通过缓存查找和写入追踪客户身份；(2) 验证运行时变换，逐次测试一个预测的身份组件。对五个开源网关（连接OpenAI和Anthropic）进行测试，系统检查主体/命名空间拆分、池关联、适配器及嵌套中继对比来定位控制变换。

Result: 五个开源网关均未在默认情况下将客户绑定到上游凭据；共享凭据下全部五个网关对两家供应商都暴露了跨客户缓存读取。在OpenRouter周度框架中，测试覆盖80.5%的合格token量，28个标签中12个（占33.7%的量）存在跨账户读取。一条生产路由上，受控程序恢复了8个连续目标位置。缓存放粒度、路由、速率限制、归因和预算仅构成逐token恢复的条件而非安全控制。防御方案在可复用公共前缀后放置拆分，保留大部分模型复用，仅增加1.7-2.5%成本。

Conclusion: LLM API中继的缓存共享是一个普遍且真实的安全漏洞，防御的关键在于确保每个客户进入供应商强制的域，或基于认证身份的命名空间必须在最终缓存查找和写入中保留。低成本防御方案可行，但仍需供应商和网关层面的协同修复。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) API relays authenticate customers separately but often forward requests through shared provider credentials. Providers scope prompt caches to upstream principals and namespaces, so relay customers mapped to one cache identity can observe each other's cache state. Prior work showed cache sharing at selected endpoints but did not identify which credential, pool, adapter, or nested hop controls the finalidentity. We present KeyPooling, a measurement method that traces customer identity through cache lookup and write, verifies runtime transformations, and tests one predicted identity component at a time. Across five open-source gateways connected to OpenAI and Anthropic, none bound customers to upstream credentials by default; under a shared credential, all five exposed cross-customer cache reads for both providers. Principal and namespace splits, pool associations, and adapter and nested-relay contrasts localized the controlling transformations. In an outcome-independent weekly OpenRouter frame, tests covered 80.5% of eligible token volume and found cross-account reads for 12 of 28 labels carrying 33.7% of volume. On one production route, a controlled procedure recovered eight consecutive target positions without target access. Broader tests identify cache granularity, routing, rate limits, attribution, and budget as conditions for token-by-token recovery, not security controls. We derive a defense contract: every customer must enter a provider-enforced domain, or a namespace derived from authenticated identity must survive every final cache lookup and write. Placing this split after reusable public prefixes preserved most modeled reuse at a 1.7-2.5% cost increase.

</details>


### [177] [Cross-Domain Joint DDoS Detection in Multi-Controller SDN via Confidence-Based Entropy Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17507)
*Zhaoyang Zhang,Shen Wang,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对多控制器SDN中DDoS检测的聚合偏差问题提出了一种跨域置信度融合框架。该框架利用轻量级边缘侧消息校准聚合控制器的决策，无需共享原始流量数据，将聚合控制器误报率从8.87%降至1.96%，F1分数从89.04%提升至96.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多控制器SDN环境下，DDoS攻击呈现"分散源、集中目标"的跨域模式。基于熵的DDoS检测器在单控制器场景下有效，但直接应用于多控制器SDN时存在被忽视的异常：攻击后过渡阶段聚合控制器持续产生过多误报，而边缘控制器已恢复正常。作者将此归因于OpenFlow统计延迟与无约束动态阈值漂移的耦合效应。

Method: 提出一个跨域置信度融合框架，利用轻量级边缘侧消息来校准聚合控制器的检测决策，无需共享原始流量数据。该框架具有非侵入性、通信高效和可增量部署的特点。实验采用三控制器线性Mininet测试平台，包含24台主机，进行10次重复实验。

Result: 该框架在保持边缘控制器性能不变的同时，将聚合控制器误报率从8.87%降低至1.96%，F1分数从89.04%提升至96.89%。

Conclusion: 跨域置信度融合是解决多控制器SDN中聚合偏差的有效手段，它通过轻量级信息交换实现了检测性能的大幅提升，同时保证了通信效率和隐私安全性。

Abstract: In multi-controller Software-Defined Networking (SDN), Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks exhibit a "dispersed source, concentrated target" pattern across domains, i.e., attack traffic originates from multiple edge-controller domains but converges on a victim in a single aggregation controller domain. While entropy-based DDoS detectors are effective in single-controller settings, their direct application in multi-controller SDN reveals a previously overlooked anomaly. Through systematic experiments, we identify an aggregation bias: during the post-attack transition phase, the aggregation controller continues to generate excessive false positives, while edge controllers have already returned to normal. We attribute this phenomenon to the coupled effects of OpenFlow statistics lag and unconstrained dynamic-threshold drift. To address this issue, we propose a cross-domain confidence-fusion framework that leverages lightweight edge-side messages to calibrate aggregation-controller decisions without sharing raw traffic data. The framework is non-intrusive, communication-efficient, and incrementally deployable. Experiments on a three-controller linear Mininet testbed with 24 hosts over 10 runs show that the method preserves edge-controller performance while reducing the aggregation false positive rate from 8.87% to 1.96% and increasing the F1 score from 89.04% to 96.89%.

</details>


### [178] [CryptDough: A Unified Analytics Engine for Secure Multiparty Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17529)
*Muhammad Faisal,Alessandra Lanz,Sam Buxbaum,Adam Godel,Vasiliki Kalavri,Mayank Varia,John Liagouris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CryptDough是一个统一的MPC（安全多方计算）分析引擎，支持多种信任模型和工作负载类型，通过分层系统设计和虚拟向量抽象，提供跨领域（关系型、时间序列、机器学习推理）的安全分析能力，性能优于现有系统2倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPC系统通常只支持单一威胁模型或单一工作负载类型，缺乏统一的系统运行时来支持跨领域分析，导致用户需要为不同场景使用不同系统。

Method: (i) 采用分层系统设计，通过逐步降低抽象层次实现模块化和可扩展性；(ii) 引入虚拟向量概念，允许用户以单线程方式编写代码，将通信、并行化和内存管理的复杂性下推到执行引擎。

Result: CryptDough泛化了现有最先进MPC系统的功能，在所支持的分析任务上与它们保持竞争力，通常在性能上优于它们超过2倍。

Conclusion: 统一的多方计算引擎可以在同一运行时内支持多种信任模型和工作负载，同时保持甚至超越专用系统的性能表现，证明了通用化并不同时意味着性能妥协。

Abstract: We present CryptDough, a unified analytics engine for secure multiparty computation (MPC). CryptDough enables multiple distrusting parties to jointly execute a data analysis pipeline on their private inputs and learn nothing beyond the result (e.g., aggregate statistics). Unlike existing MPC solutions that support a single threat model or workload type, CryptDough provides built-in support for cross-domain analytics (relational, time series, ML inference) under various threat models, all within the same system runtime.
  CryptDough contributes (i) a hierarchical system design that facilitates modularity and extensibility through progressive lowering of abstractions, and (ii) the concept of virtual vectors that enable users to write single-threaded code across all layers of the software stack, while pushing the complexity of communication, parallelization, and memory management down to the execution engine. We show that CryptDough generalizes the functionality of state-of-the-art MPC systems and remains competitive on the analytics they support, often outperforming them by more than $2\times$.

</details>


### [179] [SoK: Cross-Chain Transaction Identification and Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17532)
*Hang Zheng,Qishuang Fu,Joseph Liu,Qin Wang,Weiqing Wang,Tsz Hon Yuen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对跨链交易识别与匹配进行了系统化知识综述（SoK），将存款/提款识别方法分为四类、交易匹配方法分为三种机制，评估了现有数据集与工件的可获得性，并提出了四个开放挑战，旨在推动可审计、可复现且可执行的跨链分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链桥、即时加密货币交易所和中心化跨账本平台在多链生态中频繁转移资产，但这些系统屡屡成为高价值攻击目标及跨链洗钱渠道。由于单一账本无法记录完整的跨链转移，其证据分散在源链、目标链和链下系统中，且各系统的证据可用性与可靠性差异巨大，导致跨链交易分析远难于单链交易，亟需系统化的知识梳理。

Method: 本文采用系统化知识（SoK）的研究方法，首先将存款与提款的识别方法分类为四种方法，将交易匹配方法分类为三种机制：确定性标识符匹配、字段约束启发式以及模型辅助匹配；随后分析匹配对在下游攻击检测和资金追踪中的支撑作用；进一步评估现有数据集与工件（artifact）的可获得性，归纳出三种工件失效模式；最后提出四个开放挑战。

Result: 研究发现各类识别与匹配方法的适用性和报告性能主要由底层系统所暴露的证据决定。在数据集与工件评估方面，发现现存工件中可获取者不足一半，并归纳出三种工件失效模式。

Conclusion: 本文系统梳理了跨链交易识别与匹配的研究现状，揭示了方法适用性与系统证据暴露情况之间的依赖关系，指出了数据工件可获得性差的现实问题，并提出了四个开放挑战，为未来跨链分析的可审计性、可复现性和可操作性指明了研究方向。

Abstract: Cross-chain bridges, instant cryptocurrency exchanges, and centralized cross-ledger platforms move assets across an increasingly multi-chain ecosystem. However, these systems have repeatedly become targets of high-value attacks and channels for cross-chain money laundering. Cross-chain transactions are substantially harder to analyze than single-chain transactions: no single ledger records an entire cross-chain transfer, its evidence is scattered across the source chain, the destination chain, and off-chain systems, and the availability and reliability of that evidence vary widely across systems. In this paper, we present a systematization of knowledge (SoK) on cross-chain transaction identification and matching. First, we classify deposit and withdrawal identification methods into four approaches and transaction matching methods into three mechanisms: deterministic identifier matching, field-constraint heuristics, and model-assisted matching. We find that their applicability and reported performance are shaped mainly by the evidence the underlying system exposes, and we further examine how matched pairs support downstream attack detection and fund tracing. Second, we assess the availability of existing datasets and artifacts, finding that fewer than half remain obtainable, and distill three artifact failure modes. Finally, we outline four open challenges toward auditable, reproducible, and actionable cross-chain analysis.

</details>


### [180] [TENET: Telegram Mini App (in)security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17538)
*Andrea Ciccotelli,Federico Zappone,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Telegram Mini Apps生态系统中客户端明文存储认证材料（如会话令牌和钱包助记词）的安全风险。作者开发了专用审计工具TENET，对61个Mini Apps进行分层抽样筛查，发现37个可分析应用中有30个存在安全漏洞。即使Telegram官方Wallet也存在可能导致账户完全被攻陷的严重漏洞。负责任披露后，Telegram实施了两种新的安全存储API，官方Wallet已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps将Web应用直接集成到客户端中，但其生态系统具有独特的危险组合：缺乏平台级安全审查、无存储访问限制、用户群体涉及加密货币资产、WebView环境防护弱于独立浏览器。这种环境使客户端明文存储认证材料的问题比以往Web应用场景更为严重，可能导致未授权访问、身份冒充和金融损失。

Method: 作者提出了TENET审计工具，其设计（模式选择、熵阈值、字符集验证）基于目标秘密的结构属性，并用地面真值数据集进行实证验证。研究者采用基于流行度的分层、按流行度加权的抽样策略筛选了61个Mini Apps，最终分析了37个符合处理标准的应用。

Result: 在被分析的37个应用中，30个存在安全漏洞，分为三个严重等级：明文存储、可恢复加密和可重放令牌。Telegram官方Wallet被发现存在可能导致账户完全被攻陷的严重漏洞。负责任披露后，Telegram实施了两种新的安全存储API；事后验证确认官方Wallet不再以明文暴露恢复助记词。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态系统中存在广泛且严重的客户端存储安全隐患。作者提出了针对Telegram平台开发者和第三方Mini App创建者的缓解措施和最佳实践，以改善该生态系统的安全性。

Abstract: Telegram, with over 450 million daily active users, has introduced Mini Apps---web-based applications running directly within its client. However, this integration introduces notable security risks. As we demonstrate, many Mini Apps store authentication materials---such as session tokens and wallet mnemonic phrases---in plaintext on client devices, exposing users to unauthorized access, impersonation, and financial exploitation. While insecure client-side storage is a known risk in web applications, the Telegram Mini App ecosystem presents a uniquely dangerous combination of factors absent from prior work: no platform-level security review, no storage access restrictions, a financially motivated user base handling live cryptocurrency assets, and a WebView environment that offers weaker protections than standalone browsers. To investigate this threat, we present TENET, a purpose-built auditing tool whose design decisions---pattern selection, entropy thresholds, and charset validation---are grounded in the structural properties of the secrets targeted and empirically validated against a ground-truth dataset. We screened 61 Mini Apps using a stratified, popularity-weighted sampling strategy based on popularity. Of the 37 applications that met our processing criteria and were analyzed, 30 exhibited security flaws, which we classify into three severity tiers: plaintext storage, recoverable encryption, and replayable tokens. Notably, even Telegram's official Wallet exhibits a severe vulnerability that may lead to full account compromise. Following our responsible disclosure, Telegram implemented two new secure-storage APIs, and our post-remediation verification confirmed that its official Wallet no longer exposes the recovery mnemonic in plaintext. Finally, we propose mitigation measures and best practices for both Telegram platform developers and third-party Mini App creators.

</details>


### [181] [Software Defined Networks Key Relay for Large-Scale Quantum Key Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17539)
*Stephan Laschet,Gergely Lendvay,Thomas Lorünser,Paul James,Luca Torresetti,Alessandro Colombo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于软件定义网络（SDN）的量子密钥分发网络（QKDN）编排方案，提出了基于Dijkstra和最大-最小容量算法的多路径选择算法，并设计了支持负载均衡、查询批处理、多路径选择及群组密钥等关键功能；此外，提出了一种用于多域场景下的中继路径选择的无知多方协议，以保护各提供商敏感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子密钥分发网络的规模不断扩展，如何高效编排和管理大规模QKDN成为关键挑战。现有的集中式管理方案缺乏灵活性和可扩展性，需要一种基于SDN的架构来聚合网络KPI并优化路径选择，同时保障多域场景下各提供商的隐私。

Method: 基于ETSI和ITU规范构建SDN控制器架构，由控制器聚合全网技术KPI并根据这些指标选择最优路径。提出了多种基于Dijkstra和最大-最小容量算法的路径选择算法，内置负载均衡机制；同时提出了一种面向多域中继路径选择的无知多方（oblivious multi-party）协议。

Result: 所提出的路径选择算法在仿真环境中进行了测试，并讨论了其性能与权衡；同时，无知多方协议使得多域场景下的中继路径选择无需披露提供商敏感信息。整体方案在可扩展性、弹性和互操作性方面均有增强。

Conclusion: 该工作通过SDN控制QKDN，实现了基于KPI感知的智能路径选择和负载均衡，并通过无知多方协议保障了多域场景下的隐私安全，为量子安全网络基础设施的规模化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This work addresses the orchestration of large-scale Quantum Key Distribution Networks (QKDNs) using Software Defined Networking (SDN). Building on ETSI and ITU specifications, common best practices and architectures are outlined. The main task of the SDN Controller is to aggregate technical key performance indicators (KPI) from the network and, based on these, select the optimal path. Multiple path selection algorithms, based on Dijkstra or a maximum-minimum capacity algorithm, with built-in load balancing are presented. The algorithms were tested in simulations and their performances, and tradeoffs, are discussed. Additional critical aspects related to SDN controlled QKDNs are discussed, such as query batching, multi-path selection and group key capabilities. An oblivious multi-party protocol is proposed for relay path selection in a multi-domain scenario, so providers don't have to disclose sensitive information about their QKDN. These contributions aim to enhance scalability, resilience and interoperability in quantum-secure network infrastructures.

</details>


### [182] [Reflex-Guard: A Low-Latency Guardrail for LLM Prompt Safety Using Dense Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17556)
*Istiaque Ahmed,Afia Anjum Borsha,Ranat Das Prangon,Abu-fuad Ahmad,Thi Hong Tran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflex-Guard，一个本地运行的轻量级AI安全护栏系统，通过jailbreak感知预处理、紧凑句向量嵌入和七个快速二分类器，在37.6ms端到端延迟下实现95.9%有害提示召回率，显著优于Llama Guard 2（255ms）和SafeDecoding（723ms）等现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏方法（如LLM-as-a-judge和基于云的安全API）虽能检测不安全内容，但每次请求引入250-900ms延迟，超出实时应用100ms的响应需求；同时通过外部审核端点路由用户提示引发严重的数据隐私问题。

Method: Reflex-Guard采用三种协同组件：1) jailbreak感知的预处理模块；2) 紧凑的sentence-transformer嵌入；3) 七个快速二分类器。在30,568个样本（来自五个互补数据源）的策略性平衡数据集上进行系统评估。

Result: Reflex-Guard在37.6ms延迟下达到95.9%有害提示召回率，检测100%的GCG后缀攻击和Base64编码提示（默认阈值）；DrAttack结构化提示需将阈值降至0.03；Reflex Efficiency Score（RES）达到16.79，显著优于Llama Guard 2（11.90）和SafeDecoding（9.80）。不同攻击类型在嵌入概率空间中占据不同区域。

Conclusion: Reflex-Guard在保持高精度过滤能力的同时大幅降低延迟并保护数据隐私，为实时AI应用提供了实用的安全解决方案；分析表明攻击类型在嵌入空间中具有可区分的分布特征，并为实际部署提供了指导建议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) in real-world applications often face the risks of specially crafted prompts designed to bypass the safety controls. Existing guardrail methods, such as LLM-as-a-judge and cloud-based safety APIs are able to detect unsafe content. However, they often add a delay of about 250-900 ms to each request. This delay is too high for real-time applications, when the system usually needs to respond in less than 100 ms. Furthermore, routing user prompts through external moderation endpoints raises significant data privacy concerns. This paper introduces Reflex-Guard, a lightweight guardrail that runs locally. It uses jailbreak-aware preprocessing, compact sentence-transformer embeddings, and seven fast binary classifiers. Together, these components enable high-accuracy prompt safety filtering with much lower latency than existing solutions. Through systematic evaluation on a strategically balanced dataset of 30,568 samples drawn from five complementary sources, we demonstrate that Reflex-Guard achieves 95.9% recall on harmful prompts at 37.6 ms end-to-end latency. It is faster than existing baselines, including Llama Guard 2 at 255 ms and SafeDecoding at 723 ms. It can detect 100% of GCG suffix attacks and Base64-encoded prompts using the default threshold. However, DrAttack structured prompts required lowering the threshold to 0.03 for optimal detection, as they produced a distinct probability distribution. Reflex-Guard achieves Reflex Efficiency Score (RES) scores up to 16.79, significantly outperforming Llama Guard 2 (11.90) and SafeDecoding (9.80). This analysis offers practical deployment advice and shows that different attack types occupy distinct regions in the embedding probability space.

</details>


### [183] [HarnessRisk: A Lifecycle-Oriented Benchmark for Agent Harness Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17597)
*Yajing Bai,Jinhao Duan,Jie Peng,Xianfeng Wu,Sijia Liu,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessRisk，一个面向Agent外壳（harness）安全性的全生命周期基准，涵盖六大操作阶段共128个沙箱化测试用例，评估了三种外壳、六种大语言模型和14种配置组合下的安全性，发现Harness Configuration阶段最为脆弱，且显式的风险识别并不总能转化为安全行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准多针对单一攻击机制或有限的运行设置，难以系统比较不同外壳职责（工具管理、扩展、持久状态、权限、外部操作）下安全故障如何涌现，缺乏跨外壳、跨模型、可对比的全面评估框架。

Method: 构建生命周期导向的HarnessRisk基准，将外壳安全划分为六个操作阶段：外壳配置、能力扩展、运行操作、状态持久化、行动控制、事件恢复；设计了128个沙箱化测试用例，每个用例将一个良性的用户目标与嵌入在不可信工作流工件中的对抗性指令配对；使用效用、攻击成功率、持久性和检测率四个指标评估系统轨迹。

Result: 在3种外壳、6种大语言模型、14种模型与外壳配置下，攻击成功率为12.6%-80.9%，效用保持在75.0%-97.6%。Harness Configuration是三种外壳中最脆弱的阶段，攻击可通过在已授权工作流中篡改安全敏感参数而成功；部分配置在超过90%的运行中检测到风险，但同时仍保持较高攻击成功率，表明显式风险识别不能可靠地导向安全行动。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，Agent安全评估需要在多种外壳职责层面以及部署的模型和外壳配置级别进行，不能仅依赖风险识别；应针对特定脆弱阶段（尤其是外壳配置阶段）加强防护。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed through agent harnesses that manage tools, extensions, persistent state, permissions, and external actions. Existing safety benchmarks mainly target individual attack mechanisms or a limited subset of operational settings, making it difficult to compare how safety failures emerge across different harness responsibilities. We present HarnessRisk, a lifecycle oriented benchmark that organizes agent harness safety into six operational phases including Harness Configuration, Capability Extension, Runtime Operation, State Persistence, Action Control, and Incident Recovery. HarnessRisk contains 128 sandboxed cases, each pairing a benign user objective with an adversarial instruction embedded in an untrusted workflow artifact. We evaluate each trajectory using Utility, Attack Success Rate, Persistence, and Detection. Across three harnesses, six language models, and 14 model and harness configurations, attack success ranges from 12.6% to 80.9%, while Utility remains between 75.0% and 97.6%. Harness Configuration is the most vulnerable phase across all three harnesses, showing that attacks can succeed by altering security sensitive parameters within otherwise authorized workflows. We also find that explicit risk recognition does not reliably lead to safe action, as some configurations detect risks in more than 90% of runs while retaining substantial attack success. These results highlight the need to evaluate agent safety across multiple harness responsibilities and at the level of the deployed model and harness configuration.

</details>


### [184] [An Emulation Anchored Digital Twin Testbed for Cyberattack and Defense Analysis in Hospital IT OT Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17650)
*Prashant Rawat,Ravi Kumar Bairagi,Arunima,Geeta Yadav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个集成的医院IT和OT网络安全测试平台，并结合数字孪生用于监控、实验和防御机制验证。该测试平台仿真医院关键基础设施（EHR系统、SCADA基础设施、分段IT/OT/DMZ网络），支持受控网络攻击执行、软件补丁评估和基于强化学习的防御代理训练。实验表明计算开销极低（容器平均CPU利用率低于0.4%），OpenPLC Modbus TCP操作中位时延为0.901ms，并能捕获从DMZ传播到IT和PLC网络的多阶段SSH攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医院日益依赖IT与OT融合的基础设施支持关键医疗服务，但这种融合扩大了网络安全攻击面，且在真实系统上难以安全验证防御机制。现有测试平台往往局限于孤立的IT或OT环境，无法捕获真实的跨域医疗交互，因此需要构建一个既能仿真IT/OT交叉场景又能安全进行防御机制实验的测试环境。

Method: 构建了一个医院IT/OT网络安全测试平台，仿真中央服务器、EHR系统、基于SCADA的基础设施以及分段IT、OT和DMZ网络。测试平台支持受控网络攻击执行、软件补丁评估和基于强化学习的防御代理训练。该测试平台进一步扩展为数字孪生，利用日志和网络统计信息建模环境实时状态，并通过命令执行和容器生命周期编排实现双向交互。采用Modbus/TCP和FHIR/HL7协议实现医疗与工业组件间的真实通信。通过实验评估计算开销、通信时延及攻击捕获能力。

Result: 实验结果显示计算开销极低，平均归一化CPU利用率低于每个容器0.4%，大多数轻量级服务低于0.01%。OpenPLC Modbus TCP操作的中位往返时延为0.901ms。测试平台成功捕获了从DMZ传播到IT和PLC网络的多阶段SSH攻击，验证了其作为医院数字孪生基础的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为扩展仿真环境以实现硬件支持的医院数字孪生提供了坚实基础，弥合了IT/OT隔离测试与真实医院跨域安全验证之间的差距。

Abstract: Modern hospitals increasingly rely on integrated Information Technology (IT) and Operational Technology (OT) infrastructures to support critical healthcare services. However, this convergence expands the cybersecurity attack surface and makes safe validation of defensive mechanisms difficult on live systems. Existing testbeds often focus on isolated IT or OT environments and do not capture realistic cross-domain healthcare interactions. This work presents a hospital IT and OT cybersecurity testbed coupled with a digital twin for monitoring, experimentation, and validation of countermeasures. The testbed emulates a central server, Electronic Health Record (EHR) systems, SCADA-based infrastructure, and segmented IT, OT, and DMZ networks. It supports controlled cyberattack execution, software-patch evaluation, and training of RL-based defense agents. The testbed is further extended to a digital twin that models the real-time state of the environment using log and network statistics and enables bidirectional interaction through command execution and container lifecycle orchestration. Modbus/TCP and FHIR/HL7 support realistic communication across healthcare and industrial components. Experimental evaluation shows low computation overhead, with average normalized CPU utilization below 0.4 % per container and most lightweight services operating below 0.01%. OpenPLC Modbus TCP operations achieve a median round-trip latency of 0.901 ms. The testbed also captures a multi-stage SSH-based attack propagating from the DMZ to the IT and PLC networks. The framework provides a foundation for extending the emulated environment toward a hardware-enabled hospital digital twin.

</details>


### [185] [MobileWorldSafety: Benchmarking GUI Agent Safety Against Environmental Injection Attacks in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17659)
*Sujin Chen,Lijun Li,Tianyi Du,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MobileWorldSafety基准，包含142个基于真实Android应用的风险任务，用于系统评估GUI智能体在移动设备上受到环境注入攻击（如间接提示注入和对抗性指令）时的安全性。评估六种智能体发现攻击成功率高达40.4%-66.9%，表明当前GUI智能体在面对以普通移动上下文形式呈现的对抗内容时，普遍无法维持安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的GUI智能体正从研究原型走向早期实际部署，但它们处理大量不可信环境内容，极易受到环境注入攻击（包括间接提示注入和对抗性指令）。这种攻击可在用户毫不知情的情况下通过日常移动使用中的多种渠道操纵智能体行为。然而，现有基准未能覆盖日常用户场景，缺乏对移动设备上GUI智能体在环境注入攻击下的系统性评估。

Method: 构建MobileWorldSafety基准：包含142个基于真实Android应用的风险任务；每个任务定义程序可验证的风险指标（基于最终系统状态）；评估采用两阶段流水线——规则验证处理无歧义案例，LLM评审员裁决歧义案例，从而区分安全失败与能力失败，实现客观可复现的评估。

Result: 对六种智能体（包括通用智能体和专用GUI智能体）的评估显示，所有智能体均高度脆弱，攻击成功率范围为40.4%到66.9%。

Conclusion: 当前GUI智能体在面对以普通移动上下文呈现的对抗性内容时，往往无法维持安全对齐。MobileWorldSafety为量化这些脆弱性并推动鲁棒移动GUI智能体研究提供了基础。

Abstract: LLM-powered GUI agents that autonomously operate smartphones are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to early real-world deployment. However, because these agents routinely process untrusted environmental content, they are highly vulnerable to environmental injection attacks, which include indirect prompt injections and adversarial instructions. Such attacks can manipulate the behavior of agents without user awareness through diverse channels encountered in everyday mobile use. Despite these risks, existing benchmarks often fail to capture everyday user scenarios, lacking a systematic evaluation of GUI agents under environmental injection attacks on mobile devices. To address this gap, we introduce MobileWorldSafety, a benchmark of 142 risk tasks built on real Android applications. For each task, we define a programmatically verifiable risk indicator over the final system state and evaluate outcomes with a two-stage pipeline: rule-based verification handles unambiguous cases, while an LLM judge adjudicates ambiguous ones. This distinguishes safety failures from capability failures and enables objective and reproducible assessment. Evaluations on six agents, including both general agents and specialized GUI agents, demonstrate that all agents remain highly vulnerable, with attack success rates ranging from 40.4% to 66.9%. These findings indicate that current agents often fail to maintain safety alignment when adversarial content is presented as ordinary mobile context. MobileWorldSafety provides a foundation for quantifying these vulnerabilities and advancing research on robust mobile GUI agents.

</details>


### [186] [MemCatalyst: Amplifying Data Auditing on Vision-Language Models via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17722)
*Xukun Luan,Jinyan Liu,Yuhui Gong,Yuanguo Bi,Bing Hu,Xuesong Li,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemCatalyst，一种面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的数据投毒工具集，通过文本投毒（PT）和图像投毒（PI）两种策略，使VLMs在训练过程中过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的特定不一致性，从而增强成员推断（MI）数据审计的性能。实验表明在最小投毒预算下可显著提升MI AUC分数，且对模型性能影响可忽略，并具备跨VLM架构的黑盒迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLMs性能的优异表现主要依赖互联网海量训练数据，数据持有者（如艺术家）急需判断其数据是否在未经授权的情况下被用于模型训练，这涉及知识产权保护和个人隐私问题。数据审计（特别是成员推断）作为直接工具受到关注，但现有方法在VLM上的审计性能有限，需要一种能够放大数据审计效果的机制。

Method: MemCatalyst采用两种数据投毒策略：（1）文本投毒（PT）和（2）图像投毒（PI）。该方法在训练过程中迫使VLMs过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的特定不一致性，从而增强模型对成员信息的记忆，使其更易被审计。投毒样本的迁移性使其在黑盒（black-box）设置下对不同VLM架构同样有效。

Result: 基于五种最先进的数据审计方法和两个主流VLMs的广泛评估表明，MemCatalyst在极小的投毒样本预算下显著提升了成员推断的AUC分数，同时对模型性能的影响可忽略不计。投毒样本的跨架构迁移性在黑盒设置下得到验证。

Conclusion: MemCatalyst证明了通过数据投毒可以有效放大VLMs的数据审计可检测性，为数据持有者提供了一种实用的数据审计增强工具，同时保证了模型性能不受显著影响，并在黑盒场景中展示了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) achieve outstanding performance largely due to the amount of training data available on the internet. At the same time, data holders (e.g., artists) urgently need to determine whether their data has been used for model training without authorization, which concerns both intellectual property rights and personal privacy. Data auditing, particularly through membership inference (MI), has attracted attention as a direct tool. This work proposes MemCatalyst, a set of data poisoning tools, aiming to amplify the data auditing performance on VLMs. MemCatalyst employs two strategies: Poisoning Text (PT) and Poisoning Image (PI). MemCatalyst forces VLMs to over-learn specific inconsistencies between image features and textual semantics during training, thereby increasing their susceptibility to membership information auditing. Crucially, the transferability of poisoned samples across different VLM architectures is demonstrated to be effective in the black-box setting. Extensive evaluations using five state-of-the-art data audits on two prominent VLMs demonstrate that MemCatalyst markedly enhances MI AUC scores with a minimal budget of poisoned samples, while maintaining a negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [187] [BullsEye: Directed Firmware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17729)
*Lorenzo Ralli,Emilio Coppa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BULLSEYE，首个在基本块粒度上对闭源Linux固件进行定向灰盒模糊测试（DGF）的框架，通过静态与动态分析结合，针对第三方组件复用引入的已知漏洞进行精准探测，在40个漏洞点上全部复现目标，相比基线工具提速9.5x–72.5x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备的广泛部署扩大了攻击面，固件中第三方组件的复用常引入已知漏洞。然而现有定向灰盒模糊测试（DGF）在固件领域只能达到函数粒度，无法精确瞄准漏洞所在的基本块，导致检测效率和精度不足，亟需细粒度的固件定向漏洞检测方法。

Method: BULLSEYE采用静态分析与动态分析相结合的方法，在资源受限的固件环境中实现基本块级别距离度量的定向灰盒模糊测试，并提出新的DGF启发式策略以克服传统方法的局限。

Result: 在32个固件镜像的40个漏洞点上，BULLSEYE在预算内全部复现目标；相比最强的四种基线方法（包括AFLGO和WINDRANGER的重实现），时间暴露（TTE）几何均值降低9.5x–72.5x；相比最先进的固件重托管框架GREENHOUSE（其支持的18个目标），BULLSEYE平均提速9.8x。

Conclusion: BULLSEYE首次将定向灰盒模糊测试推进到固件基本块粒度，显著提升了第三方组件复用漏洞的检测效率与精度，为闭源固件安全分析提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Internet of Things (IoT) devices has expanded the digital attack surface, making firmware analysis critical for modern software security. A key security concern stems from the frequent reuse of third-party software components, a practice that often introduces known vulnerabilities into firmware images. Whether a given image actually exposes such a flaw is an open question, and public proof-of concept exploits make answering it urgent. Directed Greybox Fuzzing (DGF), a technique that enables targeted exploration of specific binary locations, offers a promising solution for detecting such vulnerabilities. However, DGF has reached firmware only at function granularity, too coarse to aim at the vulnerable block itself.
  This article presents BULLSEYE, the first DGF framework to schedule closed-source Linux-based firmware fuzzing by basic-block-level distance to user-specified targets. Our methodology combines static and dynamic analysis to enable DGF in the constrained firmware domain, focusing on vulnerabilities in reused third-party components. We introduce novel DGF heuristics that address limitations of traditional approaches. We compare BULLSEYE against four greybox-fuzzing baselines sharing its execution back-end, including reimplementations of AFLGO and WINDRANGER, and against GREENHOUSE, a state-of-the-art firmware re-hosting framework. On 40 vulnerability sites across 32 firmware images, BULLSEYE reproduces every target within budget, against 35 for the strongest of the four baselines, and reduces Time-to-Exposure by a geometric mean of 9.5x to 72.5x over them; against GREENHOUSE, on the 18 targets its pipeline supports, BULLSEYE is faster by a geometric mean of 9.8x.

</details>


### [188] [Achievement Unlocked: Let's Get Hacked! An Empirical Study of Cybercrime in the Video Gaming Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17754)
*Janine Schneider,Jan Kallenborn,Tim Hoffmann,Maximilian Eichhorn,Thorsten Holz,Bhupendra Acharya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过在线调查、深度访谈和公开帖子分析三种方法，对游戏玩家遭遇的网络犯罪进行了实证研究，识别出数字物品盗窃是主要攻击动机，并发现游戏内交易、投票、锦标赛等机制促使玩家进行高风险互动，同时指出客服流程漏洞被恶意利用以及部分安全机制仅提供虚假保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子游戏产业规模庞大且用户基础广泛，已成为复杂的社会经济生态系统，但游戏生态系统中的网络犯罪问题在以往研究中缺乏系统性的关注，作者希望填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用定性与观察性分析相结合的方法，包含三个数据来源：(1) 面向57名国际参与者的在线调查；(2) 对两名已确认受害者的半结构化访谈；(3) 对多个在线游戏平台上2,574条公开举报网络犯罪事件的帖子进行分析。

Result: 研究发现数字物品盗窃是攻击的主要动机；游戏相关功能和服务的固有机制（如物品交易、团队投票、锦标赛）会促使玩家参与高风险互动；攻击者会针对性地利用客服流程中的弱点；某些安全机制仅提供虚假的安全感。

Conclusion: 该研究系统性地刻画了游戏相关网络犯罪的攻击向量、动机和玩家应对方式，揭示了游戏生态系统中被忽视的安全风险点，为游戏平台设计者和安全研究者提供了针对性的防范建议方向。

Abstract: The ubiquity of the video game industry and its large user base have transformed video games into complex social and economic ecosystems. Unfortunately, this growing popularity also attracts cybercriminals who deliberately exploit game-specific mechanisms to target players. Despite this growing threat, cybercrime in the gaming ecosystem has received little systematic attention in prior research.
  In this work, we present an empirical study of cybercrime affecting video game players, combining qualitative and observational analyses to characterize gaming-related attacks, identify common attack vectors and motivations, and examine player responses. Our study is based on an online survey with 57 international participants, semi-structured interviews with two confirmed victims of gaming-related cybercrime, and an analysis of 2,574 publicly available posts reporting cybercrime incidents across multiple online gaming platforms. Our findings indicate that the theft of digital items is a prevalent motivation for attacks. We further observe that gaming-related features and services, such as item trading, team voting, and tournaments, create incentives for players to engage in risky interactions. In addition, our results highlight the targeted exploitation of weaknesses in customer support processes and reveal that certain security mechanisms provide only a false sense of protection.

</details>


### [189] [Efficient Fuzzy PSI under One-Sided Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17770)
*Xinpeng Yang,Meng Hao,Yanxue Jia,Chenkai Weng,Yonggang Wen,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在单侧假设下、仅依赖轻量级对称密钥原语即可实现一般$L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$距离模糊私密集交集（fuzzy PSI）的具体高效协议，同时支持发送方侧和接收方侧设置。通过将前缀字典树（prefix trie）技术非平凡地融入协议，首次将时间复杂度从先前的$O((\logδ)^d)$或$O(δ)$降至$O(\logδ)$，并针对稀疏输入分布提出了更高效的专用协议。实验表明该方案在计算和通信方面大幅优于现有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊私密集交集（fuzzy PSI）允许两方在输入集合之间识别近似匹配的元素（即距离不超过阈值δ）。尽管已有大量研究进展，但现有针对一般闵可夫斯基距离的构造要么依赖强双侧重几何分离假设，要么在单侧假设下产生巨大开销。因此，需要在更现实（单侧）假设下实现具体高效的模糊PSI协议。

Method: 本文仅使用对称密钥原语，在单侧假设下为一般$L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$距离构造模糊PSI协议，覆盖发送方侧和接收方侧两种设置。针对稀疏输入分布提出了更高效的变体协议。为降低随δ增长的开销，非平凡地引入了前缀字典树（prefix trie）技术，将复杂度降至$O(\logδ)$。

Result: 实验结果显示：相比van Baarsen和Pu（EUROCRYPT'24），计算提速最高达239倍，通信降低最高20倍；相比Dang等（CCS'25），计算提速最高达518倍，通信降低最高63倍；相比Bui等（ASIACRYPT'25），计算提速最高达4818倍，通信降低最高282倍。

Conclusion: 本文首次在单侧假设下实现了针对一般$L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$距离的具体高效的模糊PSI协议，并通过前缀字典树技术首次获得$O(\logδ)$的复杂度，大幅优于先前工作，为模糊PSI的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fuzzy private set intersection (PSI) enables two parties to identify approximately matching elements between their input sets, where two elements are considered a match if their distance is at most a threshold $δ$ under a given metric. Although substantial progress has been made, existing constructions for general Minkowski distances either rely on strong two-sided geometric separation assumptions or incur substantial overhead under one-sided assumptions.
  In this work, we present the first concretely efficient fuzzy PSI protocols for general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances under one-sided assumptions, relying solely on lightweight symmetric-key primitives. Our constructions support both sender-sided and receiver-sided settings. We further study sparser input distributions and present more efficient protocols tailored to this case. To reduce the overhead scaling with $δ$, we non-trivially incorporate prefix trie techniques into our protocols, achieving $O(\logδ)$ complexity for general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances for the first time, improving upon $O((\logδ)^d)$ or $O(δ)$ complexities of prior works.
  Extensive experiments, across a wide range of parameter settings, show that our protocols significantly outperform prior works under the same assumptions. Specifically, against van Baarsen and Pu (EUROCRYPT'24), our protocols achieve up to $239\times$ faster computation and up to $20\times$ lower communication. Against Dang et al. (CCS'25), we achieve up to $518\times$ speedup and up to $63\times$ communication reduction. Against Bui et al. (ASIACRYPT'25), we achieve up to $4818\times$ faster computation and up to $282\times$ lower communication.

</details>


### [190] [Diff-DDoS: Realistic Cyber-Physical Attack Synthesis and Robust Detection for 5G-Enabled CPS Using Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17796)
*Bilal Hussain,Xiao Tang,Qinghe Du,Tan Li,Muhammad Azhar,Danista Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff-DDoS，一种基于表格扩散模型的三阶段框架，用于在5G支持的网络物理系统中合成逼真的DDoS攻击样本并训练鲁棒的检测器。通过TabDDPM生成分布保持的攻击样本，并引入对抗性扩散训练（ADT）来强化检测器，在米兰CDR数据集上显著恢复了F1分数（最高达100%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的DDoS检测器在5G网络物理系统中面临数据稀缺和合成数据不真实的问题。基于手工构造攻击（固定缩放乘数）训练的检测器在遇到现实、分布保持的攻击样本时性能严重退化（F1分数下降47%至100%），亟需更真实的攻击合成方法和更鲁棒的检测训练策略。

Method: Diff-DDoS采用三阶段框架：阶段1在CDR生成的时空网格上训练基线CNN单元级检测器；阶段2使用TabDDPM（表格去噪扩散概率模型）在正常CDR聚合数据上训练，生成逼真攻击样本以暴露检测器漏洞；阶段3引入对抗性扩散训练（ADT），利用逆分类器引导生成困难但分布保持的样本，迭代训练直到检测器收敛。此外还采用基于验证的阈值校准来优化决策边界。

Result: 在米兰CDR数据集上（涵盖SMS洪泛、静默呼叫、互联网信令和混合场景），ResNet50配合ADT将F1分数恢复至79.62%（静默呼叫）、100%（互联网）和92.79%（混合场景）。经过基于验证的阈值校准后，ADT在SMS场景达到100% F1分数（对比CTGAN为47.3%），在静默呼叫场景与最强的基于梯度的对抗训练基线持平。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明表格扩散模型适用于数据稀缺的5G网络物理部署中入侵检测器的压力测试与加固。Diff-DDoS证明了生成分布保持的对抗样本能够有效提升检测器对自适应对抗攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based DDoS detectors for 5G-enabled cyber-physical systems face scarce labeled attack data and unrealistic synthetic substitutes, which limit robustness against adaptive adversaries. Detectors trained on hand-crafted attacks with fixed scaling multipliers degrade catastrophically (F1-score drops of about 47 percent to 100 percent, depending on scenario) when confronted with realistic, distribution-preserving samples. We propose Diff-DDoS, a three-phase framework for realistic attack synthesis and robust detection using tabular diffusion models. Phase 1 trains a baseline CNN cell-level detector on spatiotemporal grids from call detail records (CDRs). Phase 2 trains a tabular denoising diffusion probabilistic model (TabDDPM) on normal CDR aggregates to generate realistic attacks and expose detector vulnerabilities. Phase 3 introduces adversarial diffusion training (ADT), using inverse classifier guidance to generate hard yet distribution-preserving samples until the detector converges. On a Milano CDR dataset across SMS-flooding, silent-call, Internet-signaling, and blended scenarios, ResNet50 with ADT recovers F1-scores of 79.62 percent (silent-call), 100 percent (Internet), and 92.79 percent (blended). After validation-based threshold calibration, ADT reaches 100 percent SMS F1 versus 47.3 percent for CTGAN, and matches the strongest gradient-based adversarial-training baseline on silent-call. These results support tabular diffusion models for stress-testing and hardening intrusion detectors in data-scarce 5G cyber-physical deployments.

</details>


### [191] [The Model's Tell: Measuring Context-Leakage Attack Signals with Behavior Gauges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17829)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LeakGauge，一种在解码前通过附加后缀并在prefill阶段映射token概率来检测LLM上下文泄露攻击风险的方法。该方法不依赖隐藏状态提取，在11个LLM（含GLM-5.2和Kimi-K3）上对未见攻击达到0.944--0.996的AUROC，且对语言变化和语义性泄露保持稳健，并可通过叠加干预验证其与内部泄露方向相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越依赖外部上下文（系统提示或检索文档）来提升生成质量，但这引入了攻击面：对抗性输入可诱导模型泄露这些上下文。已有探测研究表明泄露相关信号存在于隐藏状态中，但提取隐藏状态在部署上带来额外挑战。因此需要寻找一种更易获取的、解码前的可观测信号来检测泄露行为。

Method: 提出LeakGauge框架：(1) 在输入后追加一个"测量后缀"（gauge suffix），用于诱导或捕捉泄露行为；(2) 将prefill阶段的token概率映射为攻击风险评分。关键发现是：直接使用机密内容的首个token作为测量基准不够稳健，而采用与内容无关（content-agnostic）的、将泄露行为言语化的后缀能产生更稳健的信号。同时通过激活干预（activation steering）验证风险评分对内部泄露相关方向的敏感性。

Result: 在11个LLM（包括GLM-5.2 753B和Kimi-K3 2.8T）上，LeakGauge对未见攻击达到0.944--0.996的AUROC；当内容语言变化或攻击从逐字泄露转为语义泄露时信号保持稳定；额外参数少于0.5K，额外延迟仅10.34ms。

Conclusion: LeakGauge证明LLM的上下文泄露行为在解码前留下的可观测痕迹可通过简单后缀与token概率映射来捕获，为实际部署提供了轻量、高效且跨模型稳健的泄露检测方案，并揭示了可观测信号与模型内部泄露表征之间的关联。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly rely on external contexts, such as pre-defined system prompts or retrieved documents, to improve generation quality. However, processing these contexts alongside user queries creates an attack surface: adversarial inputs can induce models to disclose them. Prior probing studies suggest that leakage-related signals emerge in hidden states, yet the need to extract these states poses additional deployment challenges. In this paper, we explore whether this internal signal leaves a more accessible ``tell'' before decoding. We propose LeakGauge, which probes this response by appending a suffix that gauges leakage behavior and mapping its prefill token probabilities to an attack-risk score. While a direct gauge uses the initial tokens of confidential content, we find that a content-agnostic one that verbalizes leakage behavior yields more robust signals. Across 11 LLMs, including GLM-5.2 (753B) and Kimi-K3 (2.8T), LeakGauge reaches an AUROC range of 0.944--0.996 on unseen attacks. The signal remains stable when the content changes language or the attack shifts from verbatim to semantic disclosure. By activation-steering interventions, we further show that the risk score is sensitive to an internal leakage-related direction, relating the observable signal to the model's internal representation. In addition, LeakGauge enables an input detector with fewer than 0.5K extra parameters and added latency of 10.34 ms. Code: \href{https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakGauge}.

</details>


### [192] [COMA: A Compositional Misleading Attack Class on Security-RAG, and a Causal Counterfactual Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17960)
*Chinmay Gondhalekar,Urjitkumar Patel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了安全运维中心（SOC）中面向分析师的RAG辅助安全助手所面临的一类新型攻击（COMA），其中每个对抗性文档在事实、指令、矛盾性和分布上都看似良性，但它们的组合会误导模型得出错误的漏洞评估与修复建议。作者实现了动作腐化（action-corruption）和判定翻转（verdict-flip）两种攻击方式，在所有测试模型中（包括前沿推理模型）均使动作腐化攻击成功，判定翻转则随模型能力增强而减少但从未消失。核心原理是：当消除歧义所需的事实必须被"推断"而非"读取"时，攻击即可成功。作者提出因果反事实防御（CCD）审计方法，通过留一法因果影响分析标记低信任文档，能无假阳性地定位攻击文档，并引入聚合变体对抗自适应分散影响攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心的分析师越来越多地依赖RAG辅助的安全助手（security copilots）进行漏洞评估和修复决策。现有攻击研究通常需要对抗性文档包含虚假信息、隐藏指令或矛盾内容，但作者发现即使每个检索到的文档都完全真实、无指令、无矛盾且分布正常，仅凭文档的"组合"即可误导模型做出危险的安全决策。这是一个此前未被系统识别的攻击类别，具有现实安全后果。

Method: 作者定义了一类称为COMA的攻击，其中所有对抗性文档均为事实正确、无指令、无矛盾且分布良性，攻击效果仅源于文档组合。他们通过两种机制实现：(1) 动作腐化（action-corruption）：将正确诊断的漏洞引导至劣质修复方案；(2) 判定翻转（verdict-flip）：通过不可判定可达性链使可利用性判定不稳定。防御方面，提出因果反事实防御（CCD），使用留一法（leave-one-out）计算每个检索文档的因果影响，标记影响集中于低信任文档的答案；并提出聚合变体（aggregate variant）应对通过分散影响躲避检测的自适应攻击者。

Result: 动作腐化攻击在全部五个测试模型（包括前沿推理模型）上、每个运行中均成功，覆盖两个合成领域和真实CVE（CVE-2021-33813）；判定翻转攻击以随机方式生效，随模型能力增强而减少但从未消失。CCD在四个良性多文档对照组上无假阳性，能准确定位攻击者控制的文档；聚合变体可捕获自适应影响力分散攻击者。作者发布了攻击种子和CCD参考实现。

Conclusion: RAG安全助手即使在检索文档全部真实且无指令的情况下，也可能被文档组合方式所误导，造成危险的安全决策。攻击成功的统一原理是"消除歧义的事实必须被推断而非读取"。CCD提供了一种基于因果影响分析的可行防御方案，能够定位攻击控制的文档。这一发现揭示了当前RAG安全系统的一个深层漏洞，需要从因果推理角度重新审视文档可信度评估。

Abstract: Every document a security copilot retrieves can be true, instruction-free, and non-contradictory --- and the copilot can still be driven to assess a critical, exploitable vulnerability correctly and then recommend a remediation that leaves it open. We study this failure in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) backing analyst-facing copilots in Security Operations Centers, and identify a class of attacks, \emph{\compmis{}} (COMA), in which every adversarial document is factually correct, instruction-free, non-contradictory, and distributionally benign --- yet the answer is misled by their \emph{composition}. We realize \compmis{} through \emph{action-corruption}, which steers a correctly-diagnosed vulnerability toward an inferior remediation, and \emph{verdict-flip}, which destabilizes the exploitability verdict via an undecidable reachability chain. Action-corruption bites all five tested models --- including frontier reasoning models --- on every run, on two synthetic domains and a real CVE (CVE-2021-33813); verdict-flip bites stochastically, decreasing with model capability but never vanishing. A single principle governs both: the attack succeeds when the disambiguating fact must be \emph{inferred} rather than \emph{read}. We propose \ccd{} (Causal Counterfactual Defense), an audit that measures the leave-one-out causal influence of each retrieved document and flags answers whose influence concentrates on low-trust documents. \ccd{} localizes the attack to attacker-controlled documents with no false positives on four benign multi-document controls; an adaptive influence-spreading adversary is caught by an \emph{aggregate} variant. We release attack seeds and a \ccd{} reference implementation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Diagonal Multi-omics Integration of Heterogenous Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16968)
*Maksim V. Kukushkin,Mikhail S. Arbatskiy,Dmitriy E. Balandin,Alexey V. Churov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究异构数据集的对角多组学整合方法，通过分析Stiefel流形上耦合拉普拉斯算子的极值迹问题，利用泛函分析中的梯度上升法求解最大化问题，并基于最大值与最小值之差引入数据集异质性的新特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学整合面临数据集的生物学异质性挑战，现有方法难以清晰阐明异质性来源与差异，需要从数学基础出发发展更系统的整合与异质性刻画方法。

Method: 将问题建模为复欧几里得空间中同胚于Stiefel流形的集合上的耦合拉普拉斯极值迹问题；采用经典泛函分析框架下规定的梯度上升法求解最大化问题；基于最大值与最小值的范数差定义数据集异质性的新指标。

Result: 提出了一种新的数据集异质性度量特征（最大最小点范数差），并建立了基于Stiefel流形上耦合拉普拉斯算子的极值迹求解框架。

Conclusion: 该方法从泛函分析角度为多组学数据整合提供了数学上严谨的途径，并以新颖方式量化了数据集间的异质性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider methods for the diagonal multi-omics integration of heterogeneous datasets. Several approaches to the nature of biological heterogeneity are analyzed and developed to comprehend more clearly the generated differences. Specifically, the extremal trace problems for the coupled Laplacian on sets homeomorphic to the Stiefel manifold embedded in the complex Euclidean space are investigated. The gradient ascent method for the maximization problem is elaborated in the classical terms of functional analysis, which is of significant interest in itself. On this basis, we introduce a novel characteristic of dataset heterogeneity by employing the norm of the difference between the maximum and minimum points.

</details>


### [194] [Policy Optimization and Statistical Inference for Online Contextual Matrix Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17173)
*Liner Xiang,Yixin Wang,Hengrui Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"在线上下文矩阵博弈"框架，将上下文信息整合到多人在线博弈中，并设计OnGameLearn算法，该算法在玩家动作和上下文两个维度上平衡探索与利用，具备统计保证（支付矩阵尾部界、纳什均衡估计收敛、参数渐近正态性、亚线性遗憾界），并提出了矩阵博弈中的"策略价值"概念及双重稳健的√T一致估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线决策常涉及动态上下文与策略性交互的共同作用（如酒店定价需同时考虑动态环境因素和竞争对手的策略响应），但现有方法各有局限：上下文Bandit只优化单智能体决策而忽略多人交互，在线矩阵博弈虽通过纳什均衡刻画策略行为却假设支付固定、忽略上下文信息。因此需要一种能将上下文信息整合进多人在线博弈的新框架。

Method: 提出在线上下文矩阵博弈框架，将上下文信号与多人博弈支付函数动态结合；设计OnGameLearn在线学习算法，在玩家动作与上下文两个维度上同时平衡探索与利用；提出矩阵博弈中"策略价值"的新概念，并建立双重稳健估计器；提供完整的统计理论分析，包括支付矩阵估计的尾部界、纳什均衡估计的收敛性、参数估计的渐近正态性以及亚线性遗憾界。

Result: 在模拟实验和真实酒店定价应用中，OnGameLearn有效应对了策略性与上下文决策相互交织的挑战；理论上验证了支付矩阵估计的尾部界、纳什均衡估计收敛性、参数估计渐近正态性及亚线性遗憾界，并且策略价值估计器具有√T一致性和双重稳健性。

Conclusion: OnGameLearn填补了上下文Bandit与在线矩阵博弈之间的空白，为同时面临动态环境和策略性博弈的在线决策问题提供了理论与算法上的统一解决方案，并在实际应用中证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Online decision making often requires navigating a landscape shaped by both dynamic contexts and strategic interactions. In competitive pricing, for example, hotels must account for both dynamic contextual factors and rivals' strategic responses. Existing approaches address only part of this challenge: contextual bandits optimize single-agent decisions using observable features but ignore multi-player interactions, while online matrix games capture strategic behavior through Nash equilibrium but assume fixed payoffs, ignoring contextual information. How should agents act then when strategic payoffs evolve with contextual signals? We introduce \emph{online contextual matrix games} to integrate contextual information into multi-player online games. We further propose \emph{OnGameLearn}, an online learning algorithm that efficiently balances exploration and exploitation across both player actions and contexts. This approach comes with statistical guarantees: tail bounds for the estimated payoff matrix, the convergence of the estimated Nash equilibrium, the asymptotic normality of the parameter estimators, and the sublinear regret bound. We also develop the notion of \emph{policy value} in matrix games and develop a doubly robust, $\sqrt{T}$-consistent estimator for it. Across simulated studies and a real-world hotel pricing application, we find that OnGameLearn effectively navigates the intertwined challenges of strategic and contextual decision-making.

</details>


### [195] [Expressivity In Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17203)
*Andrew Stuart,Florian Wolf*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从密度估计的视角研究对比学习架构（特别是CLIP）的表示能力/表达力。作者证明：对于两个模态，双塔CLIP是通用近似器；但对于三个及以上模态，广泛使用的"所有两两相似度求和"的泛化CLIP无法表示任意联合分布（尽管可以匹配所有两两条件分布）。为此提出Hadamard-CLIP，通过在每个编码器之上添加一个可学习的权重向量，在保留CLIP快速、可预计算嵌入检索的同时，恢复任意数量模态下联合分布的通用近似能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习（尤其是CLIP风格模型）在现代表示学习中取得了巨大成功，但其架构的表达力/表示能力仍然缺乏理论理解。作者希望从群体层面、密度估计的视角出发，回答一个纯粹的表征容量问题：给定的对比族参数化能够以任意精度近似哪些模态联合分布？

Method: 采用群体层面（population-level）的密度估计视角，将每个架构视为参数化密度族，研究其能否逼近模态的联合分布。通过理论分析分别考察：1）双模态下的双塔CLIP架构；2）多模态（≥3）下基于所有两两相似度求和损失的泛化CLIP；3）提出的Hadamard-CLIP——在现有编码器之上添加单个可学习权重向量的简单修改。

Result: 1）双模态下，简单双塔CLIP是通用近似器；2）多模态下，两两相似度求和（pairwise-sum）的CLIP泛化无法表示任意联合分布，但可以匹配所有两两条件分布；3）Hadamard-CLIP只需在每个编码器上添加一个可学习权重向量，即可恢复任意模态数量下的联合分布通用近似，同时保留CLIP快速、可预计算嵌入检索的优势。

Conclusion: 对比学习的表达力高度依赖架构选择：双塔CLIP在双模态下是通用的，而朴素的两两求和式多模态扩展存在表达力瓶颈。Hadamard-CLIP以极小的修改（单个可学习权重向量）弥合了这一差距，在保持计算效率的同时实现了多模态联合分布的通用近似。

Abstract: Contrastive learning has become a cornerstone of modern representation learning, powering CLIP-style models that underpin text-to-image generation, vision-language models, and retrieval across a rapidly growing range of modalities. Despite this empirical success, the expressive power of these architectures remains poorly understood. To gain insight, we study expressivity by adopting a population-level, density-estimation viewpoint: each architecture comprises a parameterized set of densities whose parameters may be chosen to approximate the joint distribution of the modalities. This isolates a question of pure representational capacity: which joint distributions can a given contrastive family of parameterizations approximate to arbitrary accuracy? We show that expressivity is sharply architecture-dependent. For two modalities, the simple two-tower CLIP architecture is a universal approximator. A natural generalization of CLIP, widely used in practice when three or more modalities are present, is based on a loss found by summing over all pairwise similarities. This provably cannot represent arbitrary joint distributions, although we prove that it remains expressive enough to match all pairwise conditionals. Motivated by this gap, we propose Hadamard-CLIP, which adds a single learned weight vector on top of the existing encoders and restores universal approximation of the joint for any number of modalities while preserving CLIP's fast, precomputable-embedding retrieval.

</details>


### [196] [SPACE: Sample-cloud Predictive Adaptive Conformal Ellipsoids for Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17333)
*Baishi Li,Kelvin J. L. Koa,Ke-Wei Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出SPACE，一种用于样本生成式多元时间序列预测器的共形包装方法。它直接从当前预测样本云中估计时间局部的协方差几何结构来构造椭球联合预测区域，并通过动态后向窗口选择方案校准区域半径，从而在分布偏移下获得更接近名义目标的联合和滚动覆盖率，较现有基准有更优的覆盖-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代概率性时间序列预测器通过预测样本来表达不确定性，但通过经验分位数转换得到的名义预测区域缺乏正式覆盖率保证，且在分布偏移下偏离名义目标。现有多元共形方法通常从历史残差中估计几何结构，依赖固定或累积的回看窗口，这限制了其利用当前预测的瞬时依赖结构的能力，并容易受到过时状态污染的影响。

Method: SPACE方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 通过直接从当前预测样本云中估计时间局部协方差几何，构造椭球联合预测区域；(2) 采用动态后向窗口选择机制来校准区域的半径，而非依赖静态的历史窗口。

Result: 在多种多元数据集、概率预测器和共形基线上的实验表明，SPACE一致地将联合和滚动覆盖率提升至更接近名义目标，相对于竞争的包装方法实现了更优的覆盖-效率权衡。

Conclusion: SPACE通过直接从当前预测样本云中估计局部几何并动态选择校准窗口，克服了现有共形包装方法对历史状态的过度依赖，为多元概率预测提供了更稳健且更高效的联合预测区域校准方案。

Abstract: Modern probabilistic time-series forecasters often express uncertainty through forecast samples. While typically converted into nominal prediction regions using empirical quantiles, these model-implied sets lack formal coverage guarantees and frequently deviate from nominal targets under distribution shift. Existing multivariate conformal methods can calibrate these regions online, but they typically estimate geometry from historical residuals using fixed or accumulating look-back windows. This reliance on the past limits their ability to exploit the instantaneous dependence structure of current predictions and leaves them vulnerable to stale-regime contamination. To address this, we propose SPACE, a conformal wrapper for sample-generating multivariate forecasters. SPACE constructs ellipsoidal joint prediction regions by estimating time-local covariance geometry directly from the current forecast sample cloud, calibrating the region's radius via a dynamic backward window-selection scheme. Across diverse multivariate datasets, probabilistic forecasters, and conformal baselines, SPACE consistently brings realized joint and rolling coverage closer to the nominal target, achieving superior coverage-efficiency tradeoffs relative to competing wrappers.

</details>


### [197] [Nonlocal Transition Kernel for Efficient Learning of Restricted Boltzmann Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17450)
*Kaiji Sekimoto,Muneki Yasuda*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于深度变温（DT）中RBM序列的往返式转移核，使单次转移即可实现非局部跳跃，相比阻塞吉布斯采样（BGS）和传统深度变温，能更频繁地进行非局部转移、以更少转移次数获得更高采样质量，并使RBM学习更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）的学习依赖对期望的采样近似，通常使用阻塞吉布斯采样（BGS）。然而BGS作为局部马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛转移核，在RBM存在高能量势垒时采样质量差，导致学习性能退化。深度变温（DT）通过并行变温缓解了这一局部性问题，但需要多步才能沿RBM序列完成非局部转移，效率受限。

Method: 提出一种定义在DT所用RBM序列上的转移核，该核具有序列上的"往返"（round-trip）结构，能够在单次转移内实现非局部移动，同时保持RBM序列的不变性。数值实验对比了该核与BGS及DT在非局部转移频率、采样质量和学习稳定性方面的表现。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的转移核能够更频繁地执行非局部转移，并且以更少的转移次数达到更高的采样质量。基于该核的RBM学习过程更加稳定，能够缓解BGS和DT学习中观察到的训练失败问题。

Conclusion: 往返式转移核通过在单次转移中实现非局部跳跃，在采样效率和学习稳定性上优于BGS和传统DT，为RBM的高效学习提供了一种更优的蒙特卡洛采样方案。

Abstract: Learning restricted Boltzmann machines (RBMs) is computationally challenging because it requires expectations whose exact evaluation is generally intractable. The expectations are typically evaluated using a sampling approximation based on blocked Gibbs sampling (BGS), which is a local Markov chain Monte Carlo transition kernel. However, the locality of BGS can lead to poor sampling quality when the RBM has high energy barriers, thereby degrading learning performance. Deep tempering (DT), which performs parallel tempering over a sequence of learnable RBMs including the training RBM, alleviates this locality issue. However, DT algorithmically requires multiple steps to move through the RBM sequence to achieve a nonlocal transition. In this paper, we propose a transition kernel defined over the RBM sequence used in DT. The proposed kernel has a round-trip structure over the sequence, enabling nonlocal moves within a single transition while leaving the RBM sequence invariant. Numerical experiments show that the proposed kernel performs nonlocal transitions more frequently and achieves higher sampling quality with fewer transitions than BGS and DT. We further verify that learning based on the proposed kernel is more stable and mitigates the training failures observed with BGS- and DT-based learning.

</details>


### [198] [Online Generalized Sparse Regression: How Does Overparametrization Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17466)
*Shuoguang Yang,Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出在线广义稀疏约束回归框架，针对在线基数约束线性回归和低秩矩阵感知问题，设计了一种在线硬阈值（online hard-thresholding）算法，通过闭环更新和仅存储汇总统计量实现高效计算，在非凸组合优化难题下仍达到最优统计率的全局收敛，数值实验优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化稀疏回归在离线场景已被广泛研究，但在线形式仍相对未被充分探索。这主要源于四大挑战：正则化参数无法在每轮在线动态更新、存储与内存复杂度难以管理、需要在每一轮通过闭环更新实现实时计算而非求解完整优化问题、以及在现实假设下获得最优统计保证。现有在线正则化方法需要动态调参，存在根本性局限。

Method: 提出在线广义稀疏约束回归框架（涵盖在线基数约束线性回归与低秩矩阵感知），采用约束形式取代正则化形式，从而消除动态参数调节需求。设计了在线硬阈值算法，每次迭代仅需闭环更新（closed-form update），仅存储汇总统计量（summary statistics），在计算、内存和存储上均高效。通过将投影集合适当过参数化（overparameterized），克服了问题固有的非凸性与组合难解性。

Result: 算法在现实假设下实现了全局收敛，且收敛速度达到最优统计率（optimal statistical rate）。数值实验表明，该方法一致性地优于现有的最先进（state-of-the-art）替代方案。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了在线稀疏回归的理论与算法空白，表明通过恰当设计约束集合并利用硬阈值闭环更新，可以同时兼顾在线学习的计算效率与最优统计性能，为在线高维稀疏学习提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: Regularized sparse regression has been extensively studied in the offline setting, but online formulation remains relatively under-explored. This gap stems from four key challenges: (i) the infeasibility of dynamically updating the regularization parameter in every online round, (ii) managing storage and memory complexity, (iii) enabling real-time computation via closed-form updates rather than solving full optimization problems at each round, and (iv) achieving optimal statistical guarantees under realistic assumptions. In this paper, we propose an online generalized-sparsity-constrained regression framework, focusing on online cardinality-constrained linear regression and low-rank matrix sensing. Unlike online regularized regression, our constrained formulation eliminates the need for dynamic parameter tuning. We introduce an efficient online hard-thresholding algorithm that performs closed-form updates and requires storing only summary statistics, making it computationally, memory, and storage efficient. Despite the inherent nonconvexity and combinatorial nature of the formulation, our algorithm achieves global convergence at the optimal statistical rate under realistic assumptions, provided that the projection set is properly overparameterized. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art alternatives.

</details>


### [199] [Feature Priming in Online Linear Regression: Sparse-Regret Lower Bounds and a Tight Univariate Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17573)
*Huibo Xu,Shi Fu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维在线预测中的"特征启动"（feature priming）规则，从理论上证明了三种此类规则在COLT 2023开放问题中关于稀疏对数形式的遗憾界无法实现，并给出了匹配的遗憾下界与上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维在线预测中，最优预测器可能仅依赖少数特征，遗憾应与稀疏度而非环境维度有关。Warmuth和Amid在COLT 2023提出开放问题：三种特征启动规则是否具有竞争力的在线遗憾保证。本文旨在回答该问题。

Method: 采用基于过去数据的自然Moore-Penrose协议，通过精确的目标质量恒等式和双符号论证将"廉价干扰插值"效应转化为限幅预测损失；利用Hadamard构造证明三种规则的Omega(min{T,sqrt(d)})遗憾下界；并通过数据秩控制遗憾，用欧几里得归一化三角构造匹配依赖关系，辅以配对岭回归构造覆盖全部三种幂启规则。

Result: 对COLT开放问题的稀疏对数形式给出否定答案，证明三种规则在零损失一稀疏比较器上实现Omega(min{T,sqrt{d}})遗憾下界；遗憾受数据秩控制，并针对幂启动单变量规则建立了匹配的上界；在冻结语言模型激活的诊断实验中观察到干扰插值、目标权重与损失之间的相同关系。

Conclusion: 特征启动规则无法实现稀疏对数形式的竞争性在线遗憾保证，其根本障碍是廉价干扰插值导致重拟合低估真正预测坐标的权重。精确的多元与Pearson边界仍为开放问题。

Abstract: In high-dimensional online prediction, the best predictor may depend on only a few features, so regret should scale with sparsity rather than the ambient dimension. Feature priming pursues this goal by estimating feature weights from past data and refitting a minimum-norm predictor on the rescaled design. Warmuth and Amid asked at COLT 2023 whether any of three such rules admits a competitive online regret guarantee. Using the natural Moore--Penrose protocol based only on past data, we give a negative answer to the sparse-logarithmic form of this COLT open problem. Our analysis identifies a common obstruction: cheap nuisance interpolation causes the refit to underweight the truly predictive coordinate. An exact target-mass identity and a two-sign argument turn this effect into clipped prediction loss. Hadamard constructions force $Ω(\min\{T,\sqrt{d}\})$ regret for all three rules against a zero-loss one-sparse comparator, with extensions to fixed prime powers and selectors among the rules. Conversely, regret is controlled by data rank, and a Euclidean-normalized triangular construction matches this dependence for powered univariate priming, even under nonnegative second-stage ridge regularization; a paired ridge construction also covers all three powered rules. Exploratory diagnostics on frozen language-model activations exhibit the same relation among nuisance interpolation, target weight, and loss. The exact multivariate and Pearson frontiers remain open.

</details>


### [200] [Toward the Optimal Regret-Instability Trade-off in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17841)
*Kaifei Wang,Yinyu Ye,Han Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在有限时间（非渐近）框架下研究了多臂老虎机算法中后悔（regret）与不稳定性（instability，即终端拉臂次数的标准差）之间的权衡关系。作者证明了乘积下界 R·S ≥ C·T^{3/2}，并提出了新的可调算法 SLE-UCB，其乘积上界达到 O(T^{3/2} log K)，与下界在 T 上精确匹配、在 K 上仅差对数因子。这解决了对数因子外均最优的 regret-instability 前沿问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多臂老虎机文献仅以后悔作为性能指标，但相同后悔水平下不同算法在不同独立实验之间可能存在差异巨大的资源分配波动（不稳定）。此前仅有的渐近分析依赖正则性假设，且 arm 依赖的前沿问题尚未解决。作者旨在有限时间框架下建立后悔与不稳定性之间的最优权衡，并回答文献中遗留的 arm 依赖权衡边界的开放问题。

Method: 1) 在有限时间后悔条件（而非渐近正则假设）下，证明 R·S ≥ C T^{3/2} 的乘积下界；2) 提出 SLE-UCB 算法：结合运行下包络（running lower-envelope）指数与递减的拉臂次数稳定器，算法可调；3) 为证明不稳定性上界，开发全新的离线 top-prefix 表示，从在线决策中去除路径依赖；结合单奖励扰动和 Efron–Stein 不等式控制拉臂次数方差。

Result: 证明了下界 R_KT·S_KT ≥ C T^{3/2}（C 与 K、T 无关），并在有限时间条件下成立，无需先前的渐近正则假设。SLE-UCB 满足 R·S = O(T^{3/2} log K)，隐含常数与 K、T 无关，与下界在 T 上精确匹配、在 K 上仅差对数因子。后悔与不稳定性关于 K 呈倒数关系，而二者乘积对 K 无多项式依赖，解决了文献中的开放问题。

Conclusion: 后悔与不稳定性之间存在固有的乘积下界 R·S ≥ C T^{3/2}，SLE-UCB 在有限时间下达到该下界的最优阶（T 上精确、K 上仅差对数因子），确立了 sharp 的 regret-instability 权衡前沿。这项工作不仅提供了新的算法，还通过离线 top-prefix 表示为分析拉臂次数方差提供了全新工具。

Abstract: Multi-armed bandit algorithms are evaluated by regret, yet comparable regret can coexist with different allocations across independent runs. We study the trade-off between worst-case regret $\mathcal{R}_{K,T}$ and instability $\mathcal S_{K,T}$, defined as the largest standard deviation of a terminal pull count, for $K$ arms and $T$ rounds. We prove the finite-time lower bound $\mathcal R_{K,T}\mathcal S_{K,T}\ge C T^{3/2}$, where $C$ is independent of $K$ and $T$, under a finite-time regret condition and without the regularity assumptions imposed in the prior asymptotic analysis. We also introduce Stabilized Lower-Envelope UCB (\textup{\textsc{SLE-UCB}}), a new tunable algorithm combining a running lower-envelope index with a decreasing pull-count stabilizer. \textup{\textsc{SLE-UCB}} satisfies $\mathcal R_{K,T}\mathcal S_{K,T}=O(T^{3/2}\log K)$, with an implicit constant independent of $K$ and $T$, matching the lower bound exactly in $T$ and within a logarithmic factor in $K$. To prove the instability bound, we develop a new offline top-prefix representation that removes path dependence from online decisions. Together with single-reward perturbations and the Efron--Stein inequality, this representation controls pull-count variance. Thus, regret and instability depend reciprocally on $K$, while their product has no polynomial dependence on $K$. These results resolve the open question raised in the literature concerning the sharp arm-dependent regret--instability frontier.

</details>


### [201] [Where A Small Language Model Helps in Invoice Categorisation, Understood Through Embedding Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18033)
*Emma Ceccherini,Daniel Lawson,Anjulika Salhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用小型语言模型（SLM）进行发票自动分类为总账（GL）代码的方法。作者分析了SBERT和DeBERTa在金融语料上的预训练嵌入几何特性，发现句子嵌入空间全局各向异性但局部各向同性，且簇与供应商身份强相关。在单个GPU上微调的SBERT达到0.96的分类准确率，超越了零样本LLM和供应商身份基线；在新客户泛化问题上，约100条客户特定发票即可达到0.9的F1分数，证明自托管SLM方案可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发票归类到正确的总账（GL）代码是财务报表和税务合规的基础，但这并非例行任务，而是需要专业会计判断：正确类别取决于采购业务性质、供应商和发票文本等多方面因素。虽然AI在各行业被越来越多地用于任务自动化，但基于内部小型语言模型（SLM）的实现可以同时降低成本并提高数据安全性、保密性和可解释性。作者希望探究这种自托管SLM方案的可行性。

Method: 首先分析小型句子变换器（SBERT）和经典SLM（DeBERTa）预训练嵌入的几何特性。然后使用单GPU微调SBERT进行发票分类，并对比零样本LLM和供应商身份基线的性能。通过几何分析与分类性能的关联，评估嵌入几何对分类效果的影响，并测试结构化输入对SLM性能的影响。

Result: 在金融语料上，句子嵌入空间全局各向异性但由局部各向同性簇组成，这些簇与供应商身份强相关。微调后的SBERT在发票分类上达到0.96准确率，高于零样本LLM和供应商身份基线，尤其对小而困难的类别和新客户的性能提升明显。在新客户泛化问题上，约100条客户特定发票即可达到0.9的F1分数。此外，预训练嵌入几何与分类性能相关，且发现结构化的、对人友好的输入反而不会提升SLM性能。

Conclusion: 自托管的小型语言模型（SLM）方案在发票GL代码分类任务上是可行且有前景的：不仅以较低成本实现高准确率，还兼顾了数据安全性和可解释性。嵌入几何分析揭示了预训练空间结构与下游分类性能的关联，并提出了一个反直觉的发现——帮助人类阅读的结构化输入并不会改善SLM的性能。

Abstract: Categorising invoices into the correct General Ledger (GL) code underpins financial reporting and tax compliance. This is a skilled accounting judgement rather than a routine task: the correct category depends subtly on the nature of the purchasing business, the vendor and the invoice text. Whilst AI is increasingly being adopted across industries to automate tasks, including invoice categorisation, implementations built on in-house small language models (SLMs) can simultaneously reduce cost and improve data security, confidentiality, and interpretability. We investigate this approach by first analysing the pre-trained embedding geometry of a small sentence transformer (SBERT) and classic SLM (DeBERTa). The sentence-embedding space of this financial corpus is globally anisotropic but composed of locally isotropic clusters, extending prior token-level findings to sentence embeddings in a financial setting, and these clusters are strongly correlated with the vendor identity. SBERT fine-tuned on a single GPU reaches 0.96 accuracy on invoice classification, above both a zero-shot LLM and a vendor identity baseline, increasing performance for smaller, challenging categories and new clients. For this important generalisation problem, SBERT reaches 0.9 F1 with roughly 100 client-specific invoices, showing that an in-house SLM implementation is promising. Combining these results with geometric analysis shows that pre-trained embedding geometry is associated with classification performance and reveals a counterintuitive finding that a structured input that would help a human reader does not improve the SLM performance.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Minimax Optimal Estimator and Improved Error Rate for the MLE in Logistic Regression with Gaussian Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17260)
*Junren Chen,Arya Mazumdar*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯设计逻辑回归中有限样本参数估计问题，首次提出了极小极大最优估计器，并将最大似然估计（MLE）的最佳已知有限样本误差率从O(√(R³d/n))改进到Õ(√(R³/n)+R²d/n)。关键贡献是构造了最优的范数估计器，达到O(√(R³/n))的极小极大最优误差率，结合MLE的定向估计，最终建立了参数估计的极小极大最优率Θ(√(Rd/n)+√(R³/n))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高斯设计逻辑回归中，参数估计的有限样本理论尚不完善。现有MLE的范数估计误差率O(√(R³d/n))存在d维依赖，而信息论下界表明Ω(√(R³/n))才是最优的。作者希望揭示MLE的额外误差项R²d/n是内在偏差而非信息论所必需的，从而构造更优的估计器。

Method: 作者首先建立范数估计的极小极大下界Ω(√(R³/n))；然后分析MLE的范数估计误差，将其改进到Õ(√(R³/n)+R²d/n)，并论证R²d/n是MLE的内在偏差（通过Zhao, Sur和Candes的高维渐近理论佐证）；接着构造一个高效的去偏差（debiased）范数估计器，消除了该偏差项，达到极小极大最优率O(√(R³/n))；最后将最优范数估计与MLE提供的方向估计相结合，得到参数θ*的极小极大最优估计器。

Result: 1) 建立了范数估计的极小极大下界Ω(√(R³/n))；2) 将MLE的范数估计误差率从O(√(R³d/n))改进到Õ(√(R³/n)+R²d/n)；3) 构造了达到O(√(R³/n))的极小极大最优去偏差范数估计器；4) 建立了参数估计的极小极大最优率Θ(√(Rd/n)+√(R³/n))；5) 给出了MLE改进的有限样本误差率Õ(√(Rd/n)+√(R³/n)+R²d/n)；6) 数值实验展示了所提估计器优于MLE。

Conclusion: 该工作表明MLE在高维逻辑回归中存在与维度无关的内在偏差R²d/n，但这种偏差并非信息论意义上不可避免的。通过构造去偏差的范数估计器，可以在不损失信息效率的前提下实现极小极大最优，为高维逻辑回归的参数估计提供了理论最优的解决方案。

Abstract: We study finite-sample parameter estimation in logistic regression with Gaussian design, where the goal is to estimate $\mathbfθ^*\in \mathbb{R}^d$ with $R=\|\mathbfθ^*\|_2\ge 1$ from i.i.d. samples $\{(\mathbf{x}_i,y_i)\}_{i=1}^n,$ $\mathbf{x}_i \sim N(0,\mathbf{I}_d)$, $y_i\mid \mathbf{x}_i \sim \mathrm{Bernoulli}((1+\exp(-\mathbf{x}_i^\top \mathbfθ^*))^{-1})$. In this paper, we provide the first minimax optimal estimator, and improve on the best known finite-sample error rate for the maximum likelihood estimator (MLE). These two accomplishments are due to a minimax optimal estimator for the parameter norm $R$. First, we establish the minimax lower bound $Ω(\sqrt{R^3/n})$ for norm estimation. We then improve the best known norm estimation error rate of the MLE, i.e., $O(\sqrt{R^3d/n})$ from Chardon, Lerasle and Mourtada (2024), to $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{R^3/n}+R^2d/n)$. The additional term, $R^2d/n$, appears to be the intrinsic bias of the MLE, as evidenced by the high-dimensional asymptotic theory of Zhao, Sur and Candes (2022) and numerical examples. We show that, however, this additional term is not information-theoretically necessary. To this end, we construct an efficient debiased norm estimator that achieves the error rate $O(\sqrt{R^3/n})$ and is therefore minimax optimal. Combining this with the optimal direction estimator given by the MLE, we establish the minimax optimal rate $Θ(\sqrt{Rd/n}+\sqrt{R^3/n})$ for estimating $\mathbfθ^*$, as well as the improved finite-sample error rate $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{Rd/n}+\sqrt{R^3/n}+R^2d/n)$ for the MLE. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed minimax optimal estimators outperform the MLE.

</details>
