### [203] [An RKHS Framework for Fixed Effects in Permanental Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17908)
*Matthew LeDuc*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文扩展了永久过程模型，使其包含固定效应项。通过在固定效应系数上加入先验，并在扩散先验极限下利用表示定理，证明了永久过程的强度函数可自然分解为固定效应项和RKHS中的函数项。该极限等价核所定义的RKHS的平方范数恰好等于极限惩罚项，从而为永久过程模型提供了清晰的科学解释，并便于将领域知识纳入估计过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永久过程模型在强度估计方面具有优势，但原有模型缺乏对固定效应的显式建模，这限制了模型的可解释性和领域知识的整合能力。作者希望通过将固定效应纳入永久过程框架，使模型更具统计解释性。

Method: 在固定效应系数上设置先验，在扩散先验极限下利用表示定理（representer theorem）推导强度函数的解析形式。通过分析极限等价核，建立其与RKHS的联系，并证明该RKHS的平方范数与极限惩罚项等价。

Result: 证明了永久过程中的强度函数可以自然分解为固定效应项与RKHS中的函数项两部分；极限等价核定义的RKHS的平方范数恰为极限惩罚项。

Conclusion: 该扩展使得永久过程模型具有直接的统计可解释性，方便将领域知识系统地整合到估计过程中。

Abstract: This short work describes an extension of the permanental process model which includes fixed effects. By starting with a prior on the fixed effects coefficients we show that, in the diffuse prior limit, the intensity function of the permanental process can be found using the representer theorem and naturally decomposed into a fixed effects term and a function which is an element of a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS). We show that the limiting equivalent kernel defines an RKHS whose squared norm is exactly the limiting penalty. This allows for straightforward scientific interpretation of permanental process models and the easy incorporation of domain knowledge into the estimation process.

</details>


### [204] [Finite-Sample Bias Correction for Plug-in Estimators of Extropy, Rényi Extropy, and Tsallis Extropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17185)
*Amadou Diadie Ba*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限支撑离散随机变量的Shannon、Rényi和Tsallis熵的plug-in估计量的有限样本偏差。通过分析中间泛函S_α的偏差，推导出三种估计量的显式一阶偏差公式，并提出偏差校正估计量。校正与未校正估计量渐近等价，仅相差O(1/n)阶项。模拟验证了理论结果并展示了偏差校正估计量的优越有限样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息论与统计估计中，熵估计量的有限样本偏差问题是一个重要但常被忽视的问题。虽然已有大量关于熵估计的渐近理论研究，但对于有限支撑离散随机变量的Shannon、Rényi和Tsallis熵的plug-in估计量的精确有限样本偏差特性尚缺乏系统的显式刻画。该研究旨在填补这一理论空白，并为实践中的小样本场景提供更可靠的偏差校正方法。

Method: 本文首先将三种熵（Shannon、Rényi和Tsallis）的plug-in估计量的偏差分析归结为对中间泛函S_α=Σ(1-p_i)^α的偏差分析。通过代数展开和概率论方法推导出一阶偏差的显式公式。基于这些偏差公式，提出偏差校正估计量，并分析校正前后估计量的渐近等价性。最后通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 获得了三种熵估计量的一阶偏差显式公式；提出的偏差校正估计量在有限样本下具有更优性能；校正与未校正估计量在渐近意义下等价，差异仅为O(1/n)阶项。模拟研究验证了所有理论推导的正确性。

Conclusion: 该研究为熵估计的有限样本偏差提供了完整的理论框架。偏差校正估计量在有限样本中表现更优，但由于两类估计量渐近等价，在渐近场景（如伴随论文中的几乎必然收敛和渐近正态性分析）下可直接使用更简单的未校正plug-in估计量。这为实际应用中的估计量选择提供了明确的理论指导。

Abstract: This paper studies the finite-sample bias of plug-in estimators for Shannon, Rényi, and Tsallis extropies for finitely supported discrete random variables. We derive explicit first-order bias formulas for all three estimators by analyzing the bias of the intermediate functional S_α= \sum_{i=1}^r (1-p_i)^α. Bias-corrected estimators are then proposed. We show that the uncorrected and corrected estimators are asymptotically equivalent, differing only by terms of order O(1/n). A simulation study validates the theoretical results and demonstrates the superior finite-sample performance of the bias-corrected estimators. The results justify the use of the simpler uncorrected plug-in estimators in asymptotic settings, such as those considered in the companion paper on almost sure convergence and asymptotic normality.

</details>


### [205] [A Characterization of Measures of Concordance of Degree Two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17669)
*Takaaki Koike,Haruki Tsunekawa*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文刻画了至多二次的二元一致性度量（concordance measures），利用规范极化（canonical polarization）技术将泛函的二次性转化为逐截面的仿射性，证明这类度量与满足平方群等变等性质的copula索引测度场一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从代数/泛函分析的角度系统刻画二元一致性度量中"次数至多二次"的一类，建立其完整的结构理论，并基于此提供构造新度量的一般方法。

Method: 核心工具是规范极化，将二次泛函转化为逐截面仿射泛函，从而在每一条截面上应用仿射泛函的积分表示。由此证明次数至多二次的度量与满足平方群等变性、积分表示等性质的copula索引测度场构成双射；独立性处的场决定仿射线性部分，场的位移决定纯二次部分；精确二次等价于场的非常数性。进一步利用平方群等变的仿射copula算子构造二次度量，并给出加权平均算子生成二次度量的充要条件。

Result: 建立了次数至多二次的一致性度量与copula索引测度场之间的双射刻画定理；证明独立性处的场值决定仿射部分，场位移决定二次部分，精确二次等价于场的非恒常性；并给出了基于平方群等变仿射copula算子的具体构造方法及加权平均算子的充要条件。

Conclusion: 通过规范极化这一统一工具，作者完成了次数至多二次的一类二元一致性度量的完整代数结构刻画，并提供了可操作的构造途径，为该类度量的理论与应用研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We characterize bivariate measures of concordance of degree at most two in the sense that the measures evaluated at copula-mixture segments are polynomials of degree at most two. Our main device is the canonical polarization, which converts quadraticity of a functional into separate affinity, thereby allowing an integral representation for affine functionals to be applied sectionwise. Using this technique, we prove that measures of degree at most two are in bijection with copula-indexed measure fields satisfying several properties including square-group equivariance. The field at independence determines the affine linearization, while its displacement determines the purely quadratic part. Moreover, exact degree two is equivalent to field non-constancy. Leveraging our characterization result, we provide a construction of degree-two measures of concordance based on affine copula operators that are square-group equivariant. As a more concrete example, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for a weighted averaging operator of square-group transformed copulas to generate a measure of concordance of degree two.

</details>


### [206] [A First-Order Entropy Law for Canonical T-Complexity of Finite-Alphabet i.i.d. Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17958)
*Thomas Schürmann*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于严格正 i.i.d. 源产生的精确长度-N 块，经典 T-复杂度 c_T 与 e^{-γ}h(p)N/log N 之比依概率收敛到 1，且对所有固定 1≤r<∞ 在 L^r 意义下也收敛到 1，其中 h(p) 为源熵（以纳特为单位），γ 为欧拉-马歇罗尼常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 T-复杂度是通用源码理论中的重要复杂度度量，旨在建立其在渐近极限下的精确行为。此前的研究可能只给出了粗糙的界或未能在所有矩意义下收敛，本文目标是在依概率和 L^r 两个层面上给出精确的一阶渐近常数。

Method: 证明分为三个技术组件：(1) 经典恢复的精确长度预算分析；(2) 理想反向链的临界尺度 E_1 估计；(3) 精确有限块边界表示。通过精确的 Doob-变换恒等式表达有限边界律相对于理想律的条件分布（每一步避免当前历史依赖的后续码字），并利用历史均匀更新估计使望远镜式的端点密度一致渐近于 1，从而确保无一步近似误差累积。

Result: 对任意严格正 i.i.d. 源，经典 T-复杂度满足 c_T(W_N)/(e^{-γ}h(p)N/log N) → 1，该收敛同时在依概率和 L^r（任意固定 1≤r<∞）意义下成立。

Conclusion: 该结果表明经典 T-复杂度在精确的一阶渐近意义下由 e^{-γ}h(p)N/log N 控制，且收敛性在矩意义下也成立，说明其渐近行为在概率意义上是稳健的。

Abstract: Let $W_N$ be an exact length-$N$ block from a strictly positive i.i.d. source $\mathbf p$ on a fixed finite alphabet. We prove that the canonical T-complexity $c_T$ satisfies \[ \frac{c_T(W_N)}{e^{-γ}h(\mathbf p) N/\log N}\longrightarrow1 \] in probability and in $L^r$ for every fixed $1\le r<\infty$, where $h(\mathbf p)$ is the source entropy in nats and $γ$ is the Euler-Mascheroni constant. The proof combines an exact length budget for canonical recovery, a critical-scale $E_1$ estimate for an ideal backward chain, and an exact finite-block boundary representation. An exact Doob-transform identity expresses the finite-boundary law relative to the ideal law conditioned at each step to avoid the current history-dependent successor codeword. A history-uniform renewal estimate then makes the telescoping endpoint density uniformly asymptotic to one, so no one-step approximation errors accumulate.

</details>


### [207] [Hamiltonian dynamics for sampling on discrete spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17961)
*Raphaël Barboni,Sebastiano Grazzi,Giacomo Zanella*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类在离散状态空间上的不可逆Hamiltonian Monte Carlo（HMC）动力学方法，通过将离散状态与连续动量变量增强来构造，无需对离散状态空间进行连续嵌入。文章建立了目标分布不变性条件，研究了过程的遍历性、指数收缩性、渐近方差和弛豫时间，并在细网格和高维超立方体两种极限下推导出标度极限，证明了相比可逆采样器具有从扩散到弹道的加速效果。此外，文中还提供了基于分裂、τ-leaping和梯度近似的近似方案以降低计算成本，并在高维超立方体、混合模型和排列等实例上进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散状态空间上的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器通常表现出缓慢的混合速率，而连续空间中的HMC方法通过注入动量实现了显著加速。然而，将HMC思想推广到离散状态空间存在挑战：现有方法往往需要对离散空间进行连续嵌入或近似处理。本文旨在开发一种无需连续嵌入的、通用的不可逆HMC动力学，以在保持对目标分布不变性的同时，实现比可逆采样器更快的收敛速度，并利用不可逆性获得弹道式的加速效果。

Method: 本文提出一种将离散状态与连续动量变量相结合的非可逆HMC框架。具体方法包括：(1) 在离散状态空间上构造带连续动量的动力学过程，不要求离散空间的连续嵌入；(2) 建立目标分布不变性的充分条件；(3) 利用事件驱动的模拟方法实现精确的连续时间模拟；(4) 为了降低计算成本，提出基于算子分裂、τ-leaping以及梯度近似的三种近似方案，在保证一定精度的前提下减少对目标分布的邻居查询需求。

Result: 论文证明了所提过程在离散状态空间上的遍历性和指数收缩性，并分析了渐近方差与弛豫时间。在细网格极限下，合理的重标度过程收敛到连续空间的Hamiltonian动力学；在高维超立方体上同样获得类似的标度极限。与可逆采样器相比，该动力学展示了从扩散（diffusive）到弹道（ballistic）的加速，即便在处理异质性目标分布时仍能保持此优势，而这些情形下标准不可逆方法只能达到扩散水平。数值实验在高维超立方体、混合模型和排列问题上验证了方法的有效性和加速效果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套完整的一般性不可逆离散HMC动力学理论框架，涵盖不变性条件、收敛性分析和极限行为刻画。该框架不仅在理论上证明了相比可逆采样器的显著加速优势，还通过多种近似方案平衡了计算开销与精度，为离散状态空间上的高效采样提供了新的实用工具。未来工作可进一步探索近似方案的严格误差界以及更广泛离散结构的应用。

Abstract: We develop a general class of non-reversible Hamiltonian Monte Carlo dynamics on discrete state spaces. The method augments the discrete state with a continuous momentum variable and does not require a continuous embedding of the discrete state space. We establish conditions for invariance of the target distribution and study the resulting processes in terms of ergodicity, exponential contractivity, asymptotic variance and relaxation time. We then derive scaling limits on increasingly fine lattices and high-dimensional hypercubes. In both settings, suitable rescalings converge to Hamiltonian dynamics in continuous space, revealing a diffusive-to-ballistic speed-up over reversible samplers, even for heterogeneous target distributions where standard non-reversible methods become diffusive. The proposed dynamics can be simulated exactly in continuous time, given access to the target distribution at all neighbours of the current state. To reduce computational cost, we provide approximation schemes based on splitting, $τ$-leaping and gradient approximations. We illustrate the proposed framework numerically on examples involving high-dimensional hypercubes, mixture models and permutations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [On the Identifiability of Masked Prediction: Mode Blindness and Mask Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01383)
*Yichao Cai,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码预测（masked prediction）在具有两个良好分离全局模式的数据上的可识别性问题。作者证明掩码调度（mask schedule）单独决定了条件预测能否固定底层联合分布：大上下文掩码对全局模式权重"失明"，而低可见性掩码和正的全掩码质量可以恢复可识别性。作者引入ε-可识别模量来量化这一现象，并用枚举、梯度训练和真实语料实验验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码预测通过拟合调度加权的条件分布族来学习表示，但尚不清楚近最优条件预测何时能唯一确定底层联合分布。当数据具有两个远离混合时间恢复保证范围的全局模式时，该问题尤为关键——理解可识别性的边界将决定掩码预测方法的可靠性。

Method: (1) 理论分析：针对双模式数据，证明大上下文掩码调度下，重新加权两个模式可使联合分布移动常数级别的全变差距离，同时掩码目标仅产生指数级微扰；(2) 引入ε-可识别模量（largest distributional error consistent with a given excess risk），证明在指数级小的超额风险下该模量仍是宏观的；(3) 通过精确信息分解，证明模式权重敏感性由可见上下文下的残差模式不确定性决定；(4) 实证验证：在可计算分布上枚举验证预测速率，用梯度训练复现模式失明与恢复现象，并在真实语料上测量自然文本位于两种认证区间之间。

Result: (1) 掩码调度单独决定可识别性：大上下文主导的调度对全局模式权重不可识别（ε-可识别模量在指数级小的超额风险下仍为宏观常数）；(2) 低可见性掩码可恢复模式权重敏感性，正的全掩码质量可在无需数据分布假设的情况下锚定联合分布；(3) 实验层面：枚举验证了理论预测的速率，梯度训练同时复现了模式失明与恢复，真实语料显示自然文本介于两个认证区间之间。

Conclusion: 掩码预测的可识别性完全由掩码调度（而非数据分布本身）决定。大上下文掩码无法识别全局模式权重，而低可见性掩码与全掩码质量可以恢复可识别性。该发现为设计掩码预测模型提供了重要的理论指导，即掩码调度的选择直接决定了所学表示的分布保真度。

Abstract: Masked prediction learns representations by fitting a schedule-weighted family of conditional laws, but it remains unclear when near-optimal conditional prediction pins down the underlying joint law. We study this question for data with two well-separated global modes, outside the reach of rapid-mixing recovery guarantees, and show that the answer is decided by the mask schedule alone. Under large-context mode pinning, reweighting the two modes can move the joint law by a constant in total variation while perturbing the masked objective exponentially little in the visible-context size: mask schedules dominated by large contexts are provably blind to the global mode weights. To quantify this, we introduce an $\varepsilon$-identifiability modulus, the largest distributional error consistent with a given excess risk, and prove that it remains macroscopic at an excess risk that is exponentially small. An exact information decomposition pinpoints what restores identifiability: mode-weight sensitivity is governed by the residual mode uncertainty given the visible context. Consequently, low-visibility masks recover this sensitivity, and positive full-mask mass anchors the joint law over all admissible models with no assumption on the data law. Empirically, we test our theory at three levels: enumeration on computable laws verifies the predicted rates, gradient training reproduces both the mode blindness and the recovery, and measurements on real corpora place natural text between the two certified regimes.

</details>


### [209] [Causal Discovery in Equal Variance Linear Gaussian DAGs via SURE-Tuned Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17132)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SURE-Ridge，一种非迭代、闭式解的因果发现方法，用于等方差线性高斯结构方程模型，通过并行节点回归和Stein无偏风险估计自适应选择正则化参数，配合自适应阈值提取DAG，在小样本和计算受限场景下优于NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连续优化的因果发现方法（如NOTEARS等）依赖迭代梯度下降和逐问题超参数调节，难以适应两类实际重要场景：样本受限（样本数与节点数相当或更少）和计算受限场景。

Method: SURE-Ridge包含三种核心设计：(1) 非迭代的闭式估计器，避免了梯度下降；(2) 并行节点回归，利用Stein无偏风险估计(SURE)自适应选择正则化参数；(3) 自适应阈值化过程，从软邻接矩阵中提取DAG结构。

Result: 在小样本体制下取得最低的结构汉明距离（SHD），在所有测试样本量下运行时间最低，均优于NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL基线方法。

Conclusion: SURE-Ridge证明了一种非迭代、闭式解的岭回归方法可以有效替代迭代优化方法，特别适合样本有限和计算资源受限的因果发现任务，在准确性和效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Recovering the directed acyclic graph (DAG) of a structural equation model (SEM) from observational data is a central problem in causal discovery. The iterative gradient descent and per-problem hyperparameter tuning of continuous-optimization methods are poorly suited to two practically important regimes: the sample-limited regime, where the number of samples is comparable to or smaller than the number of nodes in the DAG, and the compute-limited regime. This work proposes SURE-Ridge, a non-iterative, closed-form estimator for equal variance linear Gaussian SEM. The method performs parallel node-wise regressions with regularization parameters chosen adaptively by Stein's unbiased risk estimate (SURE), and applies an adaptive thresholding procedure to extract a DAG from the resulting soft adjacency matrix. Numerical results show that SURE-Ridge achieves the lowest structural Hamming distance in the small-sample regime and the lowest run time across all sample sizes tested, compared with NOTEARS, DAGMA, and GBNSL baselines.

</details>


### [210] [Tight Bounds for Data-driven Multiple Hyper-parameter Tuning with Structured Loss Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17343)
*Anh Tuan Nguyen,Viet Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对数据驱动算法设计中多维超参数调优的伪维度界问题，利用实代数几何方法改进了学习理论上的上界，并提出了多阶段下界框架证明上界紧致性，同时将拓扑框架扩展到双层验证损失调优及更广泛的半代数应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动算法设计将超参数调优视为统计学习问题，但由于模型性能对超参数的隐式非光滑依赖，建立泛化保证颇具挑战。现有基于分段多项式假设的多维界在理论上较为松散，且缺乏全面的下界，无法验证上界紧致性。

Method: 1) 利用实代数几何细化学习理论上界，通过分析块消元过程中不变连通符号胞腔（而非孤立符号向量）来避免拓扑过度计数，得到更严格的样本复杂度上界；2) 提出多阶段下界框架，区分组合容量与代数容量，通过构造不同阶段下的破碎问题实例，证明上界被紧饱和；3) 将拓扑框架拓展至一般双层验证损失调优以及更广泛的半代数应用场景。

Result: 建立了多维数据驱动调优的紧致伪维度界：上界通过实代数几何方法显著收紧，下界通过多阶段构造证明对所有主要情形上界都是紧饱和的，从而实现了理论与实际容量的匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为多维数据驱动调优提供了严格且紧致的样本复杂度刻画，还为双层验证损失调优和更广泛的半代数学习问题提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Data-driven algorithm design frames hyperparameter tuning as a statistical learning problem, but establishing generalization guarantees remains challenging due to the implicit, non-smooth dependence of model performance on hyperparameters. Existing multi-dimensional bounds under piecewise-polynomial assumptions remain theoretically loose and lack comprehensive lower bounds. We resolve this by establishing tight pseudo-dimension bounds for multi-dimensional data-driven tuning. First, we refine the learning-theoretic upper bound using real algebraic geometry; by analyzing invariant connected sign cells during block elimination rather than isolated sign vectors, we avoid topological over-counting to derive strictly sharper sample complexities. Second, we present a multi-regime lower-bound framework that disentangles combinatorial and algebraic capacities. By constructing shattered problem instances across distinct regimes, we prove our upper bounds are tightly saturated. Finally, we extend our topological framework to accommodate general bi-level validation-loss tuning and broader semi-algebraic applications.

</details>


### [211] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚大悉尼地区的联网车辆遥测数据（g-force阈值识别危险驾驶），在地方政府区域（LGA）层面构建时空热力图检测和预测即将发生的危险驾驶事件，并基准测试了八种预测模型。ARIMA以最低MAE（162.21）表现出色，与LSTM相当并优于所有集成方法，表明在训练数据有限时简约时间序列模型具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道路安全监测是反应式的，依赖伤亡事故发生后的碰撞记录分析。在事故发生前主动识别高风险位置和危险驾驶行为是一个关键但尚未充分探索的挑战，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 使用来自悉尼大都市地区的联网车辆遥测数据，通过g-force阈值量化危险驾驶（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g），构建时空热力图识别高风险区域。基准测试了三大类共八种预测模型：集成学习（随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）、深度学习（LSTM、N-BEATS）和经典时间序列方法（ARIMA、指数平滑、Prophet）。

Result: ARIMA取得了最低平均绝对误差（MAE: 162.21），与LSTM（MAE: 163.92）表现相当，优于所有集成方法，N-BEATS的MAE为180.75。悉尼内城和西部LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被识别为持续高风险区域。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限的情况下，简约的时间序列模型与深度学习方法的预测性能具有竞争力。基于物联网的联网车辆数据有望支持主动式道路安全干预，悉尼特定区域需要有针对性的政策行动。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [212] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种无需oracle、单次拟合即可判断混合PDE参数估计器所设定算子是否正确，并将其与不可识别性区分开来的统计工具。信息矩阵统计量在正确设定下中位数为0.19、拒绝率0.033（低于预设0.10上限），在两种错误设定下分别升至224和85且全部复现触发；在正确设定但不可识别的设计下保持沉默（n=200时0.050）。这种分离正是贡献所在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规精度检验是盲目的：错误设定估计器的域内RMSE为2.7×10^-2，低于观测噪声水平，但系数在零噪声下偏差29.7%、最大噪声下偏差31.2%，因此需要一种能够区分"算子设定错误"与"参数不可识别"的诊断工具，而不仅仅是检测存在性。

Method: 构建一个基于信息矩阵的统计量，使用plug-in尺度和每随机种子参数，对自伴抛物型反问题进行分析。通过单次拟合同时读取信息矩阵统计量与秩统计量，分别检测算子错误设定和参数不可识别性，并预先注册统计检验的拒绝率上限。

Result: 在正确设定下信息矩阵统计量中位数0.19，拒绝率0.033（低于0.10上限）；两种错误设定下统计量升至224和85且每次复现均触发。不可识别但正确设定的设计中统计量保持沉默（0.050，Clopper-Pearson区间[0.024,0.090]），而秩统计量在预设边界c*5=2.15×10^-3处归零。不同架构（单参数曲线拟合、裸参数、49和241参数的MLP）收敛到同一伪真值（闭式匹配0.07%），而物理信息网络收敛到不同解。

Conclusion: 该工具的核心贡献在于将"算子错误设定"与"参数不可识别"两种失败模式从一次拟合中分离出来，这比单纯的检测更有价值。在样本内是界限，样本外是方向。作者也报告了工具的盲区（预注册的阴性结果）、神经估计器在恢复任务上输给Tikhonov正则化反演的情形，以及其保证成立但训练网络违反该保证的假设条件。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [213] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Data-DPO，一种面向目标模型的SFT数据选择方法。通过单步探测目标模型的局部训练反馈，将样本间激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好，最终结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建训练子集。在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为静态属性，忽视了数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性。由于不同目标模型的能力分布存在差异，静态数据评分无法充分反映数据对特定模型训练效果的实际贡献，因此需要一种目标模型感知的数据选择方法。

Method: Data-DPO包含三个关键步骤：(1) 通过单步探测观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈，将激活差异转化为成对数据偏好；(2) 利用DPO训练轻量级奖励模型来学习目标模型感知的数据偏好；(3) 在最终选择阶段，结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性三种信号，构建稳定且有效的训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT两个数据集上的实验表明，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下持续优于现有数据选择基线，并稳定超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: Data-DPO通过引入目标模型感知的数据偏好，有效解决了传统数据选择方法静态评估数据价值的局限性，在多种数据预算下均取得优于基线和全量训练的性能，验证了目标模型导向的数据选择方法的有效性。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [214] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MASS方法，将数据选择建模为粗到细的层次覆盖问题：先用密集自编码器学习低维主流行坐标进行粗粒度语义分组，再用TopK稀疏自编码器在每组内进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，从而从大规模候选池中筛选高质量数据子集。在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT上实验表明，MASS在多个预算下优于强基线，且在小数据子集上可匹配或超越全量数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法直接在原始嵌入空间中度量多样性，而几何度量会纠缠主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声，导致无法有效区分数据的价值。随着监督微调数据规模扩大，如何高效筛选高价值子集以降低训练成本并提升性能成为关键问题。

Method: MASS采用两阶段的粗到细层次覆盖思路：(1) 粗粒度阶段：使用密集自编码器学习低维主流行坐标，对数据做语义分组；(2) 细粒度阶段：在每个组内使用TopK稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，确保选取的数据在细粒度特征层面充分覆盖。

Result: 在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT两个基准上，MASS在多个数据预算条件下一致优于现有强数据选择基线；在若干设置下，仅用少量数据子集即可匹配甚至超过全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: MASS通过解耦粗粒度语义分组与细粒度稀疏特征覆盖，有效规避了原始嵌入空间中几何度量的缺陷，为大规模监督微调数据选择提供了一种高效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [215] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对组织文档自动敏感性分类中的标签泄漏问题，构建了由16,000份美国外交电报组成的泄漏控制语料库Strategic 16K，并系统评估了六种模型架构。在干净基准上，BERT达到最佳性能（准确率89.14%，F1 89.33%），TF-IDF+逻辑回归在经典模型中表现最优且计算成本更低。这是首个在显式泄漏控制条件下基于WikiLeaks PlusD构建的可复现敏感性分类基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织文档敏感性自动分类是重要但缺乏充分研究的问题，误分类后果严重（从监管违规到安全漏洞）。AI方法虽有可扩展性，但其可靠性取决于训练数据的完整性。该领域存在普遍但鲜有报告的问题——标签泄漏：文档体中残留的分类标记使模型利用表面捷径而非学习真实的基于内容的敏感性信号，导致性能估计虚高且不可靠。

Method: 论文构建了Strategic 16K语料库，包含从WikiLeaks PlusD来源的16,000份外交电报，并采用扩展的泄漏移除协议，识别并消除三类嵌入文档体的残留分类标记。随后对六种模型架构进行系统基准测试，涵盖经典机器学习方法与基于Transformer的方法。

Result: 在干净基准上，BERT表现最强（准确率89.14%，F1 89.33%），ELECTRA次之（准确率88.57%，F1 88.90%）。经典模型中，TF-IDF+逻辑回归以显著更低的计算成本获得最强性能。

Conclusion: 该结果构成了首个在显式泄漏控制条件下基于WikiLeaks PlusD构建的完全可复现的敏感性分类基准，为后续该领域研究提供了可靠的评估基础和方法论参考。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [216] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Mr.Dec（多模态再入院风险预测解码器），将每次入院建模为每日多模态事件序列，利用Transformer解码器融合日常EHR更新和间歇性CXR影像发现，并结合疾病特定监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上取得最优性能，同时能识别入院期间的"关键日"以支持临床实时决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将复杂的纵向住院史压缩为固定表示，丢失了反映患者生理状态演变的日期级别粒度临床信号，而临床风险随住院期间证据累积动态演化，因此需要捕捉这种动态轨迹。

Method: 提出Mr.Dec模型：1)将每次入院表示为按时间排序的每日多模态事件序列；2)利用Transformer Decoder在同一时间对齐流中整合每日EHR更新和间歇性CXR发现，模拟真实临床工作流程；3)使用疾病特定监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中引入诊断感知结构。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上的评估表明，Mr.Dec通过保留临床序列完整性达到最先进性能，并能识别入院期间的"关键日"，提供可操作且临床可解释的结果以支持实时风险分层。

Conclusion: 通过保持临床序列的完整性并融合多模态每日数据，Mr.Dec在30天再入院预测任务上超越现有方法，且其"关键日"识别能力为临床实时风险分层提供了有价值的可解释支持。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [217] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMAN（Emergent Modular Atomic Network），一种优化驱动的多任务学习框架，它从单一路径计算出发，通过潜在相对相位揭示非对称增长方向，仅在出现持续优化证据并经过认证后才实例化第二条等容量独立路径，从而实现共享与任务特定表示容量的自适应分配。在控制秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2上的实验验证了其有效性与竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL方法（硬共享、多路径/专家、自适应共享、动态扩展）的能力变化通常受限于预定义结构或由任务边界/冲突信号触发。本文旨在回答一个根本性问题：网络能否从精确的单路径计算出发，仅在出现持续优化证据时才生长出新的独立路径，从而突破现有方法的容量扩展约束。

Method: EMAN包含两个关键机制：(1) 通过潜在相对相位（latent relative phases）揭示非对称（antisymmetric）增长方向，而无需实例化第二路径；(2) 在训练过程中监控多个决策信号，将局部优化证据转化为结构决策。只有当证据通过认证（certification）后，才实体化两条等容量的独立路径，并自适应地分配共享与任务特定的表示容量。

Result: 在控制秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2数据集上的广泛实验表明，EMAN在保持竞争优势计算成本的同时取得了改进的性能。

Conclusion: EMAN验证了优化驱动、基于证据认证的结构增长范式在MTL中的可行性，为随训练过程动态涌现网络结构提供了新思路，在性能和计算成本之间实现了良好的权衡。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [218] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了EEG基础模型部署中的对抗性迁移攻击安全问题。作者提出Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE)攻击框架，在攻击者只有公开编码器白盒访问权限和少量任务匹配标注参考集、但无法访问受害模型任何信息的情况下，通过收缩白化类原型恢复任务级决策几何，实现无需查询的迁移性对抗样本生成。在三个EEG任务、四种编码器、两种下游微调范式和两种场景下验证了攻击的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型编码器的公开发布虽然促进了下游应用开发，但也引入了新的安全风险——公开的表征可能使私有下游模型变得脆弱。以往研究忽视了EEG基础模型部署中的对抗性安全威胁，本文想探究在"公开编码器+私有下游模型"这一现实部署场景下，攻击者能否利用公开信息对不可访问的受害模型实施有效的对抗性迁移攻击。

Method: 提出SW-ProxyCE框架，核心思路是：(1)利用少量任务匹配的标注参考集，通过收缩白化（shrinkage-whitened）处理类原型来恢复任务的决策几何结构；(2)基于恢复的决策几何直接生成对抗性扰动，无需训练额外的替代分类器，也无需任何对受害模型的查询。整个攻击是查询无关（query-free）和任务感知（task-aware）的。

Result: 跨三个EEG任务、三种通用基础编码器及一种范式专用预训练编码器，在linear-probing和full-fine-tuning两种下游模型、cross-subject和within-subject两种场景下进行实验。结果表明：从公开编码器和有限标注参考集生成的对抗样本能有效迁移到不可访问的下游模型；SW-ProxyCE一致优于任务无关的表征位移攻击方法。这揭示EEG基础模型的强迁移性并不必然带来对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的公开部署存在可被利用的对抗性安全漏洞。强迁移性虽然有利于下游任务，但也使对抗样本更容易跨模型迁移。SW-ProxyCE证明仅凭公开编码器和少量参考数据即可实施有效攻击，这提示社区需要关注EEG基础模型部署中的鲁棒性与安全性权衡。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [219] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DOW-KE，一种无锚点的知识编辑方法，核心原则是"优化的必须是被部署的"，通过将最终编辑目标反向传播经过整个模型，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，消除传统多层locate-then-edit方法中锚点目标与实际部署更新之间的闭合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多层locate-then-edit知识编辑方法先优化选中层的残差流激活（锚点），再逐层实现为权重更新。但这一流程存在"闭合差距"（closure gap）：中间表示被优化了，而实际部署的多层权重更新联合效应从未被真正优化——锚点无论怎么设定或传播，每个更新都来自局部求解，传播引起的衰减和失真无法纠正。

Method: DOW-KE是锚点无关的方法，遵循单一原则：优化的内容必须与部署的内容完全一致。具体做法：(1) 将最终编辑目标通过完整模型反向传播，联合优化所有编辑层的更新，使跨层传播和耦合进入每个梯度步骤；(2) 将保护（preservation）投影嵌入更新参数化中，置于计算图内部，使每个梯度都作用于已部署的更新，避免事后约束重新引入差距。

Result: 在两个数据集和三个模型的大规模顺序编辑实验中，DOW-KE在六种模型-数据集设置中的五种取得了最高的整体Score和邻域Specificity（相对于评估的基线方法）。

Conclusion: 通过关闭优化与部署之间的差距，并将保护约束嵌入计算图内部，DOW-KE在顺序知识编辑的编辑准确性和邻域保留能力上均取得了优异表现，验证了"优化的即所部署的"这一设计原则的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [220] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了一个关键假设：学习分析中通常将智能辅导系统（ITS）日志的无监督聚类结果视为"学习者类型"并用于预测学习成效。基于EdNet-KT3数据集的5000名活跃学习者，研究发现行为聚类（8种学习策略）能预测后期参与度（坚持性η²≈0.106），却不能预测后期无辅助的正确率（padj≈0.093），且与知识追踪模型（SAKT）掌握的掌握度信号几乎无关（ARI=0.007）。结论是：行为聚类描述的是学习风格与参与度，而非知识增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析领域普遍默认将无监督聚类得到的学习者行为类型作为能预测学习效果的变量。作者希望检验这一假设是否成立——即行为聚类是否真的能预测学习结果（而非仅仅描述行为模式）。

Method: 使用EdNet-KT3数据集，选取5000名活跃学习者；对学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、问题练习）进行聚类，采用轮廓系数选择k=5的父类，然后将剩余约64.9%的近均值残差群体再次聚类得到4个精细风格，形成有自举稳定性的8种命名策略层级。时间线按答题次数分割：前半用于聚类，后半用于评估结果。使用ANOVA检验聚类对参与度和正确性的预测能力；使用SAKT知识追踪模型预测TOEIC七个章节的下一次答题正确性，并比较行为聚类与知识掌握度的关联（ARI）。

Result: （1）聚类得到k=5父类含4个对比极（阅读型、视频型、复习型、解题优先型）及约64.9%的近均值残差；残差再聚类得到4个精细风格，共8种策略。（2）早期聚类能预测后期参与度：坚持性η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021；但无法显著预测后期无辅助正确率（padj≈0.093）。（3）仅用学习量聚类与策略标签匹配度低（ARI=0.064）。（4）SAKT对下一次正确性的预测仅比仅知各板块难度基线的AUC提升+0.051（CI [+0.045,+0.058]），且掌握度信号与行为风格几乎独立（ARI=0.007）。

Conclusion: 智能辅导系统日志的行为聚类所捕捉的是学习风格和参与度模式，而非知识掌握或学习成效。将无监督聚类结果直接解读为可预测学习结果的"学习者类型"这一常见假设在本数据集上不成立。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [221] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RoBell-RVFL，一种鲁棒且轻量的质量感知广义钟形随机向量功能链接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制（少数类单位权重+多数类gbell成员函数权重）在核诱导特征空间中同时应对类别不平衡和标签噪声，在UCI和KEEL基准及40%标签噪声下超越现有RVFL变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界数据集中的多数类主导问题会使随机神经网络产生偏差决策边界并忽视关键少数类样本。现有方法（如SMOTE和类加权损失）主要关注类别比例而忽略类内分布，对标签噪声和离群点敏感，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: RoBell-RVFL采用双策略样本级加权机制：少数类样本使用单位权重严格保留其信息；多数类样本在核诱导特征空间中通过概率加权的广义钟形（gbell）成员函数自适应调节其影响。该设计有效抑制多数类中的噪声、边界和离群样本，将局部类别概率和类别分布信息显式融入学习过程，同时保持RVFL的闭式求解效率。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的广泛评估及高达40%标签噪声的鲁棒性测试表明，RoBell-RVFL持续且显著优于最新RVFL变体。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权对于鲁棒的RVFL学习至关重要，传统的全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境中效果不佳。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [222] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSigBERT，一个基于路径签名表示的多模态序列生存分析统一框架，用于肿瘤学。该方法将医疗自由文本通过BERT嵌入转换为句子向量，经PCA压缩后与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时间轨迹，再通过Signature变换编码高层时间交互，最后输入LASSO正则化Cox模型进行生存预测。在Léon Bérard中心超过2,500名患者的真实肿瘤学队列上实现了0.743的C-index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）包含叙事临床报告、数值测量和结构化变量等互补信息，但现有生存模型大多局限于单一模态，或未能充分利用患者轨迹的时间特性。这一局限性促使作者开发一个能够统一处理多模态数据并捕捉时间动态的生存预测框架。

Method: MultiSigBERT包含四个主要步骤：(1) 使用BERT对医学自由文本提取上下文词嵌入并平均得到句子嵌入；(2) 通过模态特异性PCA压缩嵌入并与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时间轨迹；(3) 利用来自粗糙路径理论的Signature变换对联合轨迹进行编码，无需监督即可高效捕捉跨模态的高阶时间交互；(4) 将Signature特征作为高维特征输入LASSO正则化的Cox比例风险模型以估计个体化风险评分。

Result: 在Léon Bérard中心的真实肿瘤学队列（超过120,000份医学报告和2,500多名患者的结构化记录）上，模型在独立测试集上实现了0.743（标准差0.029）的concordance index，证明了联合建模多模态时间动态和患者级几何结构对生存预测的益处。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，同时利用多模态时序动态与患者病理结构的几何特征能够显著提升生存预测性能，为肿瘤学领域中多源异构EHR数据的整合分析提供了一个实用且有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making. Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain complementary information -- including narrative clinical reports, numerical measurements, and structured variables -- yet most survival models remain limited to a single modality or fail to exploit the temporal nature of patient trajectories. We propose MultiSigBERT, a unified framework for multimodal sequential survival modeling in oncology based on path signature representations. Here, narrative medical reports (free-text) are converted into sentence embeddings by extracting and averaging contextual word embeddings. These representations are then compressed via modality-specific PCA and concatenated with structured covariates to form joint temporal trajectories which are then encoded using the Signature transform, a tool from Rough Paths theory that efficiently captures higher-order temporal interactions across modalities without supervision needed. The computed Signature features are finally incorporated as high dimensional features into a LASSO-regularized Cox model to estimate individualized risk scores. The performance of our novel MultiSigBERT pipeline is illustrated on the analysis of a real-world oncology cohort from the Léon Bérard Center, comprising over 120,000 medical reports and structured records from more than 2,500 patients. The model achieves a concordance index of 0.743 (sd 0.029) on an independent test set, demonstrating the benefit of jointly modeling multimodal temporal dynamics together with patient-level geometric structure for survival prediction.

</details>


### [223] [Position: Fairness Failure in Generative Models is an Evaluation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16974)
*Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi,Thibaut Issenhuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），认为生成式模型中的公平性失败本质上是一个评估问题：公平性研究结果难以跨论文比较，也难以用于部署决策。论文诊断了当前实践中的经验性和概念性失败模式，提出用标准化、面向生成式模型的"公平性卡片"（Fairness Cards）作为最小化报告工件，明确评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标、拒答处理），以提升可复现性、可比性和可问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式模型在过去十年取得突破性进展，但其公平性缺陷——强化社会不平等、伤害边缘化群体——仍未被充分关注且难以干预。作者认为公平性失败的根源在于评估问题：公平性发现很少能在论文间进行比较，或对部署决策具有可操作性。

Method: 论文采用诊断+提议的方式：首先系统梳理当前实践中反复出现的经验性和概念性失败模式；然后提出Fairness Cards作为一种最小化报告工件，要求明确记录评估的选择（包括提示族、反事实协议、度量指标和拒答处理），以支撑可复现性、可比性和问责性。

Result: 论文产出两项核心成果：(1) 对当前生成式模型公平性评估中系统性失败模式的诊断；(2) 提出Fairness Cards作为标准化报告工件，并附带其他推动评估标准范式转变的建议。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁从"零散偏见检查"转向"标准化、生成式特有的评估"，以此作为公平性研究范式转变的起点，从而真正解决生成模型公平性缺失的问题。

Abstract: Despite groundbreaking advancements in generative models during the last decade, concerns about their lack of fairness, reinforcing societal inequalities and harming marginalized groups, remain under-addressed and difficult to act upon. This position paper argues that fairness failures in generative models, albeit driven by multiple factors, are ultimately stemming from an evaluation problem: fairness findings are rarely comparable across papers or actionable for deployment decisions. This paper diagnoses recurring empirical and conceptual failure modes in current practice and motivates a shift from ad-hoc bias checks to standardized, generative-specific evaluation. We propose Fairness Cards as a minimal reporting artifact that makes evaluation choices explicit (prompt families, counterfactual protocols, metrics, and refusal handling) enabling reproducibility, comparability, and accountability. We conclude with additional recommendations towards a paradigm shift in evaluation standards. Our project page can be found at https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards .

</details>


### [224] [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034)
*Kenneth Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在智能体轨迹中途切换专门化LoRA适配器的工具，以解决后训练中灾难性遗忘与能力组合的矛盾。在组合两个合成编码任务上测试，模型不仅能自主切换适配器解决之前无法解决的问题，还发现了优于人类启发式基线的新策略，能力代价最高降低18倍，并在任务能力和token使用上均大幅优于子智能体方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练可以解锁新能力并提升特定任务性能，但往往以其他领域的灾难性遗忘为代价。在需要组合不同能力的长智能体轨迹中，这一权衡成为瓶颈，作者拒绝这种能力取舍，希望让智能体在轨迹中途能灵活切换不同专门化的LoRA适配器。

Method: 赋予智能体一个工具，允许其在轨迹中间切换不同的专门化LoRA适配器。实验设计为组合两个合成编码任务——逻辑简单但需要专门化。通过对比单一适配器智能体与子智能体方案来评估该方法的有效性。

Result: 模型能够自主切换适配器并解决之前无法解决的问题；在其中一个任务上发现了优于人类启发式基线的新策略；相比仅使用单一专门化适配器的智能体，能力代价最高降低18倍；在任务能力和token使用两方面均显著优于生成子智能体的方案。

Conclusion: 轨迹中途动态切换LoRA适配器是一种避免灾难性遗忘、实现多能力组合的有效范式，兼具性能与效率优势，优于子智能体式的模块化协作方案。

Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

</details>


### [225] [J-Miner: Recovering Executable Decision Knowledge from Language-Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17063)
*Yunfan Gao,Xinyi Huang,Tao Sheng,Haorui Song,Yun Xiong,Haofen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出J-Miner方法，从微调后的大型语言模型分类器中挖掘内部决策知识，将其编码为可检查、可验证、可复用的显式可执行规则。J-Miner通过聚合跨层和跨token位置的词汇对齐内部信号来挖掘文本级命名概念，并利用分类器自身预测学习可执行决策规则。实验表明规则能复现高达98.3%的分类器决策，且行为保真度比从输入词学习的同等紧凑规则高6.0-29.5个百分点。所得决策知识还能转移到轻量级独立学生模型（参数仅约为源分类器的1/24），保留99.8%的源分类器平均任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型微调后可以成为优秀的专用分类器，但通常只输出最终标签，其通过微调获得的决策知识隐含在模型内部，难以检查、验证和复用。作者希望从微调分类器中挖掘出内部的显式决策知识表示，使其可被检查和泛化重用。

Method: 提出J-Miner框架，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过聚合跨层、跨token位置的词汇对齐内部信号，挖掘文本级命名概念；(2) 利用分类器自身的预测结果学习基于这些概念的可执行决策规则，将局部的内部读入蒸馏为显式的分类器级知识表示。

Result: 在多个分类任务上，J-Miner规则复现了最多98.3%的源分类器决策，行为保真度比从输入词学习的同等紧凑规则高出6.0-29.5个百分点。决策知识可转移至参数约为源分类器1/24的轻量级学生模型，保留99.8%的源分类器平均任务精度。命名概念反映了与任务决策相关的内部语义证据，所学规则将分布式信号整合为可检查的决策结构。

Conclusion: 任务特定的决策知识可以被忠实地表示为显式、可执行的形式，并能在源分类器之外被复用。这证明了将内部决策知识外化为可检查表示并实现知识迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models can be fine-tuned into specialized classifiers that perform well across diverse text tasks and make complex judgments, but they typically expose only final labels, leaving the decision knowledge acquired through fine-tuning implicit within the model. We study how to mine this internal decision knowledge from a fine-tuned classifier and encode it in an executable representation that can be inspected, validated, and reused beyond the source classifier. We introduce J-Miner, which mines text-level named concepts by aggregating vocabulary-aligned internal signals across layers and token positions, and uses the classifier's own predictions to learn executable decision rules over them. This process distills local internal readouts into an explicit classifier-level knowledge representation. Across multiple classification tasks, J-Miner rules reproduce up to 98.3\% of source-classifier decisions and achieve 6.0--29.5 percentage points higher behavioral fidelity than equally compact rules learned from input words. Further analysis shows that the named concepts reflect internal semantic evidence associated with task decisions, while the learned rules consolidate these distributed signals into inspectable decision structures. The resulting decision knowledge also transfers to lightweight standalone students: using about 1/24 as many parameters as the source classifiers, they reconstruct and execute the representation from raw text while retaining 99.8\% of the source classifiers' mean task accuracy. These findings show that task-specific decision knowledge can be faithfully represented in an explicit, executable form and reused beyond the classifier in which it was learned.

</details>


### [226] [Certified but Private: Scalable Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Neural Network Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17070)
*Youwei Zhong,Ben Merbaum,Timos Antonopoulos,Ning Luo,Charalampos Papamanthou,Katerina Sotiraki,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PANDA是一个基于零知识证明（ZKP）的可扩展系统，用于在不泄露模型私有参数的前提下证明神经网络模型的鲁棒性和公平性。它基于CROWN框架，通过一种新颖的激活层线性松弛界证明算法，实现了对超过290万参数神经网络的本地鲁棒性证明（5分钟生成，10秒验证），比此前方法支持的网络规模大了4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习模型的广泛部署，模型鲁棒性和公平性的形式化保证在安全关键和合规场景中变得日益重要。然而，模型参数往往是商业机密，无法向审计方或终端用户披露。现有基于ZKP的鲁棒性证明系统依赖指数级时间算法，无法扩展到非平凡的神经网络规模，亟需可扩展的解决方案。

Method: PANDA构建在CROWN这一高效的鲁棒性认证框架之上，核心创新在于提出了一种新颖的算法，用于证明非线性激活层的线性松弛界（linear relaxation bounds），生成简单且轻量级的证明。该系统在零知识证明框架下证明模型性质，同时不泄露模型参数。

Result: PANDA能在5分钟内为超过290万参数的神经网络生成本地鲁棒性证明，并在10秒内完成验证。其复杂度随网络神经元数量多项式增长，相比此前方法，支持的网络规模大了4个数量级，且显著降低了证明者的开销。

Conclusion: PANDA展示了ZKP技术在模型认证领域的可行性和可扩展性，首次将形式化鲁棒性证明应用于大规模神经网络而不泄露模型参数，为安全关键和合规场景下的模型审计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of machine learning models, formal guarantees of the robustness and fairness of these models have become increasingly important in safety-critical and legal-compliance settings. However, model parameters are often commercial secrets that cannot be disclosed to auditors or end users. To this end, we present PANDA, a scalable system that uses zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) to prove the robustness and fairness properties of a model without revealing its private parameters. PANDA is built on top of CROWN, an efficient robustness certification framework that is used in many state-of-the-art formal verification tools for neural networks. The core contribution of PANDA is a novel algorithm for proving linear relaxation bounds for non-linear activation layers, yielding simple, lightweight proofs. Remarkably, our system can generate proofs of local robustness for neural networks with more than 2.9M parameters in 5 minutes, and can verify them in 10 seconds. Prior ZKP-based robustness system rely on exponential-time algorithms that cannot scale to nontrivial networks. In contrast, PANDA scales polynomially in the number of neurons in a network, allowing us to support neural networks 4 orders of magnitude larger than previous approaches with significantly reduced prover overhead.

</details>


### [227] [Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Calibration for Reliable Economic Forecast Intervals under Multiple Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17079)
*Bogdan Oancea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态体制感知共形预测（DRACP），在统一加权共形校准框架中结合密度比、局部核与概率体制感知加权及自调优在线显著性控制器，应对经济预测中共形预测的可交换性假设被违反的问题。理论上有三类结果（oracle权重的有限样本有效性、估计权重的覆盖缺口界、在线控制器的确定性/遗憾保证），在48个真实经济与金融序列上与6个基线对比。DRACP并非最高效方法（强自适应在线共形预测区间窄约20%），但它提供最可靠的校准，覆盖率最接近名义0.90（0.890），且在任何序列上从未低于0.80。消融实验表明在线控制器与条件尺度归一化贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测提供无分布假设的预测区间，但其依赖交换性假设在经济预测中常被违反——协变量漂移、概念漂移、局部异质性和潜在体制转换都会破坏该假设，导致预测区间覆盖失效。

Method: DRACP是一种动态体制感知的加权共形校准框架，融合三类组件：(1) 密度比加权处理协变量漂移；(2) 局部核加权处理局部异质性；(3) 概率体制感知加权处理潜在体制转换；外加一个自调优的在线显著性控制器动态调整显著性水平，三者统一在加权共形校准框架下。

Result: 在48个真实预测序列（涵盖欧元区/EU-27 HICP通胀、美国宏观经济与能源指标、日度金融序列）上与6个基线对比：DRACP覆盖率最接近名义0.90（实际0.890），任何序列覆盖率均不低于0.80，所有预测时域上覆盖率最优，且在2021-2023通胀飙升期表现最佳。但强自适应在线共形预测在区间评分上更优且区间窄约20%。DRACP在48个序列中仅10个欠覆盖，强自适应方法有20个。消融显示在线控制器和条件尺度归一化贡献最大，加权组件贡献较小。

Conclusion: DRACP在校准可靠性与区间效率之间提供了有原则的权衡，当预测区间必须满足覆盖标准时，优先保证可靠覆盖。它并非追求最窄区间的最优选择，而是在覆盖保证至关重要的场景（如通胀监控）下最可靠的校准工具。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free prediction intervals but relies on exchangeability, an assumption often violated in economic forecasting because of covariate shift, concept drift, local heterogeneity and latent regimes. We propose Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Prediction (DRACP), which combines density-ratio, localized kernel and probabilistic regime-aware weighting with a self-tuning online significance controller in a unified weighted conformal calibration framework. We distinguish three theoretical results: finite-sample validity under oracle importance weights, a coverage-gap bound for estimated weights with rates in effective sample size, and deterministic or regret guarantees for the online controller. We evaluate DRACP against six baselines on 48 real forecasting series covering euro-area and EU-27 HICP inflation, US macroeconomic and energy indicators, and daily financial series. Recent online methods (FACI, strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction and conformal PID) were verified against the authors' implementations. DRACP is not the most efficient method: strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction achieves the best interval score and intervals about 20% narrower. Instead, DRACP provides the most reliable calibration, achieving coverage closest to the nominal 0.90 (0.890), never falling below 0.80 on any series, maintaining the best coverage at all forecast horizons, and performing best during the 2021-2023 inflation surge. The strongly-adaptive method undercovers on 20 of 48 series versus 10 for DRACP. DRACP therefore offers a principled trade-off between calibration and efficiency, favoring reliable coverage when prediction intervals must satisfy coverage standards. An ablation study shows that the online controller and conditional-scale normalization provide most of the performance gain, whereas the weighting components make a smaller contribution.

</details>


### [228] [Backward through Time, Algebraically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17087)
*Konstantinos Kogkalidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种代数泛化的线性时序逻辑（LTL）可微评估引擎，以函数式编程的视角重构了软值系统（如神经策略、自适应控制器）中LTL公式（不）满足度作为训练信号的问题，并提供了可在PyTorch中使用的"telos"库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL传统上作用于布尔域，但在软值系统（神经策略、自适应控制器、序列模型等）中，离散的布尔判断无法作为有效的训练信号。虽然已有多种可微语义方案，但导航它们很棘手；且现有实现多为浅嵌入，要求使用者预先承诺单一语义代数及其（通常是隐式的）行为方式。作者以"拒绝这种承诺"的姿态出发，寻求一种代数和可微性上均通用的方案。

Method: 作者将读者设定为一位拒绝预先承诺单一语义代数的函数式程序员，由此设计了一个代数泛型（algebra-generic）且可微的评估引擎；同时给出一个可执行规范，用以刻画该引擎能接受的各类代数。文中实现并审计了多种代数在前向与反向传播中的行为表现。

Result: 多种语义代数被实现并在前向与反向行为上进行审计。关键发现是：每种代数本质上都是对"在哪个方向上令人失望（disappoint）"以及"如何失望"的一种选择。所有实现（及其他更多内容）以PyTorch库telos的形式发布，可在GitHub上获取。

Conclusion: 可微LTL语义并非一个普适解，而是一族具有不同行为偏好的代数选择；作者提供的代数泛型引擎使用户能够自由选择或自定义语义代数，而无需在早期做出不可逆的架构承诺，从而为软值系统的时序约束训练提供了更大的灵活性和可审计性。

Abstract: Linear temporal logic is a modal extension of propositional logic that allows one to state how a system should behave over time. Its canonical domain is the booleans, but discretely-valued judgements are of little use in steering softly-valued systems (neural policies, adaptive controllers, sequence models, etc). In such cases, the goal formula's (dis)satisfaction becomes a training signal, and differentiability becomes a prime concern. Candidate differentiable semantics abound, but navigating them is tricky. Implementations, where available, are shallow embeddings, demanding an upfront commitment to a single semantic algebra and its (usually implicit) conduct. The paper casts the reader as a functional programmer asked to come to terms with this predicament, and refusing. Out of that refusal comes an evaluation engine that is algebra-generic and amenable to differentiation, together with an executable specification of the algebras it can accept. Various algebras are implemented and audited for their behavior, both forward and backward. Each algebra turns out to be a choice of which direction to disappoint, and how. Everything described (and more) is part of the PyTorch library telos, to be found at https://github.com/konstantinosKokos/telos.

</details>


### [229] [Deep Learning for Cross-Border Electricity Price Forecasting: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17091)
*Hadeer Elashhab,Sai Srijan Papineni,Marvin Dorn,Veit Hagenmeyer,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的电力价格预测（EPF）基准框架，使用公开市场数据在德国-卢森堡（DE-LU）竞标区2024年数据集上，系统性地评估了六种深度学习模型（涵盖状态空间、MLP、RNN和Transformer架构），并模拟低数据目标市场场景（零样本、单样本和少样本学习）来验证模型的跨市场泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场数据虽公开可得，但该领域缺乏标准化的基准数据集和统一的评估框架，导致不同研究使用不同的数据集和指标，难以公平比较不同方法的性能与进展。

Method: 使用标准化数据集（包含日历特征、历史价格特征和市场衍生特征），在多个市场设置下对六种深度学习模型（涵盖状态空间、MLP、RNN和Transformer架构）进行统一评估；通过零样本、单样本和少样本学习模拟低数据目标市场条件；测试集聚焦于DE-LU竞标区2024年数据。

Result: N-HiTS和NBEATSx在有限数据场景下表现具有竞争力；Transformer模型虽可达到可比的准确度，但通常需要更多适配和调参；模型性能受特征选择和超参数调优影响显著，且最强模型之间的差异往往很小。

Conclusion: 建立一个可复现的EPF深度学习模型基准评估框架是可行的；不同架构在跨市场泛化中的表现差异并非巨大，N-HiTS和NBEATSx在数据稀缺场景中更具优势，特征工程和超参数调优对最终性能至关重要。

Abstract: While publicly available electricity market data presents a valuable resource for forecasting research, the field lacks established benchmark datasets for standardized comparison. As a result, many studies have relied on different datasets and metrics to evaluate methods in isolated settings, making it difficult to assess progress and compare state-of-the-art approaches consistently. In this work, we use public data to evaluate deep learning models for electricity price forecasting (EPF) across multiple market settings. Our goal is to establish a reproducible framework that enables a consistent evaluation of forecasting models. Although deep learning has been explored for day-ahead EPF, many prior studies are limited to single-market settings, narrow feature sets, or fixed training regimes. This work presents a comparative evaluation of six deep learning models--covering state-space, MLP, RNN, and Transformer-based architectures--emphasizing generalization across markets. We simulate low-data target-market conditions using zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot learning. Our test set focuses on the Germany-Luxembourg (DE-LU) bidding zone in 2024 using a standardized dataset with calendar, historical price, and market-derived features. Our findings suggest that N-HiTS and NBEATSx perform competitively in limited-data scenarios, while transformer-based models can reach comparable accuracy but tend to require more adaptation and tuning. Model performance also benefits from careful feature selection and hyperparameter tuning, and we note that the differences between the strongest models are often small.

</details>


### [230] [From Abductive Explanations to Global Logical Rules for Node Classification in SGCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17103)
*Bryan Lima Cavalcante,Thiago Alves Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逻辑的框架，用于简单图卷积（SGC）网络的节点分类任务。该框架使用最小溯因解释作为中间表示，为每个节点计算足以保持预测类别的最小节点-特征对集合，然后利用这些解释训练决策树以提取全局逻辑规则。实验表明该方法能生成紧凑的全局规则，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑方法（如LogicXGNN）从解释性子图中提取全局逻辑规则，但这类子图可能包含针对单个节点的冗余结构信息，限制了所提取规则的泛化能力。因此需要一种更精炼的中间表示来提升规则提取的质量和泛化性。

Method: 提出一个基于逻辑的框架，分为两步：(1) 为每个节点计算最小溯因解释，即最小的一组节点-特征对，足以保持SGC模型的预测类别；(2) 使用这些最小解释作为中间表示训练决策树，从决策树中提取全局逻辑规则。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架能够生成紧凑的全局规则，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。

Conclusion: 通过使用最小溯因解释替代传统解释性子图作为中间表示，可以有效减少冗余信息，从而提取出更紧凑且泛化性更好的全局逻辑规则，同时不损失对原始模型的保真度。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable performance in node classification tasks, motivating growing interest in methods capable of explaining their predictions. Recent logic-based approaches, such as LogicXGNN, derive global logical rules for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) from collections of explanatory subgraphs. While informative, these subgraphs may contain redundant structural information that is specific to individual nodes, potentially limiting the generality of the extracted rules. In this work, we propose a logic-based framework for node classification in Simple Graph Convolution (SGC) networks that uses minimal abductive explanations as an intermediate representation for rule extraction. For each node, we compute a minimal set of node-feature pairs sufficient to preserve the predicted class. These explanations are then used to train decision trees from which global logical rules are extracted. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact global rules while maintaining high fidelity to the original SGC model.

</details>


### [231] [Iterative tensor network transformations for element-wise evaluation of elementary and filtering functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17135)
*Xiao Wang,Tomohiro Hashizume,Pia Siegl,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNTs），一种在压缩域中对张量列（TT）编码数据执行逐元素非线性和滤波函数计算的一般性算法框架，避免了将数据展开为完整张量，从而能在指数级大规模数据集上高效运算。在三维反应流场上的非线性函数/滤波评估以及最高达2^70配置空间的Max-SAT优化问题中展示了其有效性，确立了张量网络方法在通用数据科学和大规模优化中的基础工具地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络是压缩大规模数据的强大格式，但其在通用数据处理中的应用受到非线性运算难以执行的限制。现有方法难以在压缩域中高效地逐元素评估非线性函数，这一问题阻碍了张量网络在数据科学和优化领域的广泛应用。

Method: 本文提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNT）算法框架，在张量列（tensor train）压缩域中直接执行逐元素的初等函数计算和非线性滤波操作。整个流程保持全程在压缩表示中进行，避免解压回完整张量，从而将计算成本控制在可控范围内。

Result: 在两个关键领域展示了ITNT的威力：(I) 在三维反应流场上高效评估高度非线性的初等函数和滤波函数，实现高保真反应速率计算和区域过滤；(II) 在复杂优化问题中寻找极值，如求解最大2^70配置空间的Max-SAT实例。

Conclusion: ITNT作为基础性工具，赋予张量网络方法支持通用数据科学和大规模优化任务的能力，填补了张量网络在非线性操作处理上的空白，有望将其应用范围从传统的数值线性代数扩展到更广泛的数据处理领域。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful formats for compressing large-scale data. However, their application to general data processing has been limited by the difficulty of performing nonlinear operations. Here, we introduce iterative tensor network transformations (ITNTs), a general algorithmic framework for the element-wise evaluation of elementary and nonlinear filtering functions on data encoded as tensor trains (TTs), a class of tensor networks. Our approach operates entirely in the compressed domain, enabling efficient computation on exponentially large datasets while maintaining a controlled computational cost. We demonstrate its power in two key areas: (I) evaluating highly nonlinear elementary and filtering functions on a 3D reactive flow field, enabling high-fidelity reaction rate computation and region filtering, and (II) finding extrema in complex optimization problems, such as solving Max-SAT instances on spaces up to $2^{70}$ configurations. These results establish ITNT as a foundational tool that provides tensor network methods with the capability for general-purpose data science and large-scale optimization.

</details>


### [232] [OraclePhys: A Systematic Framework for LLM Fine-Tuning on Structural Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17162)
*Mingyu Li,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OraclePhys框架，包含精确评分的结构力学基准（OraclePhys-Bench）、七种答案形式的监督数据集（OraclePhys-30K）以及三角色验证器的对照训练研究。核心发现有二：其一，标签的答案形式（非比特数）因果决定了微调所学内容——排序目标安装分布外前向模型，标量目标仅部分安装，布尔目标无可检测效果；其二，书面或分数筛选的答案能实现该能力，而优势加权分数（GRPO）虽提升奖励却使模型在留出物理数据上统计等价于初始状态。训练后的8B模型成为首个空间结构响应LLM，达到数据精度前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型从微调中内化的内容通常只能在事后诊断，缺乏将微调过程本身作为实验变量的系统性框架。作者旨在通过受控微调研究，因果地回答"标签的何种属性决定了模型学到的能力"这一根本问题。

Method: 构建OraclePhys三组件框架：(1) OraclePhys-Bench，基于有限元oracle的精确评分结构力学基准，无需人工标注或LLM评判，可对每个答案和反事实编辑评分；(2) OraclePhys-30K，包含七种答案形式（排序、标量、布尔等）的监督数据集，结构描述逐字节一致；(3) 跨七种形式与三种验证器角色的受控训练研究。对照实验跨越第二物理域、第二模型家族、改写的评估表面验证结论。

Result: 标签的答案形式因果决定微调结果：排序目标安装分布外前向模型（未训练的基座处于猜测先验），标量目标至多部分安装，布尔目标无可检测效果；向量-标量鸿沟在第二物理域、第二模型家族和改写评估表面均成立。书面或分数筛选答案可安装能力，而GRPO提升奖励但留出集统计等价于初始状态，仅适用于路由。训练后的8B模型在零样本和32样本下超越前沿LLM，达到专家水平，处于任务的数据精度前沿。

Conclusion: 结论概括为"标签对目标计算的展开程度决定了微调教给模型的内容；训练数据决定路由能力"。即答案的语义形式（而非数据体量）是能力内化的因果决定性因素，GRPO等强化方法在预算内只能改变奖励表面而不能实质性地安装物理能力。

Abstract: What a language model internalizes from fine-tuning is usually diagnosed after the fact. We make it an experimental variable. OraclePhys is a systematic fine-tuning framework with three components: OraclePhys-Bench, an exactly-graded structural-mechanics benchmark whose finite-element oracle scores every answer and counterfactual edit -- no human labels, no LLM judging; OraclePhys-30K, a supervision dataset of seven answer forms over byte-identical structure descriptions; and a controlled training study across the seven forms and three verifier roles. The study yields two findings. First, the label's answer form -- not its bit count -- causally determines what fine-tuning teaches: a ranking objective installs an out-of-distribution forward model where the untrained base sits at the guessing prior, a scalar objective at best a partial one, a boolean nothing detectable; the vector-scalar gulf survives a second physics domain, a second model family, and a paraphrased evaluation surface. Second, written or score-filtered answers install this capability, while advantage-weighted scores (GRPO) raise reward yet leave the model statistically equivalent to its start on held-out physics -- within the recipes and budgets tested -- sufficing only for routing. The trained 8B -- the first LLM on spatial structural response -- reaches the task's data-precision frontier: above a frontier LLM at zero- and 32-shot, at a specialist's level. What the label spells out about the target computation is what fine-tuning teaches; what you train on is what you route.

</details>


### [233] [The concentration game: Bayesian updating, regret, and information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18061)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个两玩家零和重复博弈统一刻画了贝叶斯更新、指数权重遗憾和浓度现象，证明了Gibbs/贝叶斯权重是该博弈的Bellman均衡策略，并给出了遗憾的精确三部分分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾界依赖于方差和有界范围等松弛代理来驱动标准界，缺乏统一的精确分解；作者希望建立一个同时涵盖贝叶斯更新、指数权重遗憾和浓度现象的通用框架。

Method: 构造学习者与自然之间的两玩家零和重复博弈：终端收益为固定相对熵下比较器能获得的最大收益，单步约束为自然在学习者混合行动下的信息预算。通过Bellman方程求解，Gibbs/贝叶斯权重作为唯一均衡出现，log-配分函数作为值函数。遗憾被精确分解为三部分：单步信息损失、重标度漂移项、比较器相对于先验的信息量。

Result: 遗憾分解是精确且通用的，方差和有界范围代理是其松弛形式。双方策略可从分解逐项读出，重复博弈可得到信息论账本。相同比较器类几何可解释经典大偏差界，多臂老虎机、后验采样、聚合和提升方法都是该遗憾分解的特例。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个信息论层面的统一遗憾分解框架，替代了传统的二次变分代理，将多个领域的现有方法统一在一个理论之下。

Abstract: We give a two-player zero-sum repeated game between a learner and nature whose value identity generates Bayesian updating and an exact accounting of exponential-weights regret at once, and supplies the comparator-class variational form that a wide class of concentration phenomena share. The terminal payoff is the most a comparator can gain at fixed relative entropy from the prior, and the one-step constraint is an information budget on nature's move under the learner's mixed action. With the learner's move otherwise unrestricted, Gibbs/Bayes weights emerge as its unique Bellman equalizer -- the mixed action that makes the per-round loss independent of which direction nature moves -- with log-partition functions playing the role of value functions. The regret decomposes exactly into three parts: a per-round information loss reflecting the variation in observed outcomes, an additive retempering drift that accounts exactly for any change of measurement scale between rounds, and the information the comparator carries relative to the prior. The variance and bounded-range proxies that drive standard regret bounds are looser relaxations of this decomposition, which holds generally and governs them all. Both players' strategies are read off from the decomposition term by term, and repeated play yields an information-theoretic ledger of self-play in place of the usual quadratic-variation surrogate. The same comparator-class geometry accounts for the classical large-deviation bounds, and methods across bandits, posterior sampling, aggregation, and boosting are specializations of the one regret decomposition.

</details>


### [234] [Q-Learning With World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17163)
*Perry Dong,Yueru Jia,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QWM框架，将世界模型与标准Q-learning结合，通过测试时搜索（test-time search）在想象轨迹上选择高价值动作，同时仅用真实数据训练策略和值函数，避免了复合偏差，在Robomimic和LIBERO基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线策略强化学习在样本效率上有所提升，但基于世界模型的RL方法通常直接在想象rollout上优化策略或值函数，存在复合偏差（compounding bias）问题，难以扩展到大规模高维问题如真实机器人操作，且随任务horizon和视觉复杂度增加而恶化。作者希望探索能否在世界模型之上直接利用标准Q-learning，在保持真实在线训练的同时提升性能。

Method: 提出QWM框架：在世界模型（world model）上对想象轨迹进行测试时搜索（test-time search），结合Q-learning选择高价值动作，用于在线rollout和评估阶段。关键设计是策略和值函数仅基于真实transition训练，从而避免复合模型偏差，同时获得预测搜索带来的样本效率优势。

Result: 在Robomimic和LIBERO两个具有挑战性的操作基准上，QWM在样本效率和最终性能两方面均显著优于强基线方法和现有state-of-the-art方法。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型与Q-learning以测试时搜索的方式结合，可以在不牺牲真实数据训练可靠性的前提下，获得基于预测搜索的样本效率收益，为大规模高维机器人操作场景提供了有效且可扩展的RL方案。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) has become increasingly sample-efficient, enabling applications such as RL fine-tuning of Vision-Language-Action models into reliable, high-performing policies. World models offer a further lever for sample efficiency, as they predict state changes rather than actions alone, but their success has largely been confined to supervised policy learning. Prior model-based RL methods often optimize the policy or value function directly on imagined rollouts, which is prone to compounding bias and struggles to scale to large, high-dimensional problems such as real-world robotics, a problem that worsens with task horizon and visual complexity. In this work, we instead ask whether we can leverage world models directly on top of standard Q-learning to improve performance, while remaining trained and grounded in the real, online setting. We propose QWM, a framework that leverages world models to perform test-time search over imagined trajectories on top of Q-learning to select high-value actions during both online rollouts and evaluation. Since the policy and value function are trained only on real transitions, QWM avoids compounding model bias while still gaining the sample-efficiency benefits of predictive search. On challenging manipulation benchmarks Robomimic and LIBERO, QWM significantly outperforms strong prior state-of-the-art methods on both sample efficiency and performance.

</details>


### [235] [SCENARIODIFF: A Scenario-level Guidance Framework for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting--Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17164)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Dat Nguyen Cong,Duc-Trong Le,Thanh Trung Huynh,Tung Kieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCENARIODIFF 是一个用于多模态时间序列预测的分层上下文推理框架，通过三层次结构化信号（历史证据、场景描述、锚点）显式引导扩散模型生成预测，在事件驱动领域表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本时间序列联合预测方法存在两大问题：一是让LLM直接预测数值，缺乏对时序建模的专业性；二是将文本与时间序列隐式融合，导致上下文影响难以解释和控制，尤其在存在噪声和弱对齐文档的场景下。

Method: SCENARIODIFF构建三层上下文结构：1) Historical Context Agent从原始文档抽取逐步证据；2) Scenario Agent为预测区间生成定性场景描述；3) Anchor Guidance Agent为事件相关未来区域生成稀疏锚点。这些信号条件化一个多模态扩散Transformer，并通过Anchor Blended Sampling在无需重训练的情况下局部优化生成轨迹。

Result: 在Time-MMD基准上验证，SCENARIODIFF在事件驱动领域尤为有效，证明显式分层场景引导对多模态时间序列预测的价值。

Conclusion: 显式、可解释的分层上下文引导比隐式文本-时序融合更有效，能够提升事件驱动场景下的预测质量，且锚点采样提供无需重训练的推理时优化能力。

Abstract: Textual context such as news, reports, and logs can provide valuable signals for time series forecasting, especially when future dynamics are driven by external events that are not yet visible in historical values. Existing multimodal forecasting methods often either ask large language models (LLMs) to predict numerical values directly or fuse text and time series implicitly, making contextual influence difficult to interpret and control. We propose SCENARIODIFF, a hierarchical contextual reasoning framework for multimodal time series forecasting under noisy and weakly aligned documents. SCENARIODIFF organizes contextual information into three levels: a Historical Context Agent extracts stepwise evidence from raw documents, a Scenario Agent produces a qualitative scenario description for the forecast horizon, and an Anchor Guidance Agent generates sparse anchor points for event-relevant future regions. These structured signals condition a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer, while Anchor Blended Sampling locally refines generated trajectories without retraining. Experiments on the Time-MMD benchmark show that SCENARIODIFF is especially effective in event-driven domains, demonstrating the value of explicit hierarchical scenario guidance for multimodal time series forecasting. Our full implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ScenarioDiff_ICDM-2C4C

</details>


### [236] [Population Health-Based Machine Learning Reveals Associations Between Psychosocial Factors and Chronic Kidney Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17174)
*Md. Atik Shams,David Eisenberg,Sumaiya Fatema,Asma Sultana,D. M Hasibul Islam,Junnatul Mawa,Anindita Datta,Nafiya Ahmed,Danastan Tasaouf Mridula,SK. Sazid Mahmud,Simon Bin Akter,Tanjila Helaly,Jorge Fresneda Fernandez,Humayera Islam,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用大规模远程医疗数据与机器学习方法（堆叠集成模型）对慢性肾病（CKD）进行自报分类并识别关键驱动因素。研究涵盖BRFSS 2021/2019与NHIS 2021/2020共四个数据集（合计约91.8万样本），采用9种插补方法处理缺失数据、采样策略缓解类别不平衡，堆叠集成模型在平衡准确率达72.56-76.12%，AUROC为79.59-82.29%。SHAP分析结合临床审查识别出定期体检、年龄、血压与心理压力等关键预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病（CKD）起病隐匿且严重影响生活质量，早期检测对于改善患者预后至关重要。然而目前缺乏一个结合大规模人群数据与可解释机器学习方法用于CKD风险分层及关键因素识别的系统性框架。

Method: 两阶段研究设计：（1）整合BRFSS 2021/2019（438,693与418,268样本）和NHIS 2021/2020（29,482与31,568样本）大规模远程健康调查数据；（2）采用9种先进的缺失数据插补方法处理缺失值，并通过采样策略缓解类别不平衡；（3）构建定制的堆叠集成（stacked ensemble）模型进行CKD自报状态分类；（4）运用SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）分析进行模型可解释性分析，并辅以临床审查验证。

Result: 堆叠集成模型在四个数据集上实现平衡准确率72.56%-76.12%及AUROC分数79.59%-82.29%。SHAP分析经临床审查后确认关键预测因子包括：定期医疗检查、年龄、血压以及心理健康压力指标。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个稳健且可解释的CKD风险分层框架，并为CKD相关因素提供了可操作的临床见解，对早期检测与干预具有重要价值。

Abstract: Chronic kidney disease (CKD) progresses silently and severely undermines quality of life, making early detection critical for improving patient outcomes. We present a two-part study that combines large-scale telehealth data with advanced machine learning to both classify self-reported CKD status and identify key drivers of disease. Using selected features from the Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System (BRFSS 2021: 438,693 samples; BRFSS 2019: 418,268 samples) and the National Health Interview Survey (NHIS 2021: 29,482 samples; NHIS 2020: 31,568 samples), we addressed missing data with nine state-of-the-art imputation methods and mitigated class imbalance via sampling strategies. Our customized stacked ensemble model achieved balanced accuracy of 72.56-76.12%, with corresponding AUROC scores of 79.59-82.29%. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis, followed by clinical review, highlighted critical predictors, including regular medical check-ups, age, blood pressure, and indicators of mental health stress. These findings deliver a robust and interpretable framework for CKD risk stratification and provide actionable insights into its associated factors.

</details>


### [237] [Task Specialization Fine-Tuning for Contextual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17180)
*Jianan Zhou,Jung-Hoon Cho,Tianyue Zhou,Han Zheng,Jie Zhang,Roy Dong,Yining Ma,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出任务特化微调（TSFT）框架，解决上下文强化学习（CRL）中"预训练-微调"范式下的预算分配问题。该方法利用参数模型预测微调性能，并通过整数线性规划精确求解离散预算分配，在组合优化、连续控制和LLM微调等多个领域显著超越基线，接近Oracle性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL方法通常从零开始训练，要么依赖单策略多任务学习，要么策略性地训练多个策略。作者倡导更统一的新范式——先用单个策略预训练获得良好初始性能，再微调多个策略实现任务特化，但这一范式面临异质边际收益和样本效率低下等独特挑战。因此核心研究问题是：在给定预训练策略和有限预算下，每个任务区域应分配多少微调才能实现样本高效的CRL。

Method: 提出TSFT（Task Specialization Fine-Tuning，任务特化微调）在线框架：使用简单参数化模型预测微调性能，将预算分配问题形式化为离散优化问题，并通过整数线性规划（ILP）精确求解最优分配方案。

Result: 在多个决策领域（组合优化、连续控制、LLM微调）的广泛实验中，TSFT在任务覆盖率上显著优于基线方法，并接近Oracle性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于模型的CRL开辟了新方向，与现代"预训练-微调"时代相契合，证明了预训练后策略特化微调是CRL的高效统一范式。

Abstract: Contextual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) seeks to generalize classical RL by maximizing task coverage across a context space of related tasks. While prior works often train from scratch and rely on either multi-task learning for a single policy or strategically training multiple policies, we advocate for a unified alternative: pretraining a single policy with good initial performance, followed by fine-tuning multiple policies for task specialization. This new paradigm, however, introduces unique challenges, such as heterogeneous marginal returns and sample inefficiency.
